    static bool DungeonTrial => Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DUNGEON_TRIAL") == "1";
    static string QueenResumeRoot => Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_QUEEN_RESUME");
    sealed class AcceptanceClock
    {
        readonly object gate = new();
        readonly Stopwatch clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        readonly string directory;
        readonly Vector2 entrance;
        readonly Vector2[] descent;
        readonly System.Threading.Timer timer;
        readonly JsonArray stops = new();
        readonly JsonArray stationaryWarnings = new();
        bool stationaryWarned;
        bool wasDead, entered, complete, closed, stationaryReported;
        int deaths;
        long? descentMs, healthMs, chakramMs, firstActionMs, firstMovementMs;
        long stationarySince, longestStopMs, lastReportMs;
        Vector2 anchor;
        JsonObject latest = new();
        int previousHealth, previousCrystals;
        public bool HealthGoalVerified { get { lock (gate) return latest["underground_jungle"]?.GetValue<bool>() == true
            && latest["permanent_health"]?.GetValue<int>() >= 300 && latest["dead"]?.GetValue<bool>() == false; } }

        public AcceptanceClock(string directory, JsonObject route)
        {
            this.directory = directory;
            anchor = Main.LocalPlayer.position;
            previousHealth = Main.LocalPlayer.statLifeMax;
            previousCrystals = Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Where(i => i.type == ItemID.LifeCrystal).Sum(i => i.stack);
            entrance = new Vector2(route["entrance"]["x"].GetValue<int>(), route["entrance"]["y"].GetValue<int>());
            descent = route["line"].AsArray().Select(p => new Vector2(p[0].GetValue<int>(), p[1].GetValue<int>()))
                .Where(p => p.Y >= entrance.Y + 12).ToArray();
            timer = new System.Threading.Timer(_ => Deadline(), null, 100, 100);
        }

        public void Observe(Player p)
        {
            lock (gate)
            {
                if (closed) return;
                long now = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
                var tile = p.Center / 16;
                bool undergroundJungle = p.ZoneJungle && (p.ZoneDirtLayerHeight || p.ZoneRockLayerHeight);
                int crystals = p.inventory.Where(i => i.type == ItemID.LifeCrystal).Sum(i => i.stack);
                if (JungleHealthTrial && !undergroundJungle && (p.statLifeMax > previousHealth || crystals > previousCrystals))
                    Fail("crystal_collected_or_used_outside_underground_jungle");
                previousHealth = p.statLifeMax; previousCrystals = crystals;
                if (p.dead && !wasDead) deaths++;
                wasDead = p.dead;
                if (!p.dead)
                {
                    entered |= Vector2.Distance(tile, entrance) <= 10;
                    if (descentMs == null && entered && tile.Y >= entrance.Y + 12
                        && descent.Any(point => Vector2.Distance(tile, point) <= 6)) descentMs = now;
                    if (healthMs == null && p.statLifeMax >= (JungleHealthTrial ? 300 : 200)) healthMs = now;
                    if (chakramMs == null && p.inventory.Any(i => i.type == ItemID.ThornChakram && i.stack > 0)) chakramMs = now;
                }
                if (firstActionMs == null && (RecedingNav.Active || p.controlUseItem || p.controlLeft || p.controlRight || p.controlJump))
                    firstActionMs = now;
                long duration = firstMovementMs == null ? 0 : now - stationarySince;
                longestStopMs = Math.Max(longestStopMs, duration);
                if (Vector2.DistanceSquared(p.position, anchor) > 16)
                {
                    if (firstMovementMs == null && (RecedingNav.Active || p.controlLeft || p.controlRight || p.controlJump))
                        firstMovementMs = now;
                    if (stationaryReported) stops.Add(new JsonObject { ["start_ms"] = stationarySince,
                        ["duration_ms"] = duration, ["x"] = anchor.X / 16, ["y"] = anchor.Y / 16 });
                    stationarySince = now; anchor = p.position; stationaryReported = stationaryWarned = false;
                }
                else if (duration >= 5000 && !stationaryReported)
                {
                    stationaryReported = true;
                }
                latest = new JsonObject { ["tick"] = (long)Main.GameUpdateCount, ["elapsed_ms"] = now,
                    ["x"] = tile.X, ["y"] = tile.Y, ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["permanent_health"] = p.statLifeMax,
                    ["dead"] = p.dead, ["underground_jungle"] = undergroundJungle,
                    ["navigation_active"] = RecedingNav.Active, ["item_use_active"] = ItemUseCoordinator.IsActive,
                    ["status"] = NativeAgent.Status };
                if (!JungleHealthTrial && deaths >= 2) Fail("second_death");
            }
        }

        string Failure() => !JungleHealthTrial && deaths >= 2 ? "second_death"
            : !JungleHealthTrial && clock.ElapsedMilliseconds >= 360000 && (descentMs == null || descentMs > 360000) ? "main_descent_deadline"
            : !JungleHealthTrial && clock.ElapsedMilliseconds >= 1080000 && (healthMs == null || healthMs > 1080000) ? "health_200_deadline"
            : !JungleHealthTrial && clock.ElapsedMilliseconds >= 1440000 && (chakramMs == null || chakramMs > 1440000) ? "chakram_deadline"
            : !JungleHealthTrial && clock.ElapsedMilliseconds >= 1800000 && !complete ? "task_deadline" : null;

        JsonObject Report(string reason) => new()
        {
            ["ok"] = complete, ["reason"] = reason, ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds,
            ["deaths"] = deaths, ["death_limit"] = JungleHealthTrial ? null : JsonValue.Create(2), ["descent_ms"] = descentMs,
            [JungleHealthTrial ? "health_300_ms" : "health_200_ms"] = healthMs, ["chakram_ms"] = chakramMs, ["first_action_ms"] = firstActionMs,
            ["first_movement_ms"] = firstMovementMs,
            ["startup_wait_ms"] = firstMovementMs ?? clock.ElapsedMilliseconds,
            ["longest_stationary_ms"] = Math.Max(longestStopMs, firstMovementMs == null ? 0 : clock.ElapsedMilliseconds - stationarySince), ["stationary_over_5s"] = stops.DeepClone(),
            ["stationary_warning_ms"] = 10000, ["stationary_warning_armed"] = firstMovementMs != null,
            ["stationary_policy"] = "warn_only", ["stationary_warnings"] = stationaryWarnings.DeepClone(),
            ["current_stationary_ms"] = firstMovementMs == null ? 0 : clock.ElapsedMilliseconds - stationarySince,
            ["last_observation"] = latest.DeepClone(),
            ["main_route_rule"] = "within 10 tiles of measured entrance, then at least 12 tiles below it and within 6 tiles of measured route"
        };

        void Deadline()
        {
            lock (gate)
            {
                if (closed) return;
                if (firstMovementMs != null && !stationaryWarned && clock.ElapsedMilliseconds - stationarySince >= 10000)
                {
                    stationaryWarned = true;
                    var warning = new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds,
                        ["stationary_since_ms"] = stationarySince,
                        ["duration_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds - stationarySince, ["observation"] = latest.DeepClone() };
                    stationaryWarnings.Add(warning);
                    File.AppendAllText(Path.Combine(directory, "stationary-warnings.jsonl"), warning.ToJsonString() + Environment.NewLine);
                    Console.WriteLine("ACCEPTANCE_STATIONARY_WARNING " + warning.ToJsonString());
                }
                string failure = Failure();
                if (failure != null) Fail(failure);
                if (clock.ElapsedMilliseconds - lastReportMs >= 1000)
                {
                    lastReportMs = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(directory, "acceptance-progress.json"), Report("running").ToJsonString());
                }
            }
        }

        void Fail(string reason)
        {
            closed = true;
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(directory, "acceptance.json"), Report(reason).ToJsonString());
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(directory, "agent-engine-result.json"), new JsonObject
                { ["ok"] = false, ["detail"] = "ACCEPTANCE_FAIL " + reason }.ToJsonString());
            Console.WriteLine("ACCEPTANCE_FAIL " + reason);
            Console.Out.Flush();
            Environment.Exit(1);
        }

        public void Finish(bool verified)
        {
            lock (gate)
            {
                string failure = Failure();
                if (failure != null) Fail(failure);
                complete = verified && (JungleHealthTrial ? HealthGoalVerified && healthMs != null
                    : descentMs != null && healthMs != null && chakramMs != null && deaths < 2);
                if (!complete) Fail("task_ended_unverified");
                closed = true;
                timer.Dispose();
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(directory, "acceptance.json"), Report("verified_results").ToJsonString());
            }
        }
    }
    bool Natural => Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_BEHAVIOR_NATURAL") == "1";
    bool NaturalEncounters => Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_NATIVE_ENCOUNTERS") == "1"
        || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SKILL_LEARNING") == "1";
    bool FullCraftTrial => Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_FULL_CRAFT_TRIAL") == "1";
    int FrameLimit => Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SKILL_LEARNING") == "1" ? 36000
        : DungeonTrial || FullCraftTrial || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_HIVE_BUILD") == "1" ? 111600 : Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MATERIAL_MODEL_REVIEW") == "1"
        || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MATERIAL_AGENT_LIVE") == "1" ? 14400
        : Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_OPPORTUNITY_AGENT_LIVE") == "1"
            || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_KNOWLEDGE_AGENT") == "1" ? 9600 : 7200;
    const int ReviewExecutionFrames = (45 + 20 + 20 + 5) * 60;
    async Task<JsonObject> Review(JsonObject wake, bool materials = false, CodexAgent session = null, string reportName = "behavior-review")
    {
        if (!ModLoader.TryGetMod("TerraBlind", out var mod)) throw new Exception("production mod missing");
        var schemas = JsonNode.Parse(Encoding.UTF8.GetString(mod.GetFileBytes("Agent/Tools.json"))).AsArray();
        AgentControlSession.DescribeClaims(schemas);
        AgentScript.DescribePrograms(schemas);
        var allowed = materials
            ? new[] { "game_api", "get_state", "npc_find", "inspect_terrain", "script_program", "script_progress", "inventory_review", "action_query", "gather_observe", "opportunity_assess", "npc_combat_observe", "item_info", "recipe" }
            : new[] { "game_api", "get_state", "npc_find", "inspect_terrain", "script_program" };
        var definitions = new JsonArray(schemas.Where(t => t["function"]["name"].ToString() is not ("run_script" or "ask"))
            .Select(t => (JsonNode)new JsonObject { ["name"] = t["function"]["name"].DeepClone(),
                ["summary"] = t["function"]["description"]?.ToString().Split('。')[0] }).ToArray());
        var entries = new JsonArray(schemas.Where(t => allowed.Contains(t["function"]["name"].ToString())).Select(t => t.DeepClone()).ToArray());
        int calls = 0;
        var replies = new JsonArray();
        var reviewCalls = new JsonArray();
        using var ownedAgent = session == null ? new CodexAgent() : null;
        var agent = session ?? ownedAgent;
        using var timeout = new CancellationTokenSource(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(materials ? 45 : 90));
        string request = materials
            ? "Inspect the failed material collection and save a JavaScript continuation named material_recovery. Do not execute it during this review. "
                + "Goal: preserve completed opportunistic chest/crystal collection and continue obtaining the recipe materials for Thorn Chakram. "
                + "Use current evidence to choose the next concrete recovery action; retain existing gather/navigation handles and progress. "
                + "Do not invent sources, equipment, healing or a safe route. Do not hardcode coordinates absent from observations. "
                + "The saved code must reobserve at execution, handle interruption, verify actual gains, and resume the same original gather when appropriate. "
                + "You may instead record a concrete unmet prerequisite if no justified action exists, but do not claim task success. "
                + "At most 8 tool calls; save source before your final reply.\nReview request:\n"
            : "Review and save a corrected revision of combat_loot_return. Preserve workflow behavior-trial and all completed step identifiers/handles. "
            + "Goal: move while attacking the nearby hostile, judge actual drops with Jev, collect a useful drop, then resume the original navigation handle. "
            + "Do not execute game actions now. Read the saved program and current terrain/state, then use script_program save with the current expected_revision. "
            + "The saved revision contains the full continuation but pauses at a pre-combat review gate. Use script_program patch to change only that gate and any concrete issue supported by observations. "
            + "Do not delete the continuation or save another immediate decision_point. Preserve the return contract: item_type,gained,original_handle,judgment,returned. "
            + "Handle interrupted actions and danger explicitly. Do not claim a kill or collection without evidence. Avoid walking off the current ledge. "
            + "At most 8 tool calls. Read current source via script_program get.\nReview request:\n";
        try
        {
        await agent.RunAsync(request + wake.ToJsonString(),
            "", Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "TerraBlindAgent"), "gpt-6-luna",
            "You review and edit saved Terraria JS behavior programs. tool(name,args) inside source uses the game APIs below. "
            + "You may only query or save source during this review. No shell/files/network. Existing workflow step results are reused on resume.\n"
            + "During review get_state is a compact current-state view, not the full runtime snapshot.\n"
            + "The saved program will execute under this production runtime contract (execution is not authorized during this review):\n"
            + AgentScript.RuntimeInstructions + "\nThe following is an API purpose index, not parameter schemas. Before writing calls in saved source, use game_api with names to obtain exact schemas; reuse already obtained definitions.\n"
            + definitions.ToJsonString(new System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions(System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions.Default)
                { Encoder = System.Text.Encodings.Web.JavaScriptEncoder.UnsafeRelaxedJsonEscaping }), entries,
            async (name, args, token) =>
            {
                if (!allowed.Contains(name) || ++calls > 8) throw new InvalidOperationException("review tool budget or scope exceeded");
                if (materials && name == "script_program" && args["operation"]?.ToString() is not ("get" or "list")
                    && (args["name"]?.ToString() != "material_recovery" || args["operation"]?.ToString() is not ("save" or "patch")))
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("review may read programs but only edit material_recovery");
                if (name == "script_progress" && args["action"]?.ToString() is not (null or "" or "get"))
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("review may only read workflow progress");
                string Record(string value)
                {
                    reviewCalls.Add(new JsonObject { ["tool"] = name, ["args"] = args.DeepClone(),
                        ["result"] = value[..Math.Min(4000, value.Length)], ["truncated"] = value.Length > 4000,
                        ["result_chars"] = value.Length });
                    return value;
                }
                string output = await AgentTools.Execute(name, args, token);
                if (name != "get_state") return Record(output);
                var state = JsonNode.Parse(output);
                var compact = new JsonObject();
                foreach (string field in new[] { "player", "buffs", "enemies", "terrain", "healing_items", "health_dynamics", "task_plan", "permanent_max_health", "last_damage", "last_death", "control_overrides" })
                    if (state[field] != null) compact[field] = state[field].DeepClone();
                var watch = new JsonObject();
                foreach (string field in new[] { "events", "passive_events", "tile_types", "radius", "health_below", "pending" })
                    watch[field] = state["watch"]?[field]?.DeepClone();
                compact["watch"] = watch;
                compact["inventory_slots"] = new JsonArray(state["inventory_slots"].AsArray().Where(i => i["type"]?.GetValue<int>() > 0).Select(i => i.DeepClone()).ToArray());
                compact["action_tasks"] = new JsonArray(state["action_tasks"].AsArray().Select(a => (JsonNode)new JsonObject
                { ["handle"] = a["handle"]?.DeepClone(), ["tool"] = a["tool"]?.DeepClone(), ["args"] = a["args"]?.DeepClone(), ["status"] = a["status"]?.DeepClone() }).ToArray());
                var workflows = new JsonObject();
                foreach (var workflow in state["script_workflows"].AsObject())
                    workflows[workflow.Key] = new JsonObject { ["segment"] = workflow.Value["segment"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["last_execution"] = workflow.Value["last_execution"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["steps"] = new JsonArray(workflow.Value["steps"].AsArray().Select(step => (JsonNode)new JsonObject
                        { ["id"] = step["id"]?.DeepClone(), ["status"] = step["status"]?.DeepClone() }).ToArray()) };
                compact["script_workflows"] = workflows;
                Console.WriteLine($"MODEL_REVIEW_STATE raw_chars={output.Length} compact_chars={compact.ToJsonString().Length}");
                return Record(compact.ToJsonString());
            }, text => replies.Add(text), timeout.Token);
        }
        finally
        {
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, reportName + ".json"), new JsonObject
        { ["model"] = "gpt-6-luna", ["calls"] = calls, ["tool_calls"] = reviewCalls,
            ["replies"] = replies, ["usage"] = agent.TokenUsage }.ToJsonString());
        }
        return await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "get", ["name"] = materials ? "material_recovery" : "combat_loot_return" });
    }
    public override void OnWorldLoad()
    {
        if (!enabled || !ShortJungleTrial && !ProgressionTrial && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_QUEEN_GOAL") != "1") return;
        if (FullQueenRun && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(QueenResumeRoot) && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_COMPARE") == null)
        {
            VanillaNavBridge.Enabled = false;
            jungleSpawn = new JsonObject { ["x"] = Main.spawnTileX, ["y"] = Main.spawnTileY,
                ["world_default"] = true, ["terrain_edits"] = 0, ["navigation_backend"] = "receding_native_lookahead" };
            Console.WriteLine("QUEEN_DEFAULT_SPAWN " + jungleSpawn.ToJsonString());
            return;
        }
        if (ProgressionTrial && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(QueenResumeRoot))
        {
            jungleSpawn = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(QueenResumeRoot, "progression-cobalt-start.json")))["spawn"].DeepClone().AsObject();
            return;
        }
        if (FullQueenRun) VanillaNavBridge.Enabled = false;
        var spawn = BiomeSearch.Jungle(Main.spawnTileX);
        if (spawn["found"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("no pure natural jungle surface spawn found");
        Main.spawnTileX = spawn["x"].GetValue<int>(); Main.spawnTileY = spawn["y"].GetValue<int>() + 1;
        jungleSpawn = new JsonObject { ["x"] = Main.spawnTileX, ["y"] = Main.spawnTileY,
            ["local_jungle_tiles"] = spawn["local_biome_tiles"].DeepClone(), ["threshold"] = SceneMetrics.JungleTileThreshold,
            ["terrain_edits"] = 0 };
        Console.WriteLine("JUNGLE_COLLECTION_SPAWN " + jungleSpawn.ToJsonString());
    }

    public override void Load()
    {
        enabled = !Main.dedServ && Terraria.Program.LaunchParameters.ContainsKey("-agentbehaviorcheck");
        if (enabled)
        {
            On_Main.Update += Open; IL_Main.DoUpdate += Focus;
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MINING_SPEED_TRIAL") == "1" || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_QUEEN_MINING_REPLAY") == "1") On_Player.PickTile += MeasureMining;
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_QUEEN_GOAL") == "1") On_WorldGen.KillTile += TraceLarva;
        }
        if (enabled && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_TRACE_PRIMARY") == "1")
        {
            primaryHook = (original, instance) =>
            {
                var player = instance.Player;
                bool before = player.controlUseItem;
                int slot = player.selectedItem;
                original(instance);
                if (player != Main.LocalPlayer || Main.gameMenu || Main.GameUpdateCount % 15 != 0) return;
                File.AppendAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "primary-input.jsonl"), new JsonObject
                {
                    ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount, ["before_use"] = before, ["before_slot"] = slot,
                    ["primary_use"] = player.controlUseItem, ["primary_slot"] = player.selectedItem,
                    ["type"] = player.HeldItem.type, ["animation"] = player.itemAnimation, ["item_time"] = player.itemTime,
                    ["mouse_left"] = Main.mouseLeft, ["trigger_left"] = Terraria.GameInput.PlayerInput.Triggers.Current.MouseLeft,
                    ["nav"] = RecedingNav.Active, ["ssp"] = StateSpacePlanner.IsActive,
                    ["item_use"] = ItemUseCoordinator.IsActive, ["mine"] = MineCoordinator.IsActive,
                    ["place"] = PlaceCoordinator.IsActive, ["place_action"] = PlaceAction.IsRunning,
                    ["legacy_combat"] = Combat.Enabled, ["x"] = player.position.X, ["y"] = player.position.Y
                }.ToJsonString() + Environment.NewLine);
            };
            MonoModHooks.Add(typeof(StateSnapshotPlayer).GetMethod("ApplyPrimaryControls", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic), primaryHook);
        }
        if (enabled && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MATERIAL_NAV_STUB") == "1")
        {
            materialNavigationHook = (original, x, y, mode) =>
            {
                if (!materialNavigationStub) { original(x, y, mode); return; }
                var player = Main.LocalPlayer;
                bool feetTarget = mode is RecedingNav.Mode.Snap or RecedingNav.Mode.Stand;
                var destination = new Vector2(x * 16 + 8 - player.width / 2f,
                    feetTarget ? (y + 1) * 16 - player.height : y * 16 + 8 - player.height / 2f);
                if (mode == RecedingNav.Mode.Stand)
                {
                    bool fits = false;
                    foreach (int offset in new[] { 0, -2, 2, -4, 4, -6, 6, -7, 7 })
                    {
                        var candidate = destination + new Vector2(offset, 0);
                        if (Collision.SolidCollision(candidate, player.width, player.height)) continue;
                        destination = candidate; fits = true; break;
                    }
                    if (!fits)
                    {
                        RecedingNav.Active = false; RecedingNav.LastStop = "test_stand_body_blocked";
                        Console.WriteLine($"MATERIAL_TEST_NAV_REJECT x={x} y={y} mode={mode} reason=body_blocked navigation=teleport_stub");
                        return;
                    }
                }
                player.Teleport(destination);
                player.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                player.fallStart = player.fallStart2 = (int)(player.position.Y / 16f);
                if (feetTarget && (ActExecutor.OriginCy(player) != y || player.Bottom.Y != (y + 1) * 16))
                    throw new Exception("material navigation substitute violated feet-cell arrival contract");
                RecedingNav.Active = false; RecedingNav.LastStop = "done";
                Console.WriteLine($"MATERIAL_TEST_NAV x={x} y={y} mode={mode} center={player.Center} feet={player.Bottom.Y} body_blocked={Collision.SolidCollision(player.position, player.width, player.height)} navigation=teleport_stub");
            };
            MonoModHooks.Add(typeof(RecedingNav).GetMethod("Start", new[] { typeof(int), typeof(int), typeof(RecedingNav.Mode) }), materialNavigationHook);
        }
    }
    public override void Unload()
    {
        if (enabled)
        {
            On_Main.Update -= Open; IL_Main.DoUpdate -= Focus;
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MINING_SPEED_TRIAL") == "1" || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_QUEEN_MINING_REPLAY") == "1") On_Player.PickTile -= MeasureMining;
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_QUEEN_GOAL") == "1") On_WorldGen.KillTile -= TraceLarva;
        }
    }
    static void TraceLarva(On_WorldGen.orig_KillTile original, int x, int y, bool fail, bool effectOnly, bool noItem)
    {
        bool larva = Main.tile[x, y].HasTile && Main.tile[x, y].TileType == TileID.Larva;
        original(x, y, fail, effectOnly, noItem);
        if (!larva || Main.tile[x, y].HasTile) return;
        var player = Main.LocalPlayer;
        Console.WriteLine("QUEEN_LARVA_BREAK " + new JsonObject { ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount,
            ["x"] = x, ["y"] = y, ["held_type"] = player.HeldItem.type, ["animation"] = player.itemAnimation,
            ["player_x"] = player.Center.X / 16, ["player_y"] = player.Center.Y / 16,
            ["source_stack"] = Environment.StackTrace }.ToJsonString());
    }
    void MeasureMining(On_Player.orig_PickTile original, Player player, int x, int y, int power)
    {
        var sample = miningMeasurement;
        bool matching = player == Main.LocalPlayer && sample != null && sample["x"].GetValue<int>() == x && sample["y"].GetValue<int>() == y;
        if (matching)
        {
            sample["first_hit_tick"] ??= JsonValue.Create((long)Main.GameUpdateCount);
            sample["hits"] = (sample["hits"]?.GetValue<int>() ?? 0) + 1;
            sample["effective_pick_speed"] = player.pickSpeed;
            sample["pick_power"] = power;
            sample["use_time"] = player.HeldItem.useTime;
            sample["use_animation"] = player.HeldItem.useAnimation;
            sample["effective_use_time"] = CombinedHooks.TotalUseTime(player.HeldItem.useTime * player.pickSpeed, player, player.HeldItem);
            sample["effective_animation"] = CombinedHooks.TotalAnimationTime(player.HeldItem.useAnimation, player, player.HeldItem);
        }
        original(player, x, y, power);
        if (matching && !Main.tile[x, y].HasTile)
            sample["mining_frames"] = (long)Main.GameUpdateCount - sample["first_hit_tick"].GetValue<long>() + 1;
    }
    static void Focus(ILContext il)
    {
        var cursor = new ILCursor(il);
        if (!cursor.TryGotoNext(MoveType.After, instruction => instruction.MatchStsfld<Main>("hasFocus"))) throw new Exception("focus hook missing");
        cursor.EmitDelegate<Action>(() => Main.hasFocus = true);
    }
    void Open(On_Main.orig_Update original, Main game, GameTime time)
    {
        original(game, time);
        if (opened || Main.menuMode != 0) return;
        opened = true;
        if (Terraria.Social.SocialAPI.Cloud != null) Terraria.Social.SocialAPI.Cloud.EnabledByDefault = false;
        WorldGen.clearWorld();
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(QueenResumeRoot))
        {
            foreach (string relative in new[] { "Worlds/Checks.wld", "Worlds/Checks.twld", "Players/JS_behavior_trial.plr", "Players/JS_behavior_trial.tplr" })
            {
                string destination = Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, relative);
                Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(destination));
                File.Copy(Path.Combine(QueenResumeRoot, relative), destination, true);
            }
            string programs = Path.Combine(QueenResumeRoot, "TerraBlindAgent", "programs");
            if ((ProgressionTrial || FullQueenRun) && Directory.Exists(programs))
                foreach (string source in Directory.EnumerateFiles(programs, "*", SearchOption.AllDirectories))
                {
                    string destination = Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "TerraBlindAgent", "programs", Path.GetRelativePath(programs, source));
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(destination)); File.Copy(source, destination, true);
                }
            if (ProgressionTrial)
                foreach (string pattern in new[] { "progression-*-start.json", "progression-*-progress.json" })
                    foreach (string source in Directory.EnumerateFiles(QueenResumeRoot, pattern))
                        File.Copy(source, Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, Path.GetFileName(source)), true);
        }
        WorldFile.GetAllMetadata(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "Worlds", "Checks.wld"), false).SetAsActive();
        var player = string.IsNullOrEmpty(QueenResumeRoot) ? PlayerFileData.CreateAndSave(new Player { name = "JS behavior trial" })
            : Player.LoadPlayer(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "Players", "JS_behavior_trial.plr"), false);
        if (player.IsCloudSave || !player.Path.StartsWith(Main.SavePath, StringComparison.Ordinal)) throw new Exception("nonisolated player");
        Player.LoadPlayer(player.Path, false).SetAsActive(); WorldGen.playWorld();
    }
    Task<T> OnMain<T>(Func<T> body)
    {
        var done = new TaskCompletionSource<T>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        queue.Enqueue(() => { try { done.SetResult(body()); } catch (Exception error) { done.SetException(error); } });
        return done.Task;
    }
    public override void PreUpdatePlayers()
    {
        if (!enabled || Main.gameMenu || Manual.On || FullQueenRun) return;
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_IDLE_INPUT_PROBE") == "1")
        {
            Terraria.GameInput.PlayerInput.Triggers.Current.MouseLeft = true;
            Main.mouseLeft = true;
        }
        Terraria.GameInput.PlayerInput.Triggers.Current.Reset();
        Main.mouseLeft = Main.mouseRight = false;
    }
    public override void PostUpdateEverything()
    {
        if (!enabled || finished) return;
        Main.soundVolume = Main.musicVolume = Main.ambientVolume = 0; Main.autoPause = false;
        while (queue.TryDequeue(out var work)) work();
        if (Main.gameMenu || !Main.LocalPlayer.active) return;
        if (frames++ == 0) Main.playerInventory = true;
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_QUEEN_MINING_REPLAY") == "1" && frames % 60 == 0 && MineCoordinator.IsActive)
            Console.WriteLine($"MINING_REPLAY_INPUT tick={Main.GameUpdateCount} slot={Main.LocalPlayer.selectedItem} use={Main.LocalPlayer.controlUseItem} animation={Main.LocalPlayer.itemAnimation} time={Main.LocalPlayer.itemTime} delay={Main.LocalPlayer.delayUseItem} mouse={Main.LocalPlayer.mouseInterface} target={Player.tileTargetX},{Player.tileTargetY} state={MineCoordinator.StatusJson()}");
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SKILL_LEARNING") == "1")
            foreach (var npc in Main.npc)
                if (npc.active && !learningTargets.Contains(npc.whoAmI)) npc.active = false;
        if ((ShortJungleTrial || ProgressionTrial) && shortJungleClock != null && firstJungleAction == null
            && ((AgentControlSession)SessionField.GetValue(null)).PrimaryActive)
        {
            var policy = (JsonObject)typeof(AgentTools).GetMethod("TaskPolicyStatus", HiddenStatic).Invoke(null, new object[] { false });
            firstJungleAction = new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = shortJungleClock.ElapsedMilliseconds,
                ["tool"] = ((AgentControlSession)SessionField.GetValue(null)).Tool, ["task_policy"] = policy };
        }
        if (ProgressionTrial && shortJungleClock != null)
        {
            foreach (var item in Main.LocalPlayer.inventory)
                if (!item.IsAir && item.type is ItemID.NightmarePickaxe or ItemID.DeathbringerPickaxe or ItemID.Waterleaf
                    or ItemID.Fireblossom or ItemID.Obsidian or ItemID.Hellforge or ItemID.ObsidianSkinPotion) progressionItems.Add(item.type);
            protectedLavaContact |= Main.LocalPlayer.lavaImmune && Main.LocalPlayer.HasBuff(BuffID.ObsidianSkin)
                && Collision.LavaCollision(Main.LocalPlayer.position, Main.LocalPlayer.width, Main.LocalPlayer.height);
            var damage = (JsonObject)DamageField.GetValue(null);
            if (damage != null && damage["tick"].GetValue<long>() != lastProgressionDamage)
            {
                lastProgressionDamage = damage["tick"].GetValue<long>();
                File.AppendAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "progression-" + progressionStage + "-damage.jsonl"),
                    new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = shortJungleClock.ElapsedMilliseconds,
                        ["evidence"] = damage.DeepClone() }.ToJsonString() + Environment.NewLine);
            }
            if (progressionStage == "prime")
            {
                int current = Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Where(i => i.type == ItemID.MechanicalSkull).Sum(i => i.stack);
                if (primeSummon == null && current < mechanicalSkulls && Main.LocalPlayer.itemAnimation > 0
                    && NPC.AnyNPCs(NPCID.SkeletronPrime))
                    primeSummon = new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = shortJungleClock.ElapsedMilliseconds,
                        ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount, ["skulls_before"] = mechanicalSkulls, ["skulls_after"] = current,
                        ["boss_active"] = true, ["item_animation"] = Main.LocalPlayer.itemAnimation };
                mechanicalSkulls = current;
            }
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_OPERATOR_COMMANDS") == "1" && frames % 30 == 0 && operatorRequest.IsCompleted)
        {
            string requestPath = Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "operator-request.json");
            if (File.Exists(requestPath))
            {
                var request = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(requestPath)).AsObject();
                File.Move(requestPath, Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "operator-running.json"), true);
                operatorRequest = Task.Run(async () =>
                {
                    var result = new JsonObject { ["id"] = request["id"]?.DeepClone() };
                    try { result["result"] = JsonNode.Parse(await AgentTools.Execute(request["tool"].ToString(), request["args"]?.AsObject() ?? new JsonObject(), default)); }
                    catch (Exception error) { result["error"] = error.ToString(); }
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "operator-result.json"), result.ToJsonString());
                });
            }
        }
        if (!NaturalEncounters)
            for (int i = 0; i < Main.maxNPCs; i++)
                if (i != target || collectingStingers && Main.npc[i].type != NPCID.Hornet) Main.npc[i].active = false;
        var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
        if (FullQueenRun)
        {
            if (queenPreviouslyDead && !p.dead) Main.playerInventory = true;
            queenPreviouslyDead = p.dead;
        }
        if (queenClock != null)
        {
            double now = queenClock.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds, elapsed = now - queenSampleMs;
            queenSampleMs = now;
            bool covered = RecedingNav.Active || MineCoordinator.IsActive || ItemUseCoordinator.IsActive || ActExecutor.IsActive
                || PlaceAnywhere.IsRunning || FightCoordinator.IsActive || FightCoordinator.AttackAnimationActive
                || (bool)EscapeField.GetValue(null) || p.itemAnimation > 0 || p.controlUseItem
                || p.controlLeft || p.controlRight || p.controlJump;
            if (p.dead) queenRespawnMs += elapsed;
            else if (covered) queenCoveredMs += elapsed;
            else queenUncoveredMs += elapsed;
            if (p.dead || !queenPositionTracked || Vector2.DistanceSquared(p.position, queenPositionAnchor) > 64)
            {
                if (queenPositionTracked && queenStationaryWarned)
                    Console.WriteLine("QUEEN_STATIONARY_END " + new JsonObject {
                        ["started_ms"] = queenStationaryStart, ["ended_ms"] = now, ["duration_ms"] = now - queenStationaryStart }.ToJsonString());
                queenPositionAnchor = p.position; queenStationaryStart = now;
                queenPositionTracked = !p.dead; queenStationaryWarned = false;
            }
            else if (!queenStationaryWarned && now - queenStationaryStart >= 2000)
            {
                queenStationaryWarned = true;
                var phase = RecedingNav.Active && !(bool)FieldReadyProperty.GetValue(null) ? "navigation_compute"
                    : p.itemAnimation > 0 || p.controlUseItem ? p.HeldItem.pick > 0 || p.HeldItem.axe > 0 ? "mining"
                        : p.HeldItem.createTile >= 0 ? "placement" : "attack_animation"
                    : (bool)EscapeField.GetValue(null) ? "survival_recovery"
                    : (int)QueryField.GetValue(null) > 0 ? "tool_query"
                    : ((AgentControlSession)SessionField.GetValue(null)).PrimaryActive ? "action_wait"
                    : (bool)ProcessingProperty.GetValue(null) ? "model_wait" : "idle";
                var stalled = new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = now, ["started_ms"] = queenStationaryStart,
                    ["phase"] = phase, ["x"] = p.Center.X / 16, ["y"] = p.Center.Y / 16,
                    ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["held"] = p.HeldItem.type, ["animation"] = p.itemAnimation,
                    ["item_time"] = p.itemTime, ["navigation"] = RecedingNav.Active,
                    ["mining"] = MineCoordinator.IsActive, ["placement"] = PlaceAnywhere.IsRunning,
                    ["primary_tool"] = ((AgentControlSession)SessionField.GetValue(null)).Tool,
                    ["agent_status"] = NativeAgent.Status };
                Console.WriteLine("QUEEN_STATIONARY_WARNING " + stalled.ToJsonString());
                File.AppendAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "queen-stationary.jsonl"), stalled.ToJsonString() + Environment.NewLine);
            }
            if (RecedingNav.Active && !(bool)FieldReadyProperty.GetValue(null)) queenComputeMs += elapsed;
            if (!p.dead && !covered)
            {
                if (queenGapStart < 0) { queenGapStart = now; queenGapWarned = false; }
                if (!queenGapWarned && now - queenGapStart >= 10000)
                {
                    queenGapWarned = true;
                    Console.WriteLine("QUEEN_UNCOVERED_WARNING " + QueenCoverage().ToJsonString());
                }
            }
            else if (queenGapStart >= 0)
            {
                queenGaps.Add(new JsonObject { ["started_ms"] = queenGapStart, ["ended_ms"] = now,
                    ["duration_ms"] = now - queenGapStart });
                queenGapStart = -1;
            }
        }
        if (handoffProbeTick)
            typeof(AgentTools).GetMethod("ContinuePreparedNode", HiddenStatic).Invoke(null, new object[] { CancellationToken.None });
        if (samplingEvasion)
            evasionFrames.Add(new JsonObject { ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount, ["x"] = p.Center.X / 16,
                ["time_ms"] = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() * 1000.0 / Stopwatch.Frequency,
                ["y"] = p.Center.Y / 16, ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["hurt_tile"] = Collision.HurtTiles(p.position, p.width, p.height, p).type,
                ["left"] = p.controlLeft, ["right"] = p.controlRight, ["jump"] = p.controlJump });
        if (dungeonSampling)
        {
            int keys = p.inventory.Where(i => i.type == ItemID.GoldenKey).Sum(i => i.stack);
            dungeonKeyObserved |= keys > 0;
            dungeonMaxKeys = Math.Max(dungeonMaxKeys, keys);
            double now = dungeonClock.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds, elapsed = now - dungeonSampleMs;
            dungeonSampleMs = now;
            string phase = p.dead ? "respawn" : RecedingNav.Active && !(bool)FieldReadyProperty.GetValue(null) ? "navigation_compute"
                : FightCoordinator.AttackAnimationActive || p.HeldItem.damage > 0 && p.HeldItem.pick == 0
                    && p.HeldItem.axe == 0 && p.HeldItem.hammer == 0 && (p.itemAnimation > 0 || p.controlUseItem) ? "combat_animation"
                : p.itemAnimation > 0 || p.itemTime > 0 || p.controlUseItem
                    ? p.HeldItem.pick > 0 || p.HeldItem.axe > 0 ? "mining"
                        : p.HeldItem.createTile >= 0 ? "placement" : "other_animation"
                : p.velocity.LengthSquared() > .04f ? "travel"
                : (bool)EscapeField.GetValue(null) ? "survival_recovery"
                : (int)QueryField.GetValue(null) > 0 ? "tool_query"
                : ((ActionTasks)ActionsField.GetValue(null)).HasPrimaryControl || ((AgentControlSession)SessionField.GetValue(null)).PrimaryActive ? "tool_wait"
                : (bool)ProcessingProperty.GetValue(null) ? "model_wait" : "idle";
            dungeonTimes[phase] = dungeonTimes.GetValueOrDefault(phase) + elapsed;
            if (Vector2.DistanceSquared(p.position, dungeonPreviousPosition) < .01f)
                dungeonStationary[phase] = dungeonStationary.GetValueOrDefault(phase) + elapsed;
            if (dungeonPreviouslyDead && !p.dead) dungeonRespawnHp += Math.Max(0, p.statLife);
            else if (!dungeonPreviouslyDead)
            {
                dungeonHpLost += Math.Max(0, dungeonPreviousHp - Math.Max(0, p.statLife));
                dungeonHpRecovered += Math.Max(0, p.statLife - dungeonPreviousHp);
            }
            dungeonPreviousPosition = p.position; dungeonPreviousHp = p.statLife; dungeonPreviouslyDead = p.dead;
            var damage = (JsonObject)DamageField.GetValue(null);
            string damageKey = damage?["tick"] + ":" + damage?["kind"];
            if (damage != null && damageKey != dungeonDamageKey)
            {
                dungeonDamageKey = damageKey;
                dungeonDamageCount++;
                dungeonDamageTotal += damage["damage"].GetValue<double>();
                File.AppendAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "dungeon-damage.jsonl"), new JsonObject {
                    ["elapsed_ms"] = now, ["damage"] = damage.DeepClone(),
                    ["terrain"] = TerrainInspection.Read(6) }.ToJsonString() + Environment.NewLine);
            }
        }
        acceptance?.Observe(p);
        if (FullCraftTrial)
        {
            p.AddBuff(BuffID.Gills, 3600);
            p.AddBuff(BuffID.Shine, 3600);
        }
        if (samplingLanding)
        {
            var chosen = (Point)typeof(NavigationSurvival).GetField("webTarget", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(null);
            landingFrames.Add(new JsonObject { ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount, ["x"] = p.position.X, ["y"] = p.position.Y,
                ["vx"] = p.velocity.X, ["vy"] = p.velocity.Y, ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["dead"] = p.dead,
                ["fall_start"] = p.fallStart, ["held"] = p.HeldItem.type, ["slot"] = p.selectedItem,
                ["animation"] = p.itemAnimation, ["item_time"] = p.itemTime, ["use_item"] = p.controlUseItem,
                ["attack_animation"] = FightCoordinator.AttackAnimationActive,
                ["web_count"] = p.inventory.Where(it => it.type == ItemID.Cobweb).Sum(it => it.stack),
                ["chosen_x"] = chosen.X, ["chosen_y"] = chosen.Y, ["tile_x"] = Player.tileTargetX, ["tile_y"] = Player.tileTargetY,
                ["chosen_type"] = Main.tile[chosen.X, chosen.Y].HasTile ? (int)Main.tile[chosen.X, chosen.Y].TileType : -1 });
        }
        if (samplingSources)
        {
            int x = ItemUseCoordinator.SnappedWx, y = ItemUseCoordinator.SnappedWy;
            bool hasTile = x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < Main.maxTilesX && y < Main.maxTilesY && Main.tile[x, y].HasTile;
            sourceInputSamples.Add($"{Main.GameUpdateCount},{ItemUseCoordinator.IsActive},{ItemUseCoordinator.Outcome},{x},{y},{hasTile},{p.controlUseItem},{p.itemAnimation},{p.itemTime},{p.HeldItem.type}");
        }
        if (!NaturalEncounters && collectingStingers && frames % 60 == 0 && hornetsSpawned < 40
            && (target < 0 || !Main.npc[target].active) && p.inventory.Where(i => i.type == ItemID.Stinger).Sum(i => i.stack) < stingersRequired)
            SpawnMaterialHornet();
        if (p.controlUseItem && Math.Abs(p.velocity.X) + Math.Abs(p.velocity.Y) > 0.2f) movingAttackFrames++;
        if (frames == 60) trial = Task.Run(Run);
        if (frames % 120 == 0)
        {
            var npc = target >= 0 && target < Main.maxNPCs ? Main.npc[target] : null;
            Console.WriteLine($"BEHAVIOR_SAMPLE frame={frames} pos={p.position} hp={p.statLife} moving_attack={movingAttackFrames}"
                + $" attack={p.controlUseItem} target_active={npc?.active} target_hp={npc?.life} target_pos={npc?.position}"
                + $" clear_line={(npc != null && Collision.CanHitLine(p.Center, 4, 4, npc.Center, 4, 4))}");
        }
        if (trial?.IsCompleted == true || !FullQueenRun && !JungleHealthTrial && !ShortJungleTrial && !ProgressionTrial && frames > FrameLimit)
        {
            finished = true;
            bool ok = trial?.IsCompletedSuccessfully == true;
            string detail = ok ? $"BEHAVIOR_COMPLETE moving_attack_frames={movingAttackFrames}" : trial?.Exception?.GetBaseException().ToString() ?? "behavior frame deadline";
            Console.WriteLine(detail);
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "agent-engine-result.json"), new JsonObject { ["ok"] = ok, ["detail"] = detail }.ToJsonString());
            Console.Out.Flush();
            if (ok || !FullQueenRun) Environment.Exit(ok ? 0 : 1);
        }
    }
    static async Task<JsonObject> Call(string tool, JsonObject args)
    {
        var result = JsonNode.Parse(await AgentTools.Execute(tool, args, default)).AsObject();
        if (tool != "get_state")
            Console.WriteLine("BEHAVIOR_TOOL " + tool + " " + result.ToJsonString());
        return result;
    }
    bool SpawnMaterialHornet()
    {
        var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
        int selected = CombatLoadout.Select(p);
        if (selected >= 0)
        {
            var weapon = p.inventory[selected];
            Console.WriteLine($"MATERIAL_LOADOUT slot={selected} type={weapon.type} name={weapon.Name} use_ammo={weapon.useAmmo} has_ammo={p.HasAmmo(weapon)}");
        }
        foreach (int dx in new[] { 96, -96, 64, -64 })
            foreach (int dy in new[] { -64, 0, 64 })
            {
                var position = p.Center + new Vector2(dx, dy);
                if (Collision.SolidCollision(position - new Vector2(20), 40, 40)
                    || !Collision.CanHitLine(p.Center, 4, 4, position, 4, 4)) continue;
                target = NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_Misc("OpportunityMaterialTrial"), (int)position.X, (int)position.Y, NPCID.Hornet);
                hornetsSpawned++;
                Console.WriteLine($"MATERIAL_HORNET spawn={hornetsSpawned} who={target} hp={Main.npc[target].life} pos={Main.npc[target].position}");
                return true;
            }
        return false;
    }
    async Task TaskPolicyTrial()
    {
        int cx = Main.spawnTileX + 30, floor = Main.spawnTileY + 5;
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); AgentTools.NewTask();
            Manual.On = false; Concessions.Enabled = false;
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false; AgentPermissions.Attack = AgentPermissions.Dodge = false;
            for (int x = cx - 5; x <= cx + 28; x++)
            for (int y = floor - 8; y <= floor + 1; y++)
            {
                var tile = Main.tile[x, y]; tile.ClearEverything();
                if (y >= floor) { tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Dirt; }
            }
            foreach (int x in new[] { cx + 3, cx + 16 })
            {
                WorldGen.PlaceTile(x, floor - 1, TileID.PressurePlates, true, true);
                if (!Main.tile[x, floor - 1].HasTile || Main.tile[x, floor - 1].TileType != TileID.PressurePlates)
                    throw new Exception("native task policy plate placement failed");
            }
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe);
            p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.Wood); p.inventory[1].stack = 9999;
            p.position = new Vector2(cx * 16, floor * 16 - p.height); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.position.Y / 16);
            return true;
        });
        var policy = await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "set", ["expected_revision"] = 0,
            ["code"] = ScriptLibrary.Example("safety_reactions")["code"].DeepClone() });
        if (policy["active"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("native task policy installation failed: " + policy);
        var steps = new JsonArray();
        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        {
            int plateX = cx + (i == 0 ? 3 : 16), goalX = cx + (i == 0 ? 6 : 20);
            steps.Add(new JsonObject { ["id"] = "leg" + i, ["description"] = "native pressure plate leg " + i,
                ["depends_on"] = i == 0 ? new JsonArray() : new JsonArray("leg0"),
                ["script"] = "return step('travel',()=>tool('nav_to',{x:" + goalX + ",y:" + (floor - 1) + "}));",
                ["conditions"] = new JsonArray(new JsonObject { ["kind"] = "tiles", ["type"] = -1, ["count"] = 1,
                    ["x"] = plateX, ["y"] = floor - 1, ["width"] = 1, ["height"] = 1 }) });
        }
        await Call("task_plan", new JsonObject { ["goal"] = "native task policy across nodes", ["mode"] = "dag", ["steps"] = steps });
        var timer = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        var results = new JsonArray();
        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        {
            var result = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["task_node"] = "leg" + i });
            if (result["ok"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("native shared policy leg failed: " + result);
            var verified = await Call("task_step", new JsonObject { ["id"] = "leg" + i, ["action"] = "verify" });
            if (verified["verified"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("native plate removal was not verified: " + verified);
            results.Add(new JsonObject { ["node"] = "leg" + i, ["elapsed_ms"] = timer.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["result"] = result });
        }
        var policyAfter = await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "get" });
        var report = await OnMain(() => new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = timer.ElapsedMilliseconds,
            ["plate1_remaining"] = Main.tile[cx + 3, floor - 1].HasTile,
            ["plate2_remaining"] = Main.tile[cx + 16, floor - 1].HasTile,
            ["plates_picked"] = Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Where(i => i.createTile == TileID.PressurePlates).Sum(i => i.stack),
            ["hp"] = Main.LocalPlayer.statLife, ["policy"] = policyAfter.DeepClone(), ["nodes"] = results,
            ["model_calls"] = 0, ["fixture_terrain"] = true });
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "task-policy-native.json"), report.ToJsonString());
        if (report["plate1_remaining"].GetValue<bool>() || report["plate2_remaining"].GetValue<bool>()
            || report["plates_picked"].GetValue<int>() != 2 || report["policy"]["active"].GetValue<bool>())
            throw new Exception("native task policy acceptance failed: " + report);
        Console.WriteLine("TASK_POLICY_NATIVE_PASS nodes=2 plates_removed=2 model_calls=0 elapsed_ms=" + timer.ElapsedMilliseconds);
    }

    async Task Run()
    {
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SKILL_LEARNING") == "1") { await SkillLearningRun(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_QUEEN_MINING_REPLAY") == "1") { await QueenMiningReplay(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_WORKSTATION_REPLAY") == "1") { await WorkstationReplay(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MINING_SPEED_TRIAL") == "1") { await MiningSpeedTrial(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_ATOMIC_POLICY_TRIAL") == "1") { await AtomicPolicyTrial(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_OPEN_ITEM_PROBE") == "1")
        {
            await Call("set_combat", new JsonObject { ["auto_dodge"] = false, ["auto_attack"] = false });
            async Task<JsonObject> OpenItem(int slot)
            {
                var inventory = (await Call("get_state", new JsonObject()))["inventory_slots"].AsArray();
                var item = inventory.First(i => i["slot"].GetValue<int>() == slot);
                return await Call("open_item", new JsonObject { ["slot"] = slot, ["expected"] = new JsonObject
                    { ["type"] = item["type"].DeepClone(), ["prefix"] = item["prefix"].DeepClone(), ["stack"] = item["stack"].DeepClone() } });
            }
            int herb = await OnMain(() => Array.FindIndex(Main.LocalPlayer.inventory, 0, 50, item => item.type == ItemID.HerbBag));
            if (herb < 0) throw new Exception("native opening probe requires the naturally collected herb bag");
            var opened = await OpenItem(herb);
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                if (Main.LocalPlayer.HasItem(ItemID.GoldenKey)) throw new Exception("locked-container fixture requires no preexisting golden key");
                Main.LocalPlayer.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.LockBox); Main.LocalPlayer.inventory[0].stack = 1;
                return true;
            });
            var locked = await OpenItem(0);
            await OnMain(() => { Main.LocalPlayer.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.GoldenKey); Main.LocalPlayer.inventory[1].stack = 1; return true; });
            var unlocked = await OpenItem(0);
            var report = await OnMain(() => new JsonObject { ["herb_bag"] = opened, ["without_key"] = locked, ["with_key"] = unlocked,
                ["golden_keys_after"] = Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Where(i => i.type == ItemID.GoldenKey).Sum(i => i.stack),
                ["lockboxes_after"] = Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Where(i => i.type == ItemID.LockBox).Sum(i => i.stack),
                ["fixture_lockbox_and_key"] = true, ["model_calls"] = 0 });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "open-item-native.json"), report.ToJsonString());
            if (opened["consumed"]?.GetValue<int>() != 1 || locked["consumed"]?.GetValue<int>() != 0
                || unlocked["consumed"]?.GetValue<int>() != 1 || report["golden_keys_after"].GetValue<int>() != 0
                || report["lockboxes_after"].GetValue<int>() != 0) throw new Exception("native container/key check failed: " + report);
            Console.WriteLine("OPEN_ITEM_PASS native_herb_bag=true locked_without_key=true key_consumed=true model_calls=0");
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_LAVA_ESCAPE_CASE") is string lavaCase)
        {
            var death = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(QueenResumeRoot, "progression-hellstone-progress.json")))["deaths"].AsArray().Last()["evidence"];
            var origin = new Vector2(death["pos_tiles"]["x"].GetValue<float>() * 16, death["pos_tiles"]["y"].GetValue<float>() * 16);
            int item = lavaCase == "skin" ? ItemID.ObsidianSkinPotion : ItemID.RecallPotion;
            await Call("set_combat", new JsonObject { ["auto_dodge"] = true, ["auto_attack"] = false });
            await Call("watch", new JsonObject { ["events"] = new JsonArray("nearby_tile"), ["tile_types"] = new JsonArray(12), ["radius"] = 10 });
            int before = await OnMain(() =>
            {
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer; Config.I.AgentEnabled = true;
                origin -= new Vector2(p.width / 2f, p.height / 2f);
                p.ClearBuff(BuffID.ObsidianSkin); p.lavaTime = 0;
                if (lavaCase == "skin")
                {
                    int slot = Array.FindIndex(p.inventory, 0, 50, i => i.IsAir);
                    if (slot < 0) slot = 0;
                    p.inventory[slot].SetDefaults(item); p.inventory[slot].stack = 1;
                }
                p.Teleport(origin); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)origin.Y / 16;
                p.statLife = p.statLifeMax2; p.immune = false; p.immuneTime = 0;
                bool lava = Collision.LavaCollision(p.position, p.width, p.height), solid = Collision.SolidCollision(p.position, p.width, p.height);
                var cells = new JsonArray();
                for (int x = (int)origin.X / 16 - 1; x <= (int)(origin.X + p.width) / 16 + 1; x++)
                for (int y = (int)origin.Y / 16 - 1; y <= (int)(origin.Y + p.height) / 16 + 1; y++)
                { var t = Main.tile[x, y]; cells.Add(new JsonObject { ["x"] = x, ["y"] = y, ["tile"] = t.HasTile ? t.TileType : -1,
                    ["liquid"] = t.LiquidAmount, ["liquid_type"] = t.LiquidType, ["slope"] = (int)t.Slope, ["half"] = t.IsHalfBlock }); }
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "lava-escape-geometry.json"), new JsonObject
                { ["lava_collision"] = lava, ["solid_collision"] = solid, ["x"] = p.position.X, ["y"] = p.position.Y, ["tiles"] = cells }.ToJsonString());
                if (lavaCase == "suffocation" ? !solid : !lava || solid)
                    throw new Exception("recorded native death position does not contain the expected hazard");
                return p.inventory.Where(i => i.type == item).Sum(i => i.stack);
            });
            JsonObject report = null;
            bool suffocationSeen = false;
            var timer = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            while (timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < 5)
            {
                await Task.Delay(50);
                report = await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                    suffocationSeen |= p.suffocating;
                    return new JsonObject { ["case"] = lavaCase, ["elapsed_ms"] = timer.ElapsedMilliseconds,
                        ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["dead"] = p.dead, ["lava_immune"] = p.lavaImmune,
                        ["lava_contact"] = Collision.LavaCollision(p.position, p.width, p.height),
                        ["distance_from_danger"] = Vector2.Distance(p.position, origin), ["control_jump"] = p.controlJump,
                        ["suffocation_seen"] = suffocationSeen,
                        ["item_before"] = before, ["item_after"] = p.inventory.Where(i => i.type == item).Sum(i => i.stack),
                        ["model_calls"] = 0, ["fixture_item_grant"] = lavaCase == "skin" };
                });
                if (report["dead"].GetValue<bool>()) break;
                if (lavaCase == "skin" ? report["lava_immune"].GetValue<bool>() : report["distance_from_danger"].GetValue<float>() > 256) break;
            }
            await Task.Delay(lavaCase == "suffocation" ? 1000 : 150);
            var final = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
            report["contact_escape"] = final["contact_escape"]?.DeepClone();
            report["item_after_settle"] = await OnMain(() => Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Where(i => i.type == item).Sum(i => i.stack));
            report["jump_after_settle"] = await OnMain(() => Main.LocalPlayer.controlJump);
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "lava-escape-native-" + lavaCase + ".json"), report.ToJsonString());
            if (report["dead"].GetValue<bool>() || report["item_before"].GetValue<int>() - report["item_after"].GetValue<int>() != 1
                || report["item_before"].GetValue<int>() - report["item_after_settle"].GetValue<int>() != 1
                || lavaCase == "suffocation" && !suffocationSeen
                || (lavaCase == "skin" ? !report["lava_immune"].GetValue<bool>() || report["jump_after_settle"].GetValue<bool>()
                    : report["distance_from_danger"].GetValue<float>() <= 256)) throw new Exception("native lava rescue failed: " + report);
            Console.WriteLine("LAVA_ESCAPE_PASS case=" + lavaCase + " native_item_consumed=1 model_calls=0 elapsed_ms=" + timer.ElapsedMilliseconds);
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_LAVA_IMMUNITY_PROBE") == "1")
        {
            var target = await OnMain(() =>
            {
                for (int y = Main.maxTilesY - 250; y < Main.maxTilesY - 10; y++)
                for (int x = 10; x < Main.maxTilesX - 10; x++)
                    if (Main.tile[x, y].HasTile && Main.tile[x, y].TileType == TileID.Hellstone) return new Point(x, y);
                throw new Exception("no natural hellstone found for immunity check");
            });
            if (await OnMain(() => Main.LocalPlayer.lavaImmune || LavaSafety.CanMine(target.X, target.Y)))
                throw new Exception("unprotected native player was allowed to mine hellstone");
            await OnMain(() => { Main.LocalPlayer.AddBuff(BuffID.ObsidianSkin, 120); return true; });
            await Task.Delay(100);
            var active = await OnMain(() => new JsonObject { ["buff_active"] = Main.LocalPlayer.HasBuff(BuffID.ObsidianSkin),
                ["lava_immune"] = Main.LocalPlayer.lavaImmune, ["can_mine"] = LavaSafety.CanMine(target.X, target.Y) });
            if (!active["buff_active"].GetValue<bool>() || !active["lava_immune"].GetValue<bool>() || !active["can_mine"].GetValue<bool>())
                throw new Exception("native obsidian skin did not permit protected mining: " + active);
            await OnMain(() => { Main.LocalPlayer.ClearBuff(BuffID.ObsidianSkin); return true; });
            await Task.Delay(100);
            var expired = await OnMain(() => new JsonObject { ["lava_immune"] = Main.LocalPlayer.lavaImmune,
                ["can_mine"] = LavaSafety.CanMine(target.X, target.Y) });
            if (expired["lava_immune"].GetValue<bool>() || expired["can_mine"].GetValue<bool>())
                throw new Exception("expired native immunity retained mining permission: " + expired);
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "lava-immunity-native.json"), new JsonObject
            {
                ["target"] = new JsonObject { ["x"] = target.X, ["y"] = target.Y }, ["active"] = active, ["expired"] = expired,
                ["performed_mining"] = false, ["model_calls"] = 0, ["terrain_edits"] = 0
            }.ToJsonString());
            Console.WriteLine("LAVA_IMMUNITY_PASS native_buff=true expiration_rechecked=true model_calls=0");
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_TASK_POLICY_PROBE") == "1") { await TaskPolicyTrial(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_FALL_PLACEMENT_PROBE") == "1") { await FallPlacementTrial(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CONSUMABLE_HANDOFF_PROBE") == "1") { await ConsumableHandoffTrial(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DESERT_CONTACT_PROBE") == "1") { await DesertContactTrial(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_NATIVE_REJECTION_NATURAL") == "1") { await NativeRejectionNaturalTrial(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_NATIVE_REJECTION_PROBE") == "1") { await NativeRejectionTrial(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_REPLY_COVER_PROBE") == "1") { await ReplyCoverTrial(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_PREPARED_HANDOFF_PROBE") == "1") { await PreparedHandoffTrial(); return; }
        if (DungeonTrial) { await DungeonGoalTrial(); return; }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_EVASION_TERRAIN_PROBE") == "1")
        {
            var decision = await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false;
                Concessions.Enabled = false; Manual.On = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
                Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false;
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
                foreach (var item in p.armor) item.TurnToAir();
                p.armor[3].SetDefaults(ItemID.CobaltShield);
                p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 15; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 400;
                p.Teleport(new Vector2(3689.4753f * 16 - p.width / 2f, 783.6875f * 16 - p.height / 2f));
                p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)p.position.Y / 16;
                if (Collision.HurtTiles(p.position, p.width, p.height, p).type >= 0
                    || Collision.SolidCollision(p.position, p.width, p.height))
                    throw new Exception("evasion fixture must start clear of natural terrain damage");
                int shot = Projectile.NewProjectile(new EntitySource_Misc("EvasionTerrainTrial"),
                    p.Center + new Vector2(0, -120), new Vector2(0, 5), ProjectileID.DeathLaser, 20, 0);
                Main.projectile[shot].tileCollide = false;
                Main.projectile[shot].hostile = true;
                Main.projectile[shot].friendly = false;
                p.controlRight = true;
                var method = typeof(FightCoordinator).GetMethod("Evade", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic);
                bool up = Collision.up, down = Collision.down;
                var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                var choice = ((int Direction, bool Jump, bool Apply))method.Invoke(null, new object[] { p, null, false });
                double elapsed = watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds;
                if (Collision.up != up || Collision.down != down) throw new Exception("forecast changed native collision flags");
                FightCoordinator.Start(20);
                samplingEvasion = true;
                return new JsonObject { ["direction"] = choice.Direction, ["jump"] = choice.Jump,
                    ["apply"] = choice.Apply, ["elapsed_ms"] = elapsed, ["terrain_edited"] = false,
                    ["collision_flags_preserved"] = true };
            });
            int start = await OnMain(() => frames);
            while (await OnMain(() => frames - start) < 36) await Task.Delay(10);
            await OnMain(() => { samplingEvasion = false; FightCoordinator.Stop(); return true; });
            var evasionResult = new JsonObject { ["decision"] = decision, ["frames"] = evasionFrames.DeepClone() };
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "evasion-terrain.json"), evasionResult.ToJsonString());
            if (!decision["apply"].GetValue<bool>() || evasionFrames.Count < 30
                || evasionFrames.Any(f => f["hurt_tile"].GetValue<int>() >= 0)
                || !evasionFrames.Any(f => f["jump"].GetValue<bool>() || f["left"].GetValue<bool>() || f["right"].GetValue<bool>()))
                throw new Exception("native evasion entered a contact hazard or failed to move: " + evasionResult);
            Console.WriteLine("EVASION_TERRAIN_PASS native_engine=true model_calls=0 frames=" + evasionFrames.Count + " decision=" + decision);
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CONTACT_MINING_PROBE") == "1"
            || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CONTACT_APPROACH_PROBE") == "1"
            || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CONTACT_AUTOMATIC_PROBE") == "1"
            || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CONTACT_OWNED_PROBE") == "1"
            || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CONTACT_PERMISSION_PROBE") == "1")
        {
            bool owned = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CONTACT_OWNED_PROBE") == "1";
            bool approach = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CONTACT_APPROACH_PROBE") == "1";
            bool automatic = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CONTACT_AUTOMATIC_PROBE") == "1" || owned || approach;
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false;
                Concessions.Enabled = false; Manual.On = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
                AgentTools.NewTask(); AgentPermissions.Dodge = automatic && !owned;
                Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false;
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
                foreach (var item in p.armor) item.TurnToAir();
                p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.MoltenPickaxe);
                var armor = new[] { ItemID.MoltenHelmet, ItemID.MoltenBreastplate, ItemID.MoltenGreaves,
                    ItemID.CloudinaBottle, ItemID.HermesBoots, ItemID.CobaltShield, ItemID.LuckyHorseshoe };
                for (int i = 0; i < armor.Length; i++) p.armor[i].SetDefaults(armor[i]);
                p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 15; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 400;
                p.Teleport(new Vector2(3692.625f * 16 - p.width / 2f, 781.6875f * 16 - p.height / 2f));
                if (approach) p.Teleport(new Vector2(3689.4753f * 16 - p.width / 2f, 783.6875f * 16 - p.height / 2f));
                if (owned) p.Teleport(new Vector2(Main.spawnTileX * 16, Main.spawnTileY * 16 - p.height));
                p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.immune = false; p.immuneTime = 0;
                p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)p.position.Y / 16;
                return true;
            });
            if (approach)
                await OnMain(() => { ActExecutor.Start(new List<ActStep> { new() { Right = true, UntilKind = "frames", UntilN = 120 } }, 150); return true; });
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CONTACT_PERMISSION_PROBE") == "1")
            {
                Task<bool> CanClear() => OnMain(() => typeof(AgentContactEscape).GetMethod("ClearContact", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic)
                    .Invoke(null, new object[] { Main.LocalPlayer }) != null);
                if (!await CanClear()) throw new Exception("native contact fixture has no mining option");
                var rule = await Call("control", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "deny", ["scope"] = "task",
                    ["behaviors"] = new JsonArray("mining"), ["reason"] = "contact permission probe" });
                if (await CanClear()) throw new Exception("survival mining ignored global mining denial");
                await Call("control", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "release", ["id"] = rule["id"].DeepClone() });
                rule = await Call("control", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "deny", ["scope"] = "task",
                    ["behaviors"] = new JsonArray("mining"), ["reason"] = "protect contact tile",
                    ["region"] = new JsonObject { ["x"] = 3692, ["y"] = 783, ["width"] = 1, ["height"] = 1 } });
                if (await CanClear()) throw new Exception("survival mining ignored protected contact tile");
                await Call("control", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "release", ["id"] = rule["id"].DeepClone() });
                if (!await CanClear()) throw new Exception("released mining prohibition remained active");
                Console.WriteLine("CONTACT_PERMISSION_PASS global_deny=true region_deny=true release_restores=true native_engine=true model_calls=0");
                return;
            }
            if (automatic)
            {
                string originalHandle = null, originalWorkflow = null;
                if (owned)
                {
                    var plan = await Call("task_plan", JsonNode.Parse("""
                        {"goal":"preserve navigation through survival takeover","mode":"dag","steps":[{"id":"contact","description":"owned route",
                        "depends_on":[],"script":"step('before_contact',()=>({observed:true}));return step('route',()=>{const r=tool('nav_to',{x:3680,y:784});if(r.error)throw new Error(r.error);return r;});",
                        "conditions":[{"kind":"item","type":327,"count":99}]}]}
                        """).AsObject());
                    originalWorkflow = plan["steps"][0]["workflow"].ToString();
                    var action = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["task_node"] = "contact", ["background"] = true });
                    originalHandle = action["handle"].ToString();
                    var ready = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                    while ((await Call("get_state", new JsonObject()))["control_execution"]?["tool"]?.ToString() != "nav_to")
                    {
                        if (ready.ElapsedMilliseconds > 3000) throw new Exception("original route never acquired native input");
                        await Task.Delay(10);
                    }
                    await OnMain(() =>
                    {
                        var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                        p.Teleport(new Vector2(3692.625f * 16 - p.width / 2f, 781.6875f * 16 - p.height / 2f));
                        p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.immune = false; p.immuneTime = 0;
                        if (Collision.HurtTiles(p.position, p.width, p.height, p).type != TileID.Spikes)
                            throw new Exception("owned escape fixture did not contact a natural spike");
                        AgentPermissions.Dodge = true;
                        p.Hurt(PlayerDeathReason.ByOther(3), 60, 0);
                        ModContent.GetInstance<AgentContactEscape>().PostUpdateEverything();
                        return true;
                    });
                }
                var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                var samples = new JsonArray();
                long? safeSince = null;
                while (clock.ElapsedMilliseconds < 5000)
                {
                    await Task.Delay(50);
                    var state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                    var sample = await OnMain(() =>
                    {
                        var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                        return new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["dead"] = p.dead,
                            ["hurt_tile"] = Collision.HurtTiles(p.position, p.width, p.height, p).type,
                            ["grounded"] = Collision.SolidCollision(p.position + new Vector2(0, 1), p.width, p.height),
                            ["x"] = p.Center.X / 16, ["y"] = p.Center.Y / 16,
                            ["escape"] = state["contact_escape"]?.DeepClone(), ["active"] = state["agent"]["contact_escape_active"].DeepClone() };
                    });
                    samples.Add(sample);
                    if (approach && sample["hp"].GetValue<int>() < 400)
                        throw new Exception("predicted contact was not cleared before actual damage: " + sample);
                    if (owned && sample["escape"]?["status"]?.ToString() == "cancelled")
                        throw new Exception("original route cleanup cancelled the active survival controller: " + sample);
                    bool safe = sample["hurt_tile"].GetValue<int>() < 0 && sample["grounded"].GetValue<bool>()
                        && !sample["active"].GetValue<bool>() && !sample["dead"].GetValue<bool>();
                    if (!safe) safeSince = null;
                    else safeSince ??= clock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
                    if (safeSince != null && clock.ElapsedMilliseconds - safeSince >= 1000) break;
                }
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "contact-automatic-probe.json"), samples.ToJsonString());
                var last = samples.Last();
                if (safeSince == null || clock.ElapsedMilliseconds - safeSince < 1000)
                    throw new Exception("contact escape failed to reach safe supported ground: " + last);
                if (last["escape"]?["cleared_tiles"]?.GetValue<int>() is not > 0)
                    throw new Exception("contact probe did not exercise contact mining");
                if (approach && last["escape"]?["reason"]?.ToString() != "predicted_contact")
                    throw new Exception("approach probe did not exercise prediction before contact");
                if (owned)
                {
                    var state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                    if (state["task_plan"]?["status"]?.ToString() != "active"
                        || state["script_workflows"]?[originalWorkflow]?["steps"]?.AsArray().Count(s => s["status"]?.ToString() == "completed") != 1)
                        throw new Exception("survival takeover lost original DAG checkpoint");
                    Console.WriteLine($"CONTACT_OWNED_PASS original_handle={originalHandle} checkpoint_retained=true native_engine=true model_calls=0");
                }
                Console.WriteLine($"CONTACT_AUTOMATIC_PASS hp={last["hp"]} elapsed_ms={clock.ElapsedMilliseconds} escape={last["escape"]} native_engine=true model_calls=0");
                if (approach) Console.WriteLine("CONTACT_APPROACH_PASS hp=400 damage_events=0 native_inputs=true native_mining=true model_calls=0");
                return;
            }
            await Call("task_plan", JsonNode.Parse("""
                {"goal":"measure native contact mining","mode":"dag","steps":[{"id":"clear","description":"remove contact hazards",
                "depends_on":[],"script":"return {};","conditions":[{"kind":"item","type":327,"count":99}]}]}
                """).AsObject());
            var measurements = new JsonArray();
            for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 6; attempt++)
            {
                await Task.Delay(100);
                var contact = await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    var p = Main.LocalPlayer; var hit = Collision.HurtTiles(p.position, p.width, p.height, p);
                    bool Side(int direction) => (bool)typeof(AgentContactEscape).GetMethod("SafeSide", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic)
                        .Invoke(null, new object[] { p, direction });
                    return new JsonObject { ["type"] = hit.type, ["x"] = hit.x, ["y"] = hit.y, ["hp"] = p.statLife,
                        ["left_safe"] = Side(-1), ["right_safe"] = Side(1), ["dead"] = p.dead,
                        ["position"] = new JsonObject { ["x"] = p.Center.X / 16, ["y"] = p.Center.Y / 16 } };
                });
                measurements.Add(contact);
                if (contact["dead"].GetValue<bool>()) throw new Exception("contact mining probe died: " + measurements);
                if (contact["type"].GetValue<int>() < 0)
                {
                    await Task.Delay(500);
