                    if (await OnMain(() => !Collision.AnyHurtingTiles(Main.LocalPlayer.position, Main.LocalPlayer.width, Main.LocalPlayer.height))) break;
                    continue;
                }
                var miningResult = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["task_node"] = "clear", ["workflow"] = "contact-mining-" + attempt,
                    ["code"] = "return tool('use_item',{slot:1,x:" + contact["x"] + ",y:" + contact["y"] + "});" });
                contact["result"] = miningResult;
            }
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "contact-mining-probe.json"), measurements.ToJsonString());
            Console.WriteLine("CONTACT_MINING_MEASUREMENTS " + measurements.ToJsonString());
            return;

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DUNGEON_MINING_PROBE") == "1")

            {
                Concessions.Enabled = false;

                var deep = cells.First(cell => cell.Y > Main.worldSurface && (cell.X < Main.maxTilesX * .35 || cell.X > Main.maxTilesX * .65));

                int weakDeep = NativeDamage(deep, weak.pick), strongDeep = NativeDamage(deep, strong.pick), weakSurface = NativeDamage(surface, weak.pick);
                if (weakDeep != 0 || strongDeep <= 0 || weakSurface <= 0
                    || DigTable.MineableWith(deep.X, deep.Y, weak.pick)
                    || !DigTable.MineableWith(deep.X, deep.Y, strong.pick)
                    || !DigTable.MineableWith(surface.X, surface.Y, weak.pick)

                    || DigTable.CostFrames(deep.X, deep.Y, p, strong) >= DigTable.Unmineable)
                    throw new Exception($"native dungeon mining mismatch weak_deep={weakDeep} strong_deep={strongDeep} weak_surface={weakSurface}");

            var report = await OnMain(() => new JsonObject { ["before"] = before.DeepClone(), ["elapsed_ms"] = elapsed,
                ["original_handle"] = handle, ["after"] = after.DeepClone(), ["trap_remaining"] = Main.tile[tx, ty].HasTile,
                ["pressure_plates_picked"] = Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Where(i => i.createTile == TileID.PressurePlates).Sum(i => i.stack),
                ["persistent_workflows"] = recoveredState["script_workflows"].AsObject().Count,
                ["model_calls"] = 0, ["navigation_stub"] = UsingNavigationStub, ["terrain_edits_before_start"] = 0 });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "prepared-recovery.json"), report.ToJsonString());
            if (after["status"]?.ToString() != "completed" || report["trap_remaining"]?.GetValue<bool>() != false
                || report["persistent_workflows"]?.GetValue<int>() != 32)
                throw new Exception("native prepared recovery failed: " + report);
            Console.WriteLine("PREPARED_RECOVERY_PASS native_guard=true native_mining=true native_resume=true persistent_workflows=32 model_calls=0 elapsed_ms=" + elapsed);
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_COLLECT_REACH_PROBE") is string reachProbe)
        {
            var values = reachProbe.Split(',').Select(value => float.Parse(value,
                System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)).ToArray();

            var report = await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; Concessions.Enabled = false;

                    ["mining_reach"] = Reach.CanMine(p, tx, ty), ["target_type"] = (int)Main.tile[tx, ty].TileType,
                    ["target_present"] = Main.tile[tx, ty].HasTile };
            });
            if (!report["interaction_reach"].GetValue<bool>() || report["mining_reach"].GetValue<bool>()
                || !report["target_present"].GetValue<bool>()) throw new Exception("recorded range mismatch not reproduced: " + report);
            var timer = Stopwatch.StartNew();

            report["elapsed_ms"] = timer.ElapsedMilliseconds; report["result"] = collected;
            await OnMain(() => { report["target_remaining"] = Main.tile[tx, ty].HasTile; return true; });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "collection-reach.json"), report.ToJsonString());
            if (collected["outcome"]?.ToString() != "removed" || report["target_remaining"].GetValue<bool>())
                throw new Exception("native collection range recovery failed: " + report);
            Console.WriteLine($"COLLECT_REACH_PASS native_world=true terrain_edits_before_start=0 model_calls=0 elapsed_ms={timer.ElapsedMilliseconds}");

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_PLACE_FAILURE_PROBE") == "1")
        {

                failure["elapsed_frames"] = Main.GameUpdateCount - failure["started_tick"].GetValue<uint>();
                failure["running"] = PlaceAnywhere.IsRunning;

                p.controlUseItem = false;

                        if (player.selectedItem != 1) wrongSlot++;
                    }
                    samples.Add(new JsonObject { ["frame"] = driven, ["y"] = player.position.Y,

                        ["animation"] = player.itemAnimation, ["fall_start"] = player.fallStart });
                    endHp = player.statLife; driven++;
                };
                return true;

            finally { await OnMain(() => { BoundaryInputPlayer.Drive = null; Combat.Enabled = false; FightCoordinator.Stop(); return true; }); }
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "fall-item.json"), new JsonObject
                { ["hp_before"] = startHp, ["hp_after"] = endHp, ["wrong_slot_frames"] = wrongSlot, ["samples"] = samples }.ToJsonString());
            if (endHp != startHp || wrongSlot > 0) throw new Exception($"fall item failed hp={endHp}/{startHp} wrong_slot={wrongSlot}");
            Console.WriteLine("FALL_ITEM_PASS native_physics=true model_calls=0");
            return;

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_HONEY_SAFETY_PROBE") == "1")
        {
            var cases = new JsonArray();
            foreach (bool immersed in new[] { false, true })
            {
                int driven = 0, wetFrames = 0, lastWet = -1;
                var samples = new JsonArray();
                await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; Main.playerInventory = false;
                    AgentPermissions.Reset(); Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = Concessions.Enabled = false;
                    var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                    int x = Main.spawnTileX, y = Main.spawnTileY - 8;
                    for (int tx = x - 20; tx <= x + 20; tx++)
                    for (int ty = y - 20; ty <= y + 6; ty++)
                    {
                        var tile = Main.tile[tx, ty]; tile.ClearEverything();
                        if (ty >= y + 3 || tx == x - 20 || tx == x + 20)
                        { tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Stone; }
                        else if (ty >= y)
                        { tile.LiquidType = LiquidID.Honey; tile.LiquidAmount = 255; }
                        else if (ty == y - 1)
                        { tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Platforms; }
                    }
                    foreach (var item in p.armor) item.TurnToAir();
                    p.armor[3].SetDefaults(ItemID.CloudinaBottle); p.armor[4].SetDefaults(ItemID.HermesBoots);
                    p.position = new Vector2(x * 16, (immersed ? y + 3 : y - 1) * 16 - p.height);
                    p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.jump = 0; p.releaseJump = true; p.RefreshExtraJumps();
                    p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.position.Y / 16f);
                    var sensor = new TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.GameSensor();
                    var movement = new TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.GameMovement();
                    var intent = new TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.DodgeIntent(TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.DodgeDirection.DownRight,
                        TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.Magnitude.Small, TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.Magnitude.Small, false);
                    BoundaryInputPlayer.Drive = player =>
                    {
                        if (driven >= 240) { movement.Release(player); return; }
                        var snapshot = sensor.CapturePlayer(player, player, (ulong)(driven / 18 + 1), intent, 0);
                        var result = movement.Apply(player, intent, snapshot with { MovementReserveScale = .25f });
                        if (player.honeyWet) { wetFrames++; lastWet = driven; }
                        if (driven % 5 == 0) samples.Add(new JsonObject { ["frame"] = driven,
                            ["x"] = player.position.X, ["y"] = player.position.Y, ["honey"] = player.honeyWet,
                            ["down"] = player.controlDown, ["jump"] = player.controlJump,
                            ["applied"] = result.Applied.Direction.ToString(), ["reason"] = result.Reason });
                        driven++;
                    };
                    return true;
                });
                try { while (driven < 240) await Task.Delay(20); }
                finally { await OnMain(() => { BoundaryInputPlayer.Drive = null; return true; }); }
                cases.Add(new JsonObject { ["initially_immersed"] = immersed, ["wet_frames"] = wetFrames,
                    ["last_wet_frame"] = lastWet, ["samples"] = samples });
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "honey-safety.json"), cases.ToJsonString());
                if (immersed ? lastWet >= 100 || wetFrames == 0 : wetFrames != 0)
                    throw new Exception($"honey safety failed immersed={immersed} wet_frames={wetFrames} last_wet={lastWet}");
                Console.WriteLine($"HONEY_SAFETY immersed={immersed} wet_frames={wetFrames} last_wet={lastWet}");
            }
            Console.WriteLine("HONEY_SAFETY_PASS native_physics=true model_calls=0");
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_HONEY_MOVEMENT_PROBE") == "1")

                {

                        TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.Magnitude.Small, TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.Magnitude.Small, false);
                    BoundaryInputPlayer.Drive = player =>
                    {
                        if (driven >= 360) { movement.Release(player); return; }
                        string reason = "raw";
                        if (driver)

                            var snapshot = sensor.CapturePlayer(player, player, (ulong)(driven / 18 + 1), intent, 0);
                            reason = movement.Apply(player, intent, snapshot with { MovementReserveScale = .25f }).Reason;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            player.controlLeft = player.controlDown = false;
                            player.controlRight = player.controlUp = true;
                            player.controlJump = driven % 30 != 0;
                        }
                        if (player.honeyWet) wetFrames++;
                        else if (wetFrames > 0 && firstDry < 0) firstDry = driven;
                        highest = Math.Min(highest, player.position.Y);
                        if (driven % 5 == 0) samples.Add(new JsonObject { ["frame"] = driven,
                            ["x"] = player.position.X, ["y"] = player.position.Y, ["vx"] = player.velocity.X,
                            ["vy"] = player.velocity.Y, ["honey"] = player.honeyWet, ["jump"] = player.controlJump,
                            ["extra_jump"] = player.AnyExtraJumpUsable(), ["reason"] = reason });
                        driven++;
                    };
                    return true;
                });
                try { while (driven < 360) await Task.Delay(20); }
                finally { await OnMain(() => { BoundaryInputPlayer.Drive = null; return true; }); }
                cases.Add(new JsonObject { ["depth"] = depth, ["driver"] = driver, ["wet_frames"] = wetFrames,
                    ["first_dry"] = firstDry, ["rise_pixels"] = startY - highest, ["samples"] = samples });
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "honey-movement.json"), cases.ToJsonString());
                Console.WriteLine($"HONEY_MOVEMENT depth={depth} driver={driver} wet_frames={wetFrames} first_dry={firstDry} rise={startY - highest:0.0}");
            }
            Console.WriteLine("HONEY_MOVEMENT_MEASURED native_physics=true model_calls=0");

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_WALL_ATTACK_PROBE") == "1")

                Combat.Enabled = true; Dodge.Enabled = false;
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer; int x = Main.spawnTileX, y = Main.spawnTileY;
                for (int tx = x - 4; tx <= x + 12; tx++)
                for (int ty = y - 12; ty <= y + 2; ty++)
                {
                    Main.tile[tx, ty].ClearEverything();
                    if (ty >= y || tx == x + 3) WorldGen.PlaceTile(tx, ty, TileID.Stone, mute: true, forced: true);
                }
                foreach (var npc in Main.npc) npc.active = false;
                foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
                p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.ThornChakram); p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe);
                p.position = new Vector2(x * 16, y * 16 - p.height); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                p.statMana = p.statManaMax2 = 200; p.itemAnimation = p.itemTime = 0;
                int who = NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_Misc("wall-attack-probe"), (x + 7) * 16, y * 16, NPCID.BlueSlime);
                var enemy = Main.npc[who]; enemy.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                if (Collision.CanHitLine(p.Center, 4, 4, enemy.Center, 4, 4)) throw new Exception("native wall setup left a firing lane");
                CombatLoadout.PreferredType = ItemID.ThornChakram;
                p.selectedItem = 1; p.controlUseItem = false;
                FightCoordinator.Start(40); FightCoordinator.Tick(p);
                bool defenseBlocked = !p.controlUseItem && p.selectedItem == 1;
                FightCoordinator.Stop(); p.controlUseItem = false; p.selectedItem = 1;
                Combat.Tick();
                bool legacyBlocked = !p.controlUseItem && p.selectedItem == 1;
                enemy.boss = true;
                p.selectedItem = 0; p.controlUseItem = true;
                var sensor = new TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.GameSensor();
                var snapshot = sensor.Capture(p, 0, TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.DodgeIntent.Idle, 0);
                var aimer = new TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.ProjectileAim();
                bool bossBlocked = !aimer.TryAttack(p, snapshot, out _) && !p.controlUseItem;
                enemy.boss = false;
                p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.Vilethorn); CombatLoadout.PreferredType = ItemID.Vilethorn;
                if (ContentSamples.ProjectilesByType[p.inventory[0].shoot].tileCollide) throw new Exception("native passing weapon setup invalid");
                p.controlUseItem = false;
                FightCoordinator.Start(40); FightCoordinator.Tick(p);
                bool defensePassing = p.controlUseItem;
                FightCoordinator.Stop(); p.controlUseItem = false;
                Combat.Tick(); bool legacyPassing = p.controlUseItem;
                enemy.boss = true; p.controlUseItem = false;
                snapshot = sensor.Capture(p, 1, TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.DodgeIntent.Idle, 0);
                bool bossPassing = aimer.TryAttack(p, snapshot, out _) && p.controlUseItem;
                return new JsonObject { ["defense_blocked"] = defenseBlocked, ["legacy_blocked"] = legacyBlocked,
                    ["boss_blocked"] = bossBlocked, ["defense_passing"] = defensePassing,
                    ["legacy_passing"] = legacyPassing, ["boss_passing"] = bossPassing };
            });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "wall-target.json"), proof.ToJsonString());
            if (proof.Any(field => field.Value?.GetValue<bool>() != true)) throw new Exception("native wall policy failed: " + proof);
            Console.WriteLine("WALL_TARGET_PASS native_collision=true native_projectile_defaults=true model_calls=0");
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_PLACEMENT_OCCUPANCY_PROBE") == "1")

                || !await OnMain(() => Main.tile[placementTx, placementTy].TileType == TileID.Tombstones
                    && Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Where(item => item.type == ItemID.Wood).Sum(item => item.stack) == 200))
                throw new Exception("native occupied placement lost object evidence or changed terrain: " + proof);
            proof["mining"] = await Call("mine", new JsonObject { ["dir"] = "right", ["target_x"] = placementTx, ["target_y"] = placementTy });
            var repairPlacement = await Call("place_at", new JsonObject { ["item"] = "9", ["x"] = placementTx, ["y"] = placementTy });
            proof["repaired"] = repairPlacement.DeepClone();
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "placement-occupancy.json"), proof.ToJsonString());
            if (repairPlacement["ok"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true
                || !await OnMain(() => Main.tile[placementTx, placementTy].HasTile && Main.tile[placementTx, placementTy].TileType == TileID.WoodBlock))
                throw new Exception("explicit obstacle removal did not enable native placement: " + proof);
            Console.WriteLine("PLACEMENT_OCCUPANCY_PASS native_tombstone=true explicit_mining=true native_wood=true model_calls=0");
            return;

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_VEIN_COLLECTION_PROBE") == "1")

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MINING_DEFENSE_PROBE") == "1")
        {
            var stages = new JsonArray();

            {
                int wallX = 0, floorY = 0;

                    NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; Main.playerInventory = false;

            {
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                target = NPC.NewNPC(p.GetSource_Misc("navigation-weapon-probe"), (int)p.Center.X - 120, (int)p.Center.Y, NPCID.BlueSlime);

                enemy.damage = 1; enemy.life = enemy.lifeMax = 20000; enemy.knockBackResist = 0;
                FightCoordinator.Start(20);
                return true;
            });
            var animationClock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            while (!await OnMain(() => Main.LocalPlayer.itemAnimation > 0 && Main.LocalPlayer.HeldItem.type == ItemID.ThornChakram))

                if (animationClock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds > 3) throw new Exception("native snapshot probe did not start a real attack");
                await Task.Delay(10);

            {
                VanillaNavBridge.Enabled = true;
                return new JsonObject { ["animation"] = Main.LocalPlayer.itemAnimation, ["item_time"] = Main.LocalPlayer.itemTime,
                    ["held"] = Main.LocalPlayer.HeldItem.type, ["x"] = Main.LocalPlayer.position.X, ["y"] = Main.LocalPlayer.position.Y };
            });

                ["mode"] = "stand" });

            {

                    ["status"] = VanillaNavigationDemo.Instance.PlannerStatus, ["mismatch"] = VanillaNavigationDemo.Instance.PlannerMismatch,
                    ["stage"] = VanillaNavBridge.Stage, ["rejection"] = VanillaNavigationDemo.Instance.PlannerRejection,
                    ["x"] = Main.LocalPlayer.position.X, ["animation"] = Main.LocalPlayer.itemAnimation,
                    ["item_time"] = Main.LocalPlayer.itemTime, ["held"] = Main.LocalPlayer.HeldItem.type }));

                { ["before"] = beforeNavigation, ["navigation"] = navigation, ["samples"] = routeSamples,
                    ["elapsed_ms"] = routeClock.ElapsedMilliseconds }.ToJsonString());

                throw new Exception("native navigation did not continue from a real weapon animation: " + navigation);

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CRAFT_ADJ_TRIAL") == "1")
        {
            var observations = new JsonObject();
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; Main.playerInventory = false;
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                Point? anvil = null;
                for (int x = 20; x < Main.maxTilesX - 20 && anvil == null; x++)

                }
                if (anvil is not Point station) throw new Exception("natural anvil missing");
                p.position = new Vector2((station.X + 2) * 16, (station.Y + 1) * 16 - p.height);
                p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.position.Y / 16);

                observations["adj_before"] = p.adjTile[TileID.Anvils];
                observations["inventory_open"] = Main.playerInventory;
                return true;
            });
            observations["first"] = await Call("craft", new JsonObject { ["name"] = "ThornChakram" });
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CRAFT_ADJ_EXPECT_FIXED") == "1")
            {
                Vector2 position = default;

                {
                    var p = Main.LocalPlayer; position = p.position;
                    observations["adj_after_craft"] = p.adjTile[TileID.Anvils];
                    observations["actual_crafted"] = p.inventory.Where(i => i.type == ItemID.ThornChakram).Sum(i => i.stack);
                    p.position += new Vector2(32 * 16, 0); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    return true;
                });
                observations["recipe_far"] = await Call("recipe", new JsonObject { ["name"] = "ThornChakram" });
                await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    observations["adj_far"] = Main.LocalPlayer.adjTile[TileID.Anvils];
                    Main.LocalPlayer.position = position; Main.LocalPlayer.velocity = Vector2.Zero;

                    || !observations["adj_after_craft"].GetValue<bool>() || observations["adj_far"].GetValue<bool>()
                    || !observations["adj_back"].GetValue<bool>()) throw new Exception("craft station refresh failed: " + observations);
                Console.WriteLine("CRAFT_ADJ_PASS inventory_open=false native_crafted=1 recipe_far_and_back=true");
                return;
            }
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                Main.LocalPlayer.AdjTiles();
                observations["adj_refreshed"] = Main.LocalPlayer.adjTile[TileID.Anvils];
                return true;

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_JEV_BOUNDARY_TRIAL") == "1")
        {
            int driven = 0, rejected = 0, violations = 0, filtered = 0;
            string arena = null;
            AgentControlPolicy policy = null;
            var positions = new JsonArray();
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false;
                policy = (AgentControlPolicy)typeof(AgentTools).GetField("Control", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(null);
                policy.Reset();
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                int x = Main.spawnTileX, y = Main.spawnTileY - 5;
                for (int tx = x - 100; tx <= x + 100; tx++)
                for (int ty = y - 35; ty <= y + 10; ty++)
                {
                    var tile = Main.tile[tx, ty]; tile.ClearEverything();
                    if (ty >= y + 1) { tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Stone; }
                }
                p.position = new Vector2(x * 16, (y + 1) * 16 - p.height); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                for (int i = 0; i < p.armor.Length; i++) p.armor[i].TurnToAir();
                p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.position.Y / 16);
                arena = policy.Deny(new[] { ControlBehavior.Dodge }, ControlLifetime.Task, null,
                    "native walking confinement trial", (x - 12, y - 30, 24, 32), outside: true);
                var sensor = new TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.GameSensor();
                var movement = new TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.GameMovement();
                var snapshot = sensor.CapturePlayer(p, p, 1, TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.DodgeIntent.Idle, 0);
                BoundaryInputPlayer.Drive = player =>
                {
                    if (driven >= 1080) { movement.Release(player); return; }
                    int direction = driven < 360 ? 1 : -1;
                    var intent = new TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.DodgeIntent(direction > 0
                        ? TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.DodgeDirection.Right : TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.DodgeDirection.Left,
                        TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.Magnitude.Small, TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.Magnitude.None, false);
                    var result = movement.Apply(player, intent, snapshot);
                    if (result.Reason == "agent spatial constraint") rejected++;
                    var available = TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.GameMovement.AvailableActions(player,
                        TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.GameSensor.ScanSolids(player.Hitbox), snapshot.WorldDistances, false, false, .075f);
                    if (!available.Any(option => option.Intent == intent)) filtered++;
                    if (!policy.BoundsAllowed(ControlBehavior.Dodge, player.position.X / 16, player.position.Y / 16,
                        player.width / 16f, player.height / 16f)) violations++;
                    positions.Add(new JsonObject { ["frame"] = driven, ["x"] = player.position.X, ["y"] = player.position.Y,
                        ["vx"] = player.velocity.X, ["vy"] = player.velocity.Y, ["applied"] = result.Applied.Direction.ToString(), ["reason"] = result.Reason });
                    driven++;
                };
                return true;
            });
            try
            {
                while (driven < 1080) await Task.Delay(20);
                await OnMain(() => { BoundaryInputPlayer.Drive = null; policy.Release(arena); return true; });
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "jev-boundary.json"), new JsonObject
                { ["frames"] = driven, ["rejected"] = rejected, ["filtered"] = filtered, ["violations"] = violations, ["positions"] = positions }.ToJsonString());
                if (violations > 0 || rejected == 0 || filtered == 0) throw new Exception($"native boundary check failed violations={violations} rejected={rejected} filtered={filtered}");
                Console.WriteLine($"JEV_BOUNDARY_PASS frames={driven} rejected={rejected} filtered={filtered} violations={violations} model_calls=0 native_physics=true");
                return;
            }
            finally { await OnMain(() => { BoundaryInputPlayer.Drive = null; policy.Reset(); return true; }); }
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_TERRAIN_MAP_TRIAL") == "1")
        {
            bool building = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_HIVE_BUILD") == "1";
            bool weaponProbe = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_HIVE_WEAPON_PROBE") == "1";
            bool noticeProbe = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_BOSS_NOTICE_PROBE") == "1";
            bool observerProbe = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_BACKGROUND_BOSS_OBSERVER_PROBE") == "1";
            bool combatProbe = weaponProbe || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_HIVE_COMBAT_TRIAL") == "1";
            bool roomProbe = observerProbe || noticeProbe || combatProbe || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_HIVE_ROOM_PROBE") == "1";
            bool vision = building || roomProbe || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MAP_VISION") == "1";
            int mapX = 0, mapY = 0;
            JsonObject lake = null;
            var map = await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; Concessions.Enabled = false;
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                int x = Main.spawnTileX, y = Main.spawnTileY;
                if (vision)
                {
                    var seen = new HashSet<Point>();
                    var largest = new List<Point>();
                    for (int tx = 100; tx < Main.maxTilesX - 100; tx++)
                    for (int ty = (int)Main.worldSurface; ty < Main.maxTilesY - 200; ty++)
                    {
                        var start = new Point(tx, ty);
                        if (seen.Contains(start) || Main.tile[tx, ty].LiquidType != LiquidID.Honey || Main.tile[tx, ty].LiquidAmount < 128) continue;
                        var component = new List<Point>(); var pending = new Queue<Point>(); pending.Enqueue(start); seen.Add(start);
                        while (pending.TryDequeue(out var cell))
                        {
                            component.Add(cell);
                            foreach (var next in new[] { new Point(cell.X - 1, cell.Y), new Point(cell.X + 1, cell.Y), new Point(cell.X, cell.Y - 1), new Point(cell.X, cell.Y + 1) })
                                if (WorldGen.InWorld(next.X, next.Y, 1) && !seen.Contains(next)
                                    && Main.tile[next.X, next.Y].LiquidType == LiquidID.Honey && Main.tile[next.X, next.Y].LiquidAmount >= 128)
                                { seen.Add(next); pending.Enqueue(next); }
                        }
                        if (component.Count > largest.Count) largest = component;
                    }
                    if (largest.Count < 100) throw new Exception("natural honey lake missing");
                    var surface = largest.GroupBy(c => c.Y).OrderBy(g => g.Key).First(g => g.Count() >= 8).ToArray();
                    int left = surface.Min(c => c.X), right = surface.Max(c => c.X);
                    x = (largest.Min(c => c.X) + largest.Max(c => c.X)) / 2;
                    y = (largest.Min(c => c.Y) + largest.Max(c => c.Y)) / 2;
                    lake = new JsonObject { ["left"] = left, ["right"] = right, ["surface_y"] = surface[0].Y, ["honey_cells"] = largest.Count };
                }
                mapX = x; mapY = y;
                p.position = new Vector2(x * 16, y * 16 - p.height);
                if (building)
                {
                    int left = lake["left"].GetValue<int>(), surface = lake["surface_y"].GetValue<int>();
                    var stands = new List<Vector2>();
                    for (int tx = left - 15; tx <= left + 10; tx++)
                    for (int ty = surface - 15; ty <= surface + 10; ty++)
                    {
                        var position = new Vector2(tx * 16, ty * 16 - p.height);
                        if (!Collision.SolidCollision(position, p.width, p.height)
                            && Collision.TileCollision(position, new Vector2(0, 2), p.width, p.height).Y < 2) stands.Add(position);
                    }
                    p.position = stands.OrderBy(position => Vector2.DistanceSquared(position, new Vector2(left * 16, surface * 16 - p.height))).First();
                    for (int i = 0; i < p.inventory.Length; i++) p.inventory[i].TurnToAir();
                    p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.Starfury);
                    p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe);
                    p.inventory[2].SetDefaults(ItemID.Wood); p.inventory[2].stack = 999;
                    p.inventory[3].SetDefaults(ItemID.Rope); p.inventory[3].stack = 999;
                    p.AddBuff(BuffID.Gills, 111600); p.AddBuff(BuffID.Shine, 111600);
                    Config.I.AgentUseCodex = true; Config.I.AgentCodexModel = "gpt-6.1-sol";
                }
                p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.position.Y / 16);
                return TerrainMap.Read(new JsonObject { ["x"] = x, ["y"] = y, ["width"] = 120, ["height"] = 80,
                    ["marks"] = new JsonArray(new JsonObject { ["label"] = vision ? "VIEW" : "SPAWN", ["x"] = x, ["y"] = y }) });
            });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "terrain-map.json"), map.ToJsonString());
            if (!File.Exists(map["image_path"].ToString()) || new FileInfo(map["image_path"].ToString()).Length < 1000)
                throw new Exception("native map PNG missing or empty");
            Console.WriteLine("TERRAIN_MAP_PASS " + map);
            if (roomProbe)
            {
                if (combatProbe) await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                    int left = lake["left"].GetValue<int>(), right = lake["right"].GetValue<int>();
                    int row = lake["surface_y"].GetValue<int>() - 2;
                    for (int x = left - 1; x <= right + 1; x++)
                        if (!Main.tile[x, row].HasTile) WorldGen.PlaceTile(x, row, TileID.WoodBlock, mute: true, forced: true);
                    p.position = new Vector2(mapX * 16, row * 16 - p.height);
                    p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.position.Y / 16);
                    p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 7; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 240;
                    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(QueenResumeRoot))
                    {
                        foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
                        p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.Starfury); p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.ThornChakram);
                    }
                    Main.playerInventory = false; Config.I.AgentEnabled = true;
                    AgentPermissions.Reset();
                    return true;
                });
                var report = await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                    var solid = TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.GameSensor.ScanSolids(p.Hitbox);
                    var world = new TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.BoundaryDistances(p.Hitbox.Left,
                        Main.maxTilesX * 16f - p.Hitbox.Right, p.Hitbox.Top, Main.maxTilesY * 16f - p.Hitbox.Bottom);
                    JsonArray Options(float scale) => new(TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.GameMovement.AvailableActions(p, solid, world,
                        false, false, scale).Select(option => (JsonNode)JsonValue.Create(option.Intent.Direction.ToString())).ToArray());
                    return new JsonObject { ["solid_room_tiles"] = new JsonObject { ["left"] = solid.Left / 16,
                        ["right"] = solid.Right / 16, ["up"] = solid.Up / 16, ["down"] = solid.Down / 16 },
                        ["default_scale"] = 1, ["default_options"] = Options(1), ["compact_scale"] = .075f,
                        ["compact_options"] = Options(.075f), ["map"] = map.DeepClone() };
                });
                report["configured"] = await Call("set_combat", new JsonObject { ["movement_reserve_scale"] = .075 });
                AgentWatch noticeWatch = null;
                long bossSpawnTick = 0;
                report["actual_controller_snapshot"] = await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                    if (noticeProbe)
                    {
                        noticeWatch = (AgentWatch)typeof(AgentTools).GetField("Watches",
                            System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(null);
                        noticeWatch.Reset(); noticeWatch.EnsureDefaults(); noticeWatch.LocalDelivery = true;
                        bossSpawnTick = (long)Main.GameUpdateCount;
                    }
                    target = NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_Misc("HiveRoomProbe"), (int)p.Center.X + 120, (int)p.Center.Y, NPCID.QueenBee);
                    var sensor = (TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.GameSensor)typeof(Dodge).GetField("_sensor",
                        System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(null);
                    sensor.Observe(p);
                    var snapshot = sensor.Capture(p, 1, TerraBlind.JevariaCombat.DodgeIntent.Idle, 0);
                    return new JsonObject { ["reserve_scale"] = snapshot.MovementReserveScale,
                        ["options"] = new JsonArray(snapshot.AvailableActions.Select(option => (JsonNode)JsonValue.Create(option.Intent.Direction.ToString())).ToArray()) };
                });
                if (noticeProbe)
                {
                    JsonNode notice = null;
                    var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                    while (notice == null && clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < 2)
                    {
                        await Task.Delay(25);
                        notice = await OnMain(() => noticeWatch.Take().FirstOrDefault(e => e["kind"]?.ToString() == "boss_appeared")?.DeepClone());
                    }
                    if (notice == null) throw new Exception("native Boss appearance did not reach the watch queue");
                    long frames = long.Parse(notice["evidence"]["tick"].ToString()) - bossSpawnTick;
                    var evidence = new JsonObject { ["spawn_tick"] = bossSpawnTick, ["detection_frames"] = frames,
                        ["notice"] = notice, ["model_calls"] = 0, ["native_boss"] = true };
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "boss-notice.json"), evidence.ToJsonString());
                    await OnMain(() => { AgentTools.Stop(); noticeWatch.LocalDelivery = false; return true; });
                    if (frames > 16 || notice["evidence"]["type"].GetValue<int>() != NPCID.QueenBee)
                        throw new Exception("native Boss observation missed the sampling interval: " + evidence);
                    Console.WriteLine("BOSS_NOTICE_PASS " + evidence);
                    return;
                }
                if (observerProbe)
                {
                    bool oldRandom = Dodge.RandomBrain;
                    var evidence = new JsonArray();
                    var livePlan = (AgentTaskPlan)typeof(AgentTools).GetField("Plan", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(null);
                    var liveWatch = (AgentWatch)typeof(AgentTools).GetField("Watches", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(null);
                    try
                    {
                        await OnMain(() =>
                        {
                            Dodge.RandomBrain = true; Main.playerInventory = false;
                            Main.LocalPlayer.immune = true; Main.LocalPlayer.immuneTime = 600;
                            Main.npc[target].aiStyle = -1; Main.npc[target].velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                            return true;
                        });
                        await Call("set_combat", new JsonObject { ["boss_control"] = true, ["movement_reserve_scale"] = .075 });
                        await Task.Delay(100);
                        foreach (bool observerReview in new[] { false, true })
                        {
                            long releasedBefore = await OnMain(() =>
                            {
                                livePlan.Reset(); AgentTools.NewTask();
                                var plan = JsonNode.Parse("""
                                    {"goal":"observe native Boss control","mode":"dag","steps":[{"id":"observe","description":"observe",
                                    "depends_on":[],"script":"","conditions":[{"kind":"npc_kills","type":222,"count":1}]}]}
                                    """).AsObject();
                                plan["steps"][0]["script"] = observerReview ? "tool('decision_point',{reason:'review current movement'});"
                                    : "return tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});";
                                livePlan.Create(plan, _ => 0);
                                if (!observerReview) liveWatch.Emit("boss_appeared", "continuity-probe", new JsonObject { ["type"] = NPCID.QueenBee });
                                return (long)typeof(Dodge).GetField("_releasedAtMs", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(null);
                            });
                            var started = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["task_node"] = "observe", ["background"] = true, ["event_delivery"] = "script" });
                            var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew(); JsonObject observerResult;
                            do { await Task.Delay(25); observerResult = await Call("action_query", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = started["handle"].DeepClone() }); }
                            while (observerResult["status"]?.ToString() == "running" && clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < 3);
                            long releasedAfter = await OnMain(() => (long)typeof(Dodge).GetField("_releasedAtMs", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(null));
                            bool delivered = observerReview ? observerResult["result"]?["value"]?["wake"] != null
                                : observerResult["result"]?["value"]?["events"] is JsonArray events && events.Count > 0;
                            evidence.Add(new JsonObject { ["explicit_review"] = observerReview, ["status"] = observerResult["status"]?.DeepClone(),
                                ["delivered"] = delivered, ["release_before_ms"] = releasedBefore, ["release_after_ms"] = releasedAfter });
                            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "boss-observer-continuity.json"), evidence.ToJsonString());
                            if (!delivered || releasedAfter != releasedBefore)
                                throw new Exception("native observation cancelled itself or released Boss intent: " + evidence);
                        }
                    }
                    finally { await OnMain(() => { AgentTools.Stop(); livePlan.Reset(); Dodge.RandomBrain = oldRandom; return true; }); }
                    Console.WriteLine("BOSS_OBSERVER_CONTINUITY_PASS native_controller=true model_calls=0 random_brain=true damage_disabled_for_probe=true");
                    return;
                }
                var defaultPreview = await Call("set_combat", new JsonObject { ["movement_reserve_scale"] = 1d });
                var compactPreview = await Call("set_combat", new JsonObject { ["movement_reserve_scale"] = .075 });
                report["live_previews"] = new JsonObject { ["default"] = defaultPreview["boss_control"]?.DeepClone(),
                    ["compact"] = compactPreview["boss_control"]?.DeepClone() };
                bool Lateral(JsonNode preview) => preview?["boss_control"]?["movement_snapshot"]?["options"] is JsonArray options
                    && options.Any(option => option["direction"]?.ToString().Contains("Left") == true
                        || option["direction"]?.ToString().Contains("Right") == true);
                if (defaultPreview["boss_control"]?["movement_snapshot"]?["source"]?.ToString() != "live_preview"
                    || Lateral(defaultPreview) || !Lateral(compactPreview)
                    || compactPreview["boss_control"]?["movement_snapshot"]?["source"]?.ToString() != "live_preview")
                    throw new Exception("Boss status returned stale or inconsistent movement options: " + report);
                report["restored_reserve"] = await OnMain(() => { AgentTools.Stop(); return Dodge.MovementReserveScale; });
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "hive-room.json"), report.ToJsonString());
                if (Math.Abs(report["actual_controller_snapshot"]["reserve_scale"].GetValue<float>() - .075f) > .0001f
                    || !report["actual_controller_snapshot"]["options"].AsArray().Any(o => o.ToString() is "Left" or "Right")
                    || report["restored_reserve"].GetValue<float>() != 1)
                    throw new Exception("Agent reserve did not reach real Boss sensor or restore after stop: " + report);
                Console.WriteLine("HIVE_ROOM_OBSERVED " + report);
                if (combatProbe)
                {
                    if (weaponProbe)
                    {
                        var observed = new JsonArray();
                        foreach (int weapon in new[] { ItemID.ThornChakram, ItemID.Starfury })
                        {
                            await OnMain(() => { Main.playerInventory = true; return true; });
                            await Call("set_combat", new JsonObject { ["boss_control"] = true, ["weapon_type"] = weapon,
                                ["movement_reserve_scale"] = .075 });
                            await Task.Delay(500);
                            observed.Add(await OnMain(() => new JsonObject { ["requested"] = weapon,
                                ["actual"] = Main.LocalPlayer.HeldItem.type, ["inventory_open"] = Main.playerInventory,
                                ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount }));
                        }
                        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "hive-weapons.json"), observed.ToJsonString());
                        if (observed.Any(row => row["actual"].GetValue<int>() != row["requested"].GetValue<int>()
                            || row["inventory_open"].GetValue<bool>()))
                            throw new Exception("Boss controller ignored Agent weapon preference: " + observed);
                        Console.WriteLine("HIVE_WEAPONS_PASS " + observed);
                        return;
                    }
                    await Call("set_combat", new JsonObject { ["boss_control"] = true, ["auto_dodge"] = true,
                        ["auto_attack"] = true, ["movement_reserve_scale"] = .075,
                        ["weapon_type"] = int.Parse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_HIVE_COMBAT_WEAPON") ?? "0") });
                    if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_HIVE_COMBAT_GUARD") == "1")
                        await OnMain(() => { FightCoordinator.Start(40); return true; });
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "hive-combat-setup.json"), (await Call("get_state", new JsonObject())).ToJsonString());
                    var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew(); var samples = new JsonArray();
                    while (clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < 180)
                    {
                        await Task.Delay(500);
                        var state = await OnMain(() => new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds,
                            ["hp"] = Main.LocalPlayer.statLife, ["dead"] = Main.LocalPlayer.dead,
                            ["x"] = Main.LocalPlayer.position.X, ["y"] = Main.LocalPlayer.position.Y,
                            ["weapon_type"] = Main.LocalPlayer.HeldItem.type,
                            ["boss_hp"] = Main.npc[target].life, ["boss_active"] = Main.npc[target].active,
                            ["defeated"] = NPC.downedQueenBee });
                        state["controller"] = await Call("boss_status", new JsonObject()); samples.Add(state);
                        if (state["dead"].GetValue<bool>() || !state["boss_active"].GetValue<bool>()) break;
                    }
                    samples.Last().AsObject()["last_death"] = (await Call("get_state", new JsonObject()))["last_death"]?.DeepClone();
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "hive-combat.json"), samples.ToJsonString());
                    await Call("set_combat", new JsonObject { ["boss_control"] = false });
                    if (samples.Last()?["defeated"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true)
                        throw new Exception("native hive combat did not defeat Queen Bee: " + samples.Last());
                    Console.WriteLine($"HIVE_COMBAT_PASS elapsed_ms={clock.ElapsedMilliseconds} native_physics=true setup_hp=240 setup_weapons=Starfury,ThornChakram");
                }
                return;
            }
            if (vision && !building)
            {
                var definitions = JsonNode.Parse(Encoding.UTF8.GetString(ModLoader.GetMod("TerraBlind").GetFileBytes("Agent/Tools.json"))).AsArray();
                var tools = new JsonArray(definitions.First(t => t["function"]["name"].ToString() == "terrain_map").DeepClone());
                using var codex = new CodexAgent();
                var replies = new List<string>(); int mapCalls = 0;
                var elapsed = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                await codex.RunAsync($"调用terrain_map，中心x={mapX},y={mapY},width=120,height=80。读图片找金色蜂蜜湖，报告湖面主要连续蜂蜜段的左右世界格坐标和液面y。回复JSON：{{\"left\":整数,\"right\":整数,\"surface_y\":整数,\"layout\":\"上方空间的简短描述\"}}。",
                    Config.I.AgentCodexPath, Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "TerraBlindAgent"), "gpt-6.1-sol",
                    "通过提供的terrain_map工具观察游戏地图。工具包含图片和坐标换算，液体中金色表示蜂蜜。", tools,
                    async (name, args, token) => { mapCalls++; return await AgentTools.Execute(name, args, token); },
                    text => { replies.Add(text); Console.WriteLine("MAP_VISION_REPLY " + text); }, default);
                string reply = replies.Last();
                var answer = JsonNode.Parse(reply[reply.IndexOf('{')..(reply.LastIndexOf('}') + 1)]).AsObject();
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "map-vision.json"), new JsonObject
                { ["expected"] = lake, ["answer"] = answer, ["elapsed_ms"] = elapsed.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["map_calls"] = mapCalls,
                    ["model"] = "gpt-6.1-sol", ["usage"] = codex.TokenUsage?.DeepClone() }.ToJsonString());
                foreach (string key in new[] { "left", "right", "surface_y" })
                    if (Math.Abs(answer[key].GetValue<int>() - lake[key].GetValue<int>()) > 5)
                        throw new Exception("map vision coordinate mismatch " + key + ": " + answer + " expected=" + lake);
                if (mapCalls == 0) throw new Exception("model did not request map image");
                Console.WriteLine($"MAP_VISION_PASS calls={mapCalls} elapsed_ms={elapsed.ElapsedMilliseconds} expected={lake} answer={answer}");
            }
            if (building)
            {
                var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "hive-before.json"), lake.ToJsonString());
                    NativeAgent.Submit("/goal 观察当前蜂巢的地图，在蜂蜜湖上方铺一层连续木方块，横跨整个湖面的左右两岸，形成可走的平台。施工高度选液面上方1到3格。用真实方块逐格验收整层覆盖，并保留可继续活动的上方空间。背包有足够木材、绳子和镐子。");

                using var journal = new StreamWriter(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "hive-build-samples.jsonl")) { AutoFlush = true };

                    {
                        int left = lake["left"].GetValue<int>(), right = lake["right"].GetValue<int>(), surface = lake["surface_y"].GetValue<int>();

                            if (Enumerable.Range(left, right - left + 1).All(x => Main.tile[x, y].HasUnactuatedTile && Main.tile[x, y].TileType == TileID.WoodBlock)) return y;
                        return -1;

                    if (coveredRow >= 0 && state["task_plan"]?["status"]?.ToString() == "completed")
                    {
                        Console.WriteLine($"HIVE_BUILD_PASS elapsed_ms={clock.ElapsedMilliseconds} row={coveredRow} source={lake} native_tiles=true model=gpt-6.1-sol");

                throw new Exception("hive construction acceptance deadline");

            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_CRYSTAL_HANDOFF_TRIAL") == "1")
        {
            Point first = default, second = default;
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; Concessions.Enabled = false;
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                var hearts = new List<Point>();
                for (int x = 20; x < Main.maxTilesX - 20; x++)
                for (int y = (int)Main.worldSurface + 12; y < Main.maxTilesY - 210; y++)
                {
                    var tile = Main.tile[x, y];
                    if (!tile.HasTile || tile.TileType != TileID.Heart || tile.TileFrameX % 36 != 0 || tile.TileFrameY != 0) continue;
                    var position = new Vector2(x * 16 + 16 - p.width / 2f, (y + 2) * 16 - p.height);
                    if (Collision.SolidCollision(position, p.width, p.height)
                        || Collision.TileCollision(position, new Vector2(0, 2), p.width, p.height).Y >= 2) continue;
                    int grass = 0;
                    for (int tx = x - 12; tx <= x + 12; tx++)
                    for (int ty = y - 12; ty <= y + 12; ty++)
                        if (Main.tile[tx, ty].HasTile && Main.tile[tx, ty].TileType == TileID.JungleGrass) grass++;
                    if (grass >= 20) hearts.Add(new Point(x, y));
                }
                var pair = (from a in hearts from b in hearts
                    let distance = Vector2.Distance(a.ToVector2(), b.ToVector2())
                    where Math.Abs(a.X - b.X) >= 12 && distance is >= 20 and <= 80
                    orderby distance select (a, b)).First();
                first = pair.a; second = pair.b;

                return true;

                    {
                        var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                        bool secondRoute = RecedingNav.Active
                            && (int)typeof(RecedingNav).GetField("_goalWx", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static).GetValue(null) == second.X
                            && (int)typeof(RecedingNav).GetField("_goalWy", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static).GetValue(null) == second.Y;
                        return new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount,
                            ["crystals"] = p.inventory.Where(it => it.type == ItemID.LifeCrystal).Sum(it => it.stack),
                            ["x"] = p.position.X, ["y"] = p.position.Y, ["vx"] = p.velocity.X, ["vy"] = p.velocity.Y,
                            ["second_route"] = secondRoute, ["field_ready"] = (bool)typeof(RecedingNav).GetField("_fieldReady", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static).GetValue(null) };
                    });
                    samples.Add(sample);
                    long elapsed = sample["elapsed_ms"].GetValue<long>();
                    if (pickup < 0 && sample["crystals"].GetValue<int>() > 0) pickup = elapsed;
                    if (sample["second_route"].GetValue<bool>())
                    {
                        if (request < 0) request = elapsed;
                        if (sample["field_ready"].GetValue<bool>() && fieldReady < 0) fieldReady = elapsed;
                        if (pickup >= 0 && Math.Abs(sample["vx"].GetValue<float>()) > .01f)
                        {
                            Console.WriteLine($"CRYSTAL_HANDOFF_PASS pickup_ms={pickup} request_after_pickup_ms={request - pickup} field_after_pickup_ms={fieldReady - pickup} movement_after_pickup_ms={elapsed - pickup} model_calls=0");
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                    if (operation.IsCompleted) throw new Exception("crystal sequence ended before second movement: " + await operation);
                }
                throw new Exception("crystal handoff did not start moving within 1800 frames");
            }
            finally
            {
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "crystal-handoff.json"), samples.ToJsonString());
                cancel.Cancel();
                try { await operation; } catch (OperationCanceledException) { }
            }
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_NATIVE_STAND_TRIAL") is string standCase)
        {
            int gx = 0, gy = 0;
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; Concessions.Enabled = false;
                VanillaNavBridge.Enabled = false;
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                for (int i = 0; i < p.inventory.Length; i++) p.inventory[i].TurnToAir();
                for (int i = 0; i < p.armor.Length; i++) p.armor[i].TurnToAir();
                p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe);
                p.inventory[3].SetDefaults(ItemID.Wood); p.inventory[3].stack = 9999;
                p.inventory[5].SetDefaults(ItemID.WoodPlatform); p.inventory[5].stack = 500;
                p.selectedItem = 1; p.itemAnimation = p.itemTime = 0;
                p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 100;
                if (standCase == "recorded")
                {
                    p.position = new Vector2(48928.566f, 12838);
                    gx = 3056; gy = 803;
                }
                else
                {
                    int x = Main.spawnTileX, y = (int)Main.worldSurface + 40;
                    for (int tx = x - 12; tx <= x + 30; tx++)
                    for (int ty = y - 18; ty <= y + 3; ty++)
                    {
                        var tile = Main.tile[tx, ty]; tile.ClearEverything();
                        if (ty > y) { tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Stone; }
                    }
                    gx = x + 8; gy = y;
                    if (standCase == "platform") gy--;
                    if (standCase == "step")
                    {
                        for (int tx = x + 5; tx <= x + 12; tx++)
                        for (int ty = y - 3; ty <= y; ty++)
                        { var tile = Main.tile[tx, ty]; tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Stone; }
                        gy -= 4;
                    }
                    p.position = new Vector2(x * 16 - 2, (y + 1) * 16 - p.height);
                }
                p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.releaseJump = true;
                p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.position.Y / 16);
                Main.screenPosition = p.Center - new Vector2(Main.screenWidth, Main.screenHeight) / 2;
                var tiles = new JsonArray();
                for (int tx = gx - 1; tx <= gx + 1; tx++)
                for (int ty = gy - 2; ty <= gy + 2; ty++)
                {
                    var tile = Main.tile[tx, ty];
                    tiles.Add(new JsonObject { ["x"] = tx, ["y"] = ty, ["type"] = tile.HasTile ? (int)tile.TileType : -1,
                        ["slope"] = (int)tile.Slope, ["half"] = tile.IsHalfBlock });
                }
                Console.WriteLine("NATIVE_STAND_TERRAIN " + tiles.ToJsonString());
                RecedingNav.Start(gx, gy, RecedingNav.Mode.Stand);
                return true;
            });
            var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            var samples = new JsonArray();
            try
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < 1800; i++)
                {
                    var sample = await OnMain(() =>
                    {
                        var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                        return new JsonObject { ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount, ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds,
                            ["x"] = p.position.X, ["y"] = p.position.Y, ["vx"] = p.velocity.X, ["vy"] = p.velocity.Y,
                            ["dx"] = Math.Abs(p.Center.X - (gx * 16 + 8)), ["dy"] = Math.Abs(p.Bottom.Y - (gy + 1) * 16),
                            ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["active"] = RecedingNav.Active, ["outcome"] = RecedingNav.LastStop,
                            ["engine_owned"] = VanillaNavigationDemo.Instance.NavigationOwned, ["stage"] = VanillaNavBridge.Stage,
                            ["plan"] = VanillaNavigationDemo.Instance.LastControl.Plan };
                    });
                    samples.Add(sample);
                    if (sample["hp"].GetValue<int>() <= 0) throw new Exception("stand trial died");
                    if (!sample["active"].GetValue<bool>())
                    {
                        bool arrived = sample["outcome"]?.ToString() == "done";
                        if (standCase != "recorded" && !arrived) throw new Exception("reachable stand target failed: " + sample);
                        if (arrived && (sample["dx"].GetValue<float>() > 8 || sample["dy"].GetValue<float>() > 8 || sample["vy"].GetValue<float>() != 0))
                            throw new Exception("stand arrival violated exact position or ground contract");
                        Console.WriteLine($"NATIVE_STAND_PASS case={standCase} elapsed_ms={clock.ElapsedMilliseconds} outcome={sample["outcome"]} model_calls=0");
                        return;
                    }
                }
                throw new Exception("native stand did not produce arrival or failure within 1800 frames");
            }
            finally
            {
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "native-stand.json"), samples.ToJsonString());
                await OnMain(() => { RecedingNav.Stop(); return true; });
            }
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_WEB_LANDING_TRIAL") == "1")
        {
            int landingCase = int.TryParse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_WEB_LANDING_CASE"), out int selectedCase) ? selectedCase : 1;
            int placementType = landingCase is 4 or 6 or 7 ? TileID.Platforms : TileID.Cobweb;
            int landingHealth = 100;
            var samples = new JsonArray();
            int initialInterventions = NavigationSurvival.Interventions;
            int supportedFrames = 0;
            bool webPlaced = false;
            bool combatObserved = false;
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; Concessions.Enabled = false;
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                for (int i = 0; i < p.inventory.Length; i++) p.inventory[i].TurnToAir();
                for (int i = 0; i < p.armor.Length; i++) p.armor[i].TurnToAir();
                p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe);
                p.inventory[40].SetDefaults(ItemID.Cobweb); p.inventory[40].stack = 100;
                p.selectedItem = 0; p.itemAnimation = p.itemTime = 0;
                p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 100;
                p.immune = false; p.immuneTime = 0;
                p.position = new Vector2(10766.779f, 12102.987f);
                p.velocity = new Vector2(0, 6.990001f);
                p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = 753;
                if (landingCase is 6 or 7)
                {
                    p.inventory[40].SetDefaults(ItemID.WoodPlatform); p.inventory[40].stack = 100;
                    p.position = landingCase == 6 ? new Vector2(44804.41f, 3951.2f) : new Vector2(45100.68f, 5417.08f);
                    p.velocity = landingCase == 6 ? new Vector2(2.94f, .8f) : new Vector2(2.94f, .19f);
                    p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = landingCase == 6 ? 246 : 340;
                }
                if (landingCase == 2)
                {
                    p.position = new Vector2(51510.98f, 7018.92f); p.velocity = new Vector2(2.94f, 10.01f);
                    p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = 395;

                if (landingCase == 4)

                    int x = Main.spawnTileX, y = (int)Main.worldSurface + 40;
                    for (int tx = x - 6; tx <= x + 18; tx++)
                    for (int ty = y - 8; ty <= y + 62; ty++)
                    {

                        if (ty >= y + 60 || ty == y && tx <= x)

                    p.inventory[40].SetDefaults(ItemID.WoodPlatform); p.inventory[40].stack = 100;

                    p.Hurt(PlayerDeathReason.ByCustomReason("fall recovery fixture"), 1, 1);
                    landingHealth = p.statLife;
                    Console.WriteLine($"KNOCKBACK_START hp={p.statLife} vx={p.velocity.X} vy={p.velocity.Y}");

                    AgentPermissions.Reset(); Config.I.AgentEnabled = true;

                    if (landingCase == 10)

                        p.position.Y = 9620;
                        p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.position.Y / 16);
                    }
                    target = NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_Misc("fall-web-combat"), (int)p.Center.X + 80,
                        (int)p.Center.Y - 40, NPCID.BlueSlime);
                    var npc = Main.npc[target];
                    npc.aiStyle = -1; npc.noGravity = true; npc.noTileCollide = true;
                    npc.damage = 0; npc.life = npc.lifeMax = 100000;
                    CombatLoadout.PreferredType = ItemID.Starfury;
                    FightCoordinator.Start(100);

                if (landingCase == 1 && (!decoration.HasTile || decoration.TileType != TileID.SmallPiles || Main.tile[673, 825].HasTile))
                    throw new Exception("recorded natural landing tiles differ from the death evidence");
                var cells = new JsonArray();
                for (int y = 823; y <= 827; y++)
                    cells.Add(new JsonObject { ["y"] = y, ["active"] = Main.tile[673, y].HasTile,
                        ["type"] = Main.tile[673, y].TileType, ["solid"] = Main.tileSolid[Main.tile[673, y].TileType],
                        ["replaceable"] = TileID.Sets.BreakableWhenPlacing[Main.tile[673, y].TileType] });
                Console.WriteLine("WEB_LANDING_TILES " + cells.ToJsonString());
                cells = new JsonArray();
                int landingX = (int)p.Center.X / 16, landingY = (int)(p.Bottom.Y / 16) + 2;
                for (int x = landingX - 1; x <= landingX + 1; x++)
                for (int y = landingY - 2; y <= landingY + 2; y++)
                {
                    var tile = Main.tile[x, y];
                    cells.Add(new JsonObject { ["x"] = x, ["y"] = y, ["type"] = tile.HasTile ? (int)tile.TileType : -1,
                        ["wall"] = (int)tile.WallType, ["anchor"] = ItemUseCoordinator.HasAnchor(x, y),
                        ["slope"] = (int)tile.Slope });
                }
                Console.WriteLine("WEB_LANDING_AREA " + cells.ToJsonString());
                Main.screenPosition = p.Center - new Vector2(Main.screenWidth, Main.screenHeight) / 2;
                samplingLanding = true;
                RecedingNav.Active = true;
                if (!AxisLock.Take("fall-landing-check", Ax.Move | Ax.Jump | Ax.Vertical | Ax.Use))
                    throw new Exception("fall check could not isolate vertical descent");
                return true;
            });
            try
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < 300; i++)
                {
                    var sample = await OnMain(() =>
                    {
                        var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                        var chosen = (Point)typeof(NavigationSurvival).GetField("webTarget", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(null);
                        return new JsonObject { ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount, ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["dead"] = p.dead,
                            ["x"] = p.position.X, ["y"] = p.position.Y, ["vy"] = p.velocity.Y, ["no_fall_damage"] = p.noFallDmg,
                            ["fall_start"] = p.fallStart,
                            ["web_count"] = p.inventory.Where(it => it.type == ItemID.Cobweb).Sum(it => it.stack),
                            ["placement_stock"] = p.inventory.Where(it => !it.IsAir && it.createTile == placementType).Sum(it => it.stack),
                            ["platform_stock"] = p.inventory.Where(it => !it.IsAir && it.createTile == TileID.Platforms).Sum(it => it.stack),
                            ["held_type"] = p.HeldItem.type, ["item_animation"] = p.itemAnimation, ["item_time"] = p.itemTime,
                            ["use_item"] = p.controlUseItem, ["delay_use_item"] = p.delayUseItem, ["mouse_interface"] = p.mouseInterface,
                            ["target_x"] = Player.tileTargetX, ["target_y"] = Player.tileTargetY,
                            ["chosen_x"] = chosen.X, ["chosen_y"] = chosen.Y,
                            ["chosen_has_tile"] = Main.tile[chosen.X, chosen.Y].HasTile,

                            ["interventions"] = NavigationSurvival.Interventions,
                            ["web_above_decoration"] = Main.tile[673, 825].HasTile && Main.tile[673, 825].TileType == TileID.Cobweb,
                            ["supported"] = Collision.TileCollision(p.position, new Vector2(0, 2), p.width, p.height, false, false, 1).Y < 2 };
                    });
                    samples.Add(sample);
                    if (sample["dead"].GetValue<bool>()) throw new Exception("recorded landing still caused death");
                    if (sample["hp"].GetValue<int>() != landingHealth) throw new Exception("protected landing lost health");
                    combatObserved |= sample["held_type"].GetValue<int>() == ItemID.Starfury && sample["item_animation"].GetValue<int>() > 0;
                    if (landingCase is 9 or 10 && (sample["placement_stock"].GetValue<int>() != 100
                        || sample["interventions"].GetValue<int>() != initialInterventions))
                        throw new Exception("safe or immune fall unnecessarily reserved item use");
                    if (landingCase == 5 && sample["platform_stock"].GetValue<int>() != 100)
                        throw new Exception("lethal accumulated fall used a platform instead of resetting fall speed");
                    webPlaced |= sample["placement_stock"].GetValue<int>() < 100 && sample["chosen_has_tile"].GetValue<bool>()
                        && sample["chosen_type"].GetValue<int>() == placementType;
                    bool protectedLanding = (landingCase is 7 or 9 or 10 || webPlaced && sample["interventions"].GetValue<int>() > initialInterventions)
                        && sample["supported"].GetValue<bool>()
                        && sample["vy"].GetValue<float>() == 0;
                    supportedFrames = protectedLanding ? supportedFrames + 1 : 0;
                    if (supportedFrames >= 12)
                    {
                        if (landingCase is 9 or 10 && !combatObserved) throw new Exception("safe fall prevented ordinary weapon use");
                        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "web-landing.json"), samples.ToJsonString());
                        if (landingCase == 3)
                        {
                            var plan = JsonNode.Parse("""{"goal":"verify live underground jungle","steps":[{"id":"location","description":"native biome and layer","conditions":[{"kind":"biome","name":"jungle","depth":"below_surface","count":1}]}]}""").AsObject();
                            await AgentTools.Execute("task_plan", plan, CancellationToken.None);
                            var verified = JsonNode.Parse(await AgentTools.Execute("task_step",
                                new JsonObject { ["id"] = "location", ["action"] = "verify" }, CancellationToken.None));
                            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "biome-condition.json"), verified.ToJsonString());
                            if (verified["verified"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("native jungle cavern did not verify biome condition");
                            Console.WriteLine("BIOME_CONDITION_PASS native_engine=true fixed_destination=false model_calls=0");
                        }
                        Console.WriteLine($"WEB_LANDING_PASS case={landingCase} native_engine=true recorded_world=true existing_support={landingCase == 7} restored_plants={(landingCase is 3 or 5 ? 2 : 0)} model_calls=0");
                        return;
                    }
                }
                throw new Exception("recorded landing did not finish a protected landing in 300 frames");
            }
            finally
            {
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "web-landing.json"), samples.ToJsonString());
                await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    samplingLanding = false;
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "web-landing-frames.json"), landingFrames.ToJsonString());
                    AxisLock.Release("fall-landing-check"); FightCoordinator.Stop(); RecedingNav.Stop(); NavigationSurvival.Reset(Main.LocalPlayer); return true;
                });
            }
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DROP_IDENTITY") == "1")
        {
            await OnMain(() => { foreach (var item in Main.item) item.active = false; return true; });
            var gather = new GameGather(new[] { new GatherTarget(ItemID.Stinger, 9, Array.Empty<string>(), Array.Empty<int>()) });
            var samples = new JsonArray();
            JsonObject previous = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
            {
                int slot = await OnMain(() => Item.NewItem(new EntitySource_Misc("DropIdentityTrial"),
                    new Rectangle((int)Main.LocalPlayer.Center.X + 200, (int)Main.LocalPlayer.Center.Y, 16, 16), ItemID.Stinger));
                var candidate = (await gather.Observe(default))["candidates"].AsArray()
                    .First(c => c["kind"]?.ToString() == "drop" && c["who"]?.GetValue<int>() == slot).AsObject();
                var same = (await gather.Observe(default))["candidates"].AsArray()
                    .First(c => c["who"]?.GetValue<int>() == slot);
                if (candidate["id"].ToString() != same["id"].ToString()) throw new Exception("unchanged native drop changed identity");
                if (previous != null && (previous["who"].GetValue<int>() != slot
                    || previous["id"].ToString() == candidate["id"].ToString()
                    || (await gather.Act(previous, default))["outcome"]?.ToString() != "target_changed"))
                    throw new Exception("native reused drop slot inherited identity or accepted stale action");
                samples.Add(candidate.DeepClone()); previous = candidate;
                await OnMain(() => { Main.item[slot].active = false; return true; });
            }
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "drop-identity.json"), samples.ToJsonString());
            Console.WriteLine("DROP_IDENTITY_PASS native_spawns=3 reused_slot=true stale_action_rejected=true model_calls=0");
            return;

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_COMBAT_TRACE") == "1")
        {
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                for (int x = 95; x <= 115; x++)

                    || withWall["blocker"]?["x"]?.GetValue<int>() != 106 || withWall["blocker"]?["y"]?.GetValue<int>() != 100
                    || withoutPlatform["clear"]?.GetValue<bool>() != false)

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_INVENTORY_MANAGEMENT") == "1")
        {
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false;
                Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false;

                return true;
            });
            var overview = await Call("inventory_review", new JsonObject());
            JsonNode Inventory(int slot) => overview["inventory"].AsArray().First(i => i["slot"].GetValue<int>() == slot).DeepClone();
            foreach (var (inventory, equipment) in new[] { (0, 0), (1, 3) })
            {
                var equipped = await Call("equip_item", new JsonObject { ["inventory_slot"] = inventory, ["equipment_slot"] = equipment,
                    ["expected_inventory"] = Inventory(inventory), ["expected_equipment"] = overview["equipment"][equipment].DeepClone() });
                if (equipped["ok"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("native equipment rejected: " + equipped);
            }
            var dropArgs = new JsonObject { ["slot"] = 2, ["expected"] = Inventory(2) };
            var favorite = await Call("inventory_drop", dropArgs);
            if (favorite["error"]?.ToString() != "favorited_item") throw new Exception("native favorite was discarded");
            await OnMain(() => { Main.LocalPlayer.inventory[2].favorited = false; return true; });

            if (stale["error"]?.ToString() != "stale_inventory") throw new Exception("native stale inventory was discarded");
            dropArgs["expected"]["stack"] = 17;
