            {
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                int who = dropped["world_item"]?.GetValue<int>() ?? -1;
                return dropped["ok"]?.GetValue<bool>() == true && p.inventory[2].IsAir && who >= 0 && who < Main.maxItems
                    && Main.item[who].active && Main.item[who].type == ItemID.StoneBlock && Main.item[who].stack == 17
                    && p.armor[0].type == ItemID.WoodHelmet && p.statDefense > 0
                    && p.armor[3].type == ItemID.CloudinaBottle && p.GetJumpState(ExtraJump.CloudInABottle).Enabled
                    && Main.mouseItem.IsAir;
            });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "inventory-management.json"), new JsonObject
                { ["review"] = overview, ["favorite"] = favorite, ["stale"] = stale, ["drop"] = dropped, ["verified"] = verified }.ToJsonString());
            if (!verified) throw new Exception("native inventory/equipment effects unverified");
            Console.WriteLine("INVENTORY_MANAGEMENT_PASS native_equipment=true native_drop=true protected=true model_calls=0");
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_PROJECTILE_ONLY_DEFENSE") == "1")
        {
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                int cx = (int)(p.Center.X / 16), cy = (int)(p.Center.Y / 16);
                for (int x = cx - 12; x <= cx + 12; x++)
                    for (int y = cy - 12; y <= cy + 12; y++) Main.tile[x, y].ClearEverything();
                foreach (var npc in Main.npc) npc.active = false;
                float oldGravity = p.gravity;
                p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.gravity = 0;
                int shot = Projectile.NewProjectile(p.GetSource_Misc("projectile-only-defense-test"),
                    p.Center + new Vector2(0, -120), new Vector2(0, 5), ProjectileID.Stinger, 10, 0);
                Main.projectile[shot].hostile = true; Main.projectile[shot].friendly = false;
                var policy = (AgentControlPolicy)typeof(AgentTools).GetField("Control",
                    System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(null);
                string veto = null;
                int contact = -1;
                try
                {
                    FightCoordinator.Start(20);
                    FightCoordinator.Tick(p);
                    bool moved = p.controlLeft || p.controlRight;
                    if (!moved || p.controlUseItem) throw new Exception("native incoming projectile was ignored without an NPC target");
                    veto = policy.Deny(new[] { ControlBehavior.Dodge }, ControlLifetime.Task, null,
                        "native arena boundary", ((int)(p.position.X / 16), cy - 12, 20, 24), outside: true);
                    p.controlLeft = p.controlRight = p.controlJump = false;
                    FightCoordinator.Tick(p);
                    if (!p.controlRight || p.controlLeft)
                        throw new Exception("native projectile reflex crossed the confined left boundary");
                    policy.Release(veto); veto = null;
                    veto = policy.Deny(new[] { ControlBehavior.Dodge }, ControlLifetime.Task, null, "test movement veto");
                    p.controlLeft = p.controlRight = p.controlJump = false;
                    FightCoordinator.Tick(p);
                    if (p.controlLeft || p.controlRight || p.controlJump)
                        throw new Exception("native projectile defense overrode movement veto");
                    Console.WriteLine("PROJECTILE_ONLY_DEFENSE_PASS native_inputs=true no_npc=true veto_respected=true");
                    policy.Release(veto); veto = null;
                    Main.projectile[shot].active = false;
                    contact = NPC.NewNPC(p.GetSource_Misc("contact-defense-test"), (int)p.Center.X + 80, (int)p.Center.Y, NPCID.BlueSlime);
                    var enemy = Main.npc[contact];
                    enemy.Center = p.Center + new Vector2(80, 0); enemy.velocity = new Vector2(-5, 0);
                    p.controlLeft = p.controlRight = p.controlJump = false;
                    FightCoordinator.Tick(p);
                    var evidence = new JsonObject { ["npc_type"] = enemy.type, ["npc_velocity_x"] = enemy.velocity.X,
                        ["player_velocity_x"] = p.velocity.X, ["left"] = p.controlLeft,
                        ["right"] = p.controlRight, ["jump"] = p.controlJump, ["model_calls"] = 0 };
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "contact-defense.json"), evidence.ToJsonString());
                    if (!p.controlLeft && !p.controlRight && !p.controlJump)
                        throw new Exception("native approaching monster contact was ignored: " + evidence);
                    Console.WriteLine("CONTACT_DEFENSE_PASS native_inputs=true boundary_respected=true " + evidence);
                }
                finally
                {
                    if (veto != null) policy.Release(veto);
                    FightCoordinator.Stop(); Main.projectile[shot].active = false; p.gravity = oldGravity;
                    if (contact >= 0) Main.npc[contact].active = false;
                }
                return true;
            });
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_DATABASE_PROBE") == "1")
        {
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                var timer = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                var sources = new JsonArray();
                for (int type = 1; type < NPCID.Count; type++)
                {
                    var drops = new List<Terraria.GameContent.ItemDropRules.DropRateInfo>();
                    foreach (var rule in Main.ItemDropsDB.GetRulesForNPCID(type, true))
                        rule.ReportDroprates(drops, new Terraria.GameContent.ItemDropRules.DropRateInfoChainFeed(1f));
                    foreach (var drop in drops.Where(d => d.itemId == ItemID.Stinger))
                        sources.Add(new JsonObject { ["npc_type"] = type, ["rate"] = drop.dropRate,
                            ["min"] = drop.stackMin, ["max"] = drop.stackMax,
                            ["conditions"] = new JsonArray((drop.conditions ?? new()).Select(c => (JsonNode)JsonValue.Create(c.GetType().FullName)).ToArray()) });
                }
                double elapsed = timer.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds;
                if (!sources.Any(s => s["npc_type"].GetValue<int>() == NPCID.Hornet)
                    || sources.Any(s => s["npc_type"].GetValue<int>() == NPCID.ManEater))
                    throw new Exception("native drop reporting did not distinguish hornets from man eaters");
                var plants = new JsonArray();
                for (int x = 3340; x <= 3356; x++)
                    for (int y = 507; y <= 523; y++)
                    {
                        var tile = Main.tile[x, y];
                        if (!tile.HasTile || tile.TileType != TileID.JunglePlants) continue;
                        var map = Terraria.Map.MapHelper.CreateMapTile(x, y, 255);
                        plants.Add(new JsonObject { ["x"] = x, ["y"] = y, ["frame_x"] = tile.TileFrameX,
                            ["map_name"] = Lang.GetMapObjectName(map.Type),
                            ["object_style"] = Terraria.ObjectData.TileObjectData.GetTileStyle(tile),
                            ["spelunkable"] = Main.IsTileSpelunkable(x, y) });
                    }
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "source-database-probe.json"), new JsonObject
                { ["npc_sources"] = sources, ["npc_scan_ms"] = elapsed, ["plants"] = plants }.ToJsonString());

            });
            var queried = await Call("npc_drop_sources", new JsonObject { ["item_type"] = (int)ItemID.Stinger });
            if (queried["error"] != null || !queried["sources"].AsArray().Any(s => s["npc_type"].GetValue<int>() == NPCID.Hornet)
                || queried["sources"].AsArray().Any(s => s["npc_type"].GetValue<int>() == NPCID.ManEater)
                || queried["got"].AsObject().Count != 0 || queried["lost"].AsObject().Count != 0)
                throw new Exception("production drop source tool reported incorrect sources or mutated inventory");
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "npc-drop-sources-tool.json"), queried.ToJsonString());
            Console.WriteLine("NPC_DROP_SOURCES_TOOL_PASS real_rules=true inventory_unchanged=true");
            var observedPlants = await Call("find_tiles", new JsonObject { ["name"] = "JunglePlants", ["n"] = 12, ["max_dist"] = 2000 });
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                if (observedPlants["error"] != null || observedPlants["tiles"]?.AsArray().Count is not > 0)
                    throw new Exception("global plant observation found no native candidates");
                foreach (var cell in observedPlants["tiles"].AsArray())
                {
                    var tile = Main.tile[cell["x"].GetValue<int>(), cell["y"].GetValue<int>()];
                    if (cell["tile_type"]?.GetValue<int>() != tile.TileType
                        || cell["frame_x"]?.GetValue<int>() != tile.TileFrameX
                        || cell["frame_y"]?.GetValue<int>() != tile.TileFrameY)
                        throw new Exception("global plant observation lost native variant data");

            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "global-plant-variants.json"), observedPlants.ToJsonString());
            Console.WriteLine($"GLOBAL_PLANT_VARIANTS_PASS count={observedPlants["tiles"].AsArray().Count}");
            var filteredGlobal = await Call("find_tiles", new JsonObject { ["name"] = "JunglePlants", ["n"] = 1,
                ["max_dist"] = 2000, ["frame_x"] = 144, ["frame_y"] = 0 });
            if (filteredGlobal["tiles"]?.AsArray().Count != 1 || filteredGlobal["tiles"][0]["frame_x"].GetValue<int>() != 144
                || filteredGlobal["stale_candidates_removed"].GetValue<int>() != 0)
                throw new Exception("global native frame filter ran after nearest truncation: " + filteredGlobal);
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "global-frame-filter.json"), filteredGlobal.ToJsonString());
            Console.WriteLine("GLOBAL_FRAME_FILTER_PASS native_engine=true n=1 source_area=false");
            var luminousPlants = await Call("find_tiles", new JsonObject { ["name"] = "JunglePlants", ["n"] = 100,
                ["emits_light"] = true, ["source_area"] = new JsonObject
                { ["x"] = 3340, ["y"] = 507, ["width"] = 17, ["height"] = 17 } });

            if (glowingCells?.Count is not > 0 || glowingCells.Any(c => c["frame_x"].GetValue<int>() != 144
                || c["emission"]["g"].GetValue<float>() <= c["emission"]["r"].GetValue<float>()
                || c["emission"]["emits_light"].GetValue<bool>() != true)
                || luminousPlants["got"].AsObject().Count != 0 || luminousPlants["lost"].AsObject().Count != 0)
                throw new Exception("native emitted light did not distinguish green glowing plants without inventory changes: " + luminousPlants);
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "plant-emission.json"), luminousPlants.ToJsonString());
            Console.WriteLine($"PLANT_EMISSION_PASS native_engine=true glowing_plants={glowingCells.Count} model_calls=0");
            var wikiPosition = await OnMain(() =>
            {
                var previous = (Main.LocalPlayer.position, Main.LocalPlayer.velocity, Main.LocalPlayer.ghost, Main.LocalPlayer.fallStart);
                Main.LocalPlayer.ghost = true;
                Main.LocalPlayer.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                Main.LocalPlayer.position = new Vector2(3348 * 16, 515 * 16);
                return previous;
            });

                var sourcePage = await Call("wiki_page", new JsonObject { ["title"] = "Jungle Spores" });
                var source = sourcePage["nearby_listed_tiles"]?.AsArray().FirstOrDefault(t => t["wiki_tile_id"].GetValue<int>() == 61);
                if (source?["observed"]?["variants"]?.AsArray().Any(v => v["frame_x"].GetValue<int>() == 144
                    && v["nearest"]["emission"]["emits_light"].GetValue<bool>()) != true)
                    throw new Exception("wiki material page did not expose the native luminous source: " + sourcePage);
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "wiki-source-observation.json"), sourcePage.ToJsonString());
                Console.WriteLine("WIKI_SOURCE_OBSERVATION_PASS real_wiki=true native_tiles=true model_calls=0");
            }
            finally { await OnMain(() =>

                Main.LocalPlayer.position = wikiPosition.position;
                Main.LocalPlayer.velocity = wikiPosition.velocity;
                Main.LocalPlayer.ghost = wikiPosition.ghost;
                Main.LocalPlayer.fallStart = wikiPosition.fallStart;
                return true;

        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_STAND_BODY_CONTRACT") == "1")
        {
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                materialNavigationStub = true;
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                int x = Main.spawnTileX, y = Main.spawnTileY - 10;
                for (int tx = x - 3; tx <= x + 2; tx++)
                    for (int ty = y - 4; ty <= y + 1; ty++) Main.tile[tx, ty].ClearEverything();
                for (int ty = y - 3; ty <= y; ty++) Main.tile[x + 1, ty].ResetToType(TileID.Stone);
                RecedingNav.Start(x, y, RecedingNav.Mode.Stand);
                if (RecedingNav.LastStop != "done" || Collision.SolidCollision(p.position, p.width, p.height)
                    || (int)(p.Center.X / 16) != x || ActExecutor.OriginCy(p) != y)
                    throw new Exception("stand substitute failed to fit the body within the requested arrival cell");
                var position = p.position;
                for (int ty = y - 3; ty <= y; ty++) Main.tile[x, ty].ResetToType(TileID.Stone);
                RecedingNav.Start(x, y, RecedingNav.Mode.Stand);
                if (RecedingNav.LastStop != "test_stand_body_blocked" || RecedingNav.Active || p.position != position)
                    throw new Exception("stand substitute reported arrival or moved into a blocked body footprint");
                Console.WriteLine("STAND_BODY_CONTRACT_PASS adjusted_within_cell=true rejected_blocked=true");
                return true;
            });
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_AUTOMATIC_HEAL_EVIDENCE") == "1")
        {
            await OnMain(() => { NativeAgent.Stop(); return true; });
            var veto = await Call("control", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "deny", ["scope"] = "task",
                ["behaviors"] = new JsonArray("survival"), ["reason"] = "verify recovery ownership" });
            var before = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                p.statLife = 40; p.potionDelay = 0;
                p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.LesserHealingPotion); p.inventory[0].stack = 2;
                SurvivalReflex.Tick();
                if (p.statLife != 40 || p.inventory[0].stack != 2)
                    throw new Exception("survival reflex ignored the agent veto");
                return true;
            });
            var denied = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
            if (!JsonNode.DeepEquals(before["last_automatic_heal"], denied["last_automatic_heal"]))
                throw new Exception("denied healing changed the evidence");
            await Call("control", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "release", ["id"] = veto["id"].DeepClone() });
            var actual = await OnMain(() =>
            {
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                SurvivalReflex.Tick();
                if (p.statLife <= 40 || p.potionDelay <= 0 || p.inventory[0].stack != 1)
                    throw new Exception("automatic healing did not match actual engine health, cooldown and consumption");
                return p.statLife;
            });
            var healed = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
            var recorded = healed["last_automatic_heal"];
            if (recorded == null || recorded["hp_before"].GetValue<int>() != 40
                || recorded["hp_after"].GetValue<int>() != actual || recorded["potion_delay_after"].GetValue<int>() <= 0)
                throw new Exception("healing evidence did not match the engine");
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                p.statLife = 40;
                SurvivalReflex.Tick();

                    throw new Exception("cooldown call fabricated another heal or consumed a second potion");

            var cooldown = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
            if (!JsonNode.DeepEquals(recorded, cooldown["last_automatic_heal"]))
                throw new Exception("cooldown call replaced healing evidence");
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "automatic-heal-evidence.json"), recorded.ToJsonString());

        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_OPPORTUNITY_TRIAL") == "1")
        { await Opportunities(); return; }
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false; Manual.On = false;
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer; originX = (int)p.Center.X / 16; floor = (int)p.Bottom.Y / 16 + 3;
            if (Natural)
            {
                bool found = false;
                for (int y = (int)Main.rockLayer; y < Main.maxTilesY - 250 && !found; y += 3)
                for (int x = 100; x < Main.maxTilesX - 100 && !found; x += 3)
                {
                    if (!WorldGen.SolidTile(x, y) || Main.tile[x, y].TileType != TileID.JungleGrass) continue;
                    if (Collision.SolidCollision(new Vector2((x - 3) * 16, (y - 4) * 16), 8 * 16, 4 * 16)
                        || Collision.SolidCollision(new Vector2((x + 8) * 16, (y - 6) * 16), 40, 40)) continue;
                    if (Main.tile[x, y - 1].LiquidAmount > 0 || !WorldGen.SolidTile(x + 3, y)) continue;
                    originX = x; floor = y; found = true;
                }
                if (!found) throw new Exception("no suitable native jungle cave found; terrain was not altered");
                Console.WriteLine($"BEHAVIOR_NATURAL x={originX} y={floor} fixture_tile_edits=0");
            }
            else for (int x = originX - 30; x <= originX + 35; x++)
            {
                for (int y = floor - 15; y < floor; y++) Main.tile[x, y].ClearEverything();
                Main.tile[x, floor].ResetToType(TileID.Stone);
            }
            foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.ThrowingKnife); p.inventory[0].stack = 500;
            p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 15; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 400;

            p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)p.position.Y / 16;

        string code = """
            tool('watch',{events:['nearby_npc_killed','nearby_drop'],radius:40});
            const original = step('original',()=>tool('action_start',{tool:'nav_to',args:{x:GOAL_X,y:GOAL_Y}}));
            step('pause-original',()=>tool('action_control',{handle:original.handle,command:'pause'}));
            function chooseMovement() {
              const terrain=tool('inspect_terrain',{radius:12});
              const enemies=tool('npc_find',{type:NPC_TYPE});
              const answer=tool('jev_judge',{state:{terrain,enemies},questions:{movement:{type:'choice',
                instructions:'Choose movement for the next one second of throwing-knife combat. NPC cell and terrain origin use tile coordinates. Read player_bounds and rows. Do not walk off a ledge into a lower pocket, liquids, or a wall. Prefer auto when the current area permits attack or when terrain is uncertain. A horizontal move is useful only if it improves the firing position without falling into a worse pocket.',
                criteria:{auto:'Let the existing controller dodge threats and restore a blocked firing line.',left:'Move left while aiming and attacking for one second.',right:'Move right while aiming and attacking for one second.'}}}});
              if(answer.error) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Movement judgment failed: '+JSON.stringify(answer)});
              return answer.answers.movement.choice;
            }
            const fight = step('combat',()=>tool('action_start',{tool:'fight',args:{npc_types:[NPC_TYPE],max_dist:40,seconds:60}}));
            const initialMovement=chooseMovement();
            tool('action_control',{handle:fight.handle,command:'pause'});
            tool('action_control',{handle:fight.handle,command:'resume',args:{npc_types:[NPC_TYPE],max_dist:40,seconds:60,movement:initialMovement}});
            let death=false, previousLife=null, judgments=1, resetMovement=true;
            for(let i=0;i<30&&!death;i++) {
              const notice=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:1000});
              death=notice.events.some(e=>e.kind==='nearby_npc_killed');
              if(death) break;
              if(resetMovement) {
                tool('action_control',{handle:fight.handle,command:'pause'});
                tool('action_control',{handle:fight.handle,command:'resume',args:{npc_types:[NPC_TYPE],max_dist:40,seconds:60,movement:'auto'}});
                resetMovement=false;
              } else if(notice.paused_action) tool('action_control',{handle:notice.paused_action,command:'resume'});
              if(i%6===5) {
                const enemies=tool('npc_find',{type:NPC_TYPE}).npcs;
                const life=enemies.reduce((sum,n)=>sum+n.life,0);
                if(previousLife!==null&&life>=previousLife&&enemies.length) {
                  if(judgments>=3) tool('decision_point',{reason:'No combat progress after local adjustments; inspect terrain and preserved combat handle instead of waiting indefinitely.'});
                  const movement=chooseMovement(); judgments++;
                  tool('action_control',{handle:fight.handle,command:'pause'});
                  tool('action_control',{handle:fight.handle,command:'resume',args:{npc_types:[NPC_TYPE],max_dist:40,seconds:60,movement}});
                  resetMovement=true;
                }
                previousLife=life;
              }
            }
            if(!death) tool('decision_point',{reason:'No verified kill after bounded local combat; inspect the current target and terrain, then modify this program without restarting the original goal.'});
            if(fight) tool('action_control',{handle:fight.handle,command:'cancel'});
            let drops=[];
            for(let i=0;i<5&&!drops.length;i++) {
              drops=tool('nearby_drops',{radius:40}).drops.filter(d=>d.item_type!==279);
              if(!drops.length) tool('script_events',{wait_ms:250});
            }
            if(!drops.length) throw new Error('no visible native drop to judge');
            const terrain=tool('inspect_terrain',{radius:12});
            const decision=tool('jev_judge',{state:{goal:'Collect a useful dropped material or money then return to the original destination',drops,terrain},
              questions:{pickup:{type:'noul',instructions:'Is collecting one of these drops useful for the stated goal? This is usefulness, not a claim that the route is safe.'}}});
            if(decision.error) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(decision));
            if(decision.answers.pickup.noul<0.7) {tool('decision_point',{reason:'drop judgment needs review'});}
            const itemType=drops.find(d=>d.item_type===209||d.item_type===23)?.item_type??drops[0].item_type;
            const before=tool('get_state').inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===itemType).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0);
            let collected=tool('gather',{targets:[{item_type:itemType,count:before+1}]});
            for(let i=0;i<20&&collected.handle;i++) {
              const notice=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
              if([...(collected.events??[]),...notice.events].some(e=>['player_died','low_health','suffocating'].includes(e.kind)))
                tool('decision_point',{reason:'Danger while collecting; inspect current state before resuming the preserved gather handle.'});
              let status=tool('action_query',{handle:collected.handle,wait_ms:1000});
              if(status.status==='completed') {collected=status;break;}
              if(status.status==='paused') tool('action_control',{handle:collected.handle,command:'resume'});
              else if(status.status!=='running') throw new Error(JSON.stringify(status));
              collected={handle:collected.handle};
            }
            const after=tool('get_state').inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===itemType).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0);
            if(after<=before) throw new Error('drop not verified in inventory: '+JSON.stringify(collected));
            tool('action_control',{handle:original.handle,command:'resume'});
            let status;
            for(let i=0;i<25;i++) {
              status=tool('action_query',{handle:original.handle,wait_ms:1000});
              if(status.status!=='running') break;
            }
            if(status.status!=='completed') throw new Error('original goal not resumed: '+JSON.stringify(status));
            return {item_type:itemType,gained:after-before,original_handle:original.handle,judgment:decision,returned:true};
            """.Replace("GOAL_X", (originX + (Natural ? 3 : 18)).ToString()).Replace("GOAL_Y", (floor - 1).ToString())
                .Replace("NPC_TYPE", (Natural ? NPCID.Hornet : NPCID.BlueSlime).ToString());
        string reviewCode = code.Insert(code.IndexOf("function chooseMovement", StringComparison.Ordinal),
            "tool('decision_point',{reason:'Review local terrain before selecting combat movement; retain original action handle.'});\n");
        var saved = await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "save", ["name"] = "combat_loot_return", ["expected_revision"] = 0, ["code"] = reviewCode });
        var review = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["program"] = "combat_loot_return", ["expected_revision"] = saved["revision"].DeepClone(),
            ["workflow"] = "behavior-trial", ["event_delivery"] = "script" });
        if (review["value"]?["status"]?.ToString() != "yield" || review["value"]?["wake"] == null)
            throw new Exception("review did not yield: " + review.ToJsonString());
        string replay = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_BEHAVIOR_REPLAY");
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(replay))
        {
            var edited = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(replay));
            Console.WriteLine("BEHAVIOR_REPLAY source_sha256=" + Convert.ToHexString(System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(edited["code"].ToString()))));
            saved = await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "save", ["name"] = "combat_loot_return", ["expected_revision"] = saved["revision"].DeepClone(), ["code"] = edited["code"].DeepClone() });
        }
        else if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_BEHAVIOR_REVIEW") == "1") saved = await Review(review);
        else saved = await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "save", ["name"] = "combat_loot_return", ["expected_revision"] = saved["revision"].DeepClone(), ["code"] = code });
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            target = NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_Misc("BehaviorTrial"), (int)p.Center.X + (Natural ? 128 : 75),
                (int)p.Bottom.Y - (Natural ? 80 : 0), Natural ? NPCID.Hornet : NPCID.BlueSlime);
            if (!Natural) Main.npc[target].life = Main.npc[target].lifeMax = 120;
            return true;
        });
        var result = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["program"] = "combat_loot_return", ["expected_revision"] = saved["revision"].DeepClone(),
            ["workflow"] = "behavior-trial", ["event_delivery"] = "script" });
        if (result["error"] != null || result["value"]?["returned"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("behavior did not complete: " + result.ToJsonString());
        var finalState = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            if (Main.LocalPlayer.dead || movingAttackFrames == 0 || result["value"]?["gained"]?.GetValue<int>() <= 0)
                throw new Exception("native movement/combat/loot evidence missing");
            var originalActions = finalState["action_tasks"].AsArray().Where(a => a["tool"]?.ToString() == "nav_to").ToArray();
            if (originalActions.Length != 1 || originalActions[0]["handle"]?.ToString() != result["value"]?["original_handle"]?.ToString()
                || result["program"]?["revision"]?.GetValue<int>() < 2)
                throw new Exception("edited program replayed the original task or did not use revision 2");
            return true;
        });
    }

    async Task MiningSpeedTrial()
    {
        int cx = Main.spawnTileX + 30, ground = Main.spawnTileY + 5;
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); AgentTools.NewTask(); Manual.On = false; Concessions.Enabled = false;
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false; AgentPermissions.Attack = AgentPermissions.Dodge = false;
            for (int x = cx - 6; x <= cx + 8; x++)
            for (int y = ground - 8; y <= ground + 1; y++)
            {
                Main.tile[x, y].ClearEverything();
                if (y >= ground) Main.tile[x, y].ResetToType(TileID.Dirt);
            }
            var player = Main.LocalPlayer;
            foreach (var item in player.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            player.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe); player.selectedItem = 0;
            player.position = new Vector2(cx * 16, ground * 16 - player.height); player.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            player.fallStart = player.fallStart2 = (int)player.position.Y / 16;
            return true;
        });
        var samples = new JsonArray();
        foreach (bool fast in new[] { false, true })
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
        {
            int x = cx + 2, y = ground - 2;
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                Config.I.FastMining = fast;
                Main.LocalPlayer.itemAnimation = Main.LocalPlayer.itemTime = 0;
                Main.LocalPlayer.hitTile.ClearThisTile(x, y);
                Main.tile[x, y].ResetToType(TileID.Stone);
                miningMeasurement = new JsonObject { ["fast_mining"] = fast, ["sample"] = i,
                    ["x"] = x, ["y"] = y, ["pick_type"] = Main.LocalPlayer.inventory[0].type, ["tile_type"] = (int)TileID.Stone };
                return true;
            });
            var timer = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            var result = await Call("use_item", new JsonObject { ["slot"] = 0, ["x"] = x, ["y"] = y, ["strict"] = true });
            var sample = await OnMain(() => (JsonObject)miningMeasurement.DeepClone());
            sample["api_elapsed_ms"] = timer.ElapsedMilliseconds;
            if (result["outcome"]?.ToString() != "removed" || sample["mining_frames"] == null)
                throw new Exception("native mining speed sample failed: " + result + " " + sample);
            samples.Add(sample);
        }
        await OnMain(() => { miningMeasurement = null; Config.I.FastMining = true; return true; });
        var averages = new JsonObject();
        foreach (bool fast in new[] { false, true })
            averages[fast ? "on" : "off"] = samples.Where(sample => sample["fast_mining"].GetValue<bool>() == fast)
                .Average(sample => sample["mining_frames"].GetValue<long>());
        var report = new JsonObject { ["samples"] = samples, ["mean_mining_frames"] = averages, ["model_calls"] = 0,
            ["fixture_terrain"] = true, ["native_pick_tile"] = true };
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "mining-speed-native.json"), report.ToJsonString());
        if (averages["on"].GetValue<double>() >= averages["off"].GetValue<double>())
            throw new Exception("native mining speed did not improve: " + report);
        Console.WriteLine("MINING_SPEED_NATIVE_PASS " + averages.ToJsonString());
        int drop = await OnMain(() => Item.NewItem(new EntitySource_Misc("pickup-range-trial"), (cx + 6) * 16,
            (ground - 2) * 16, 16, 16, ItemID.Stinger));
        var pickupClock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        var pickup = await Call("gather", new JsonObject { ["targets"] = new JsonArray(new JsonObject { ["item_type"] = (int)ItemID.Stinger, ["count"] = 1 }) });
        var pickupReport = await OnMain(() => new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = pickupClock.ElapsedMilliseconds,
            ["result"] = pickup.DeepClone(), ["actual"] = Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Where(item => item.type == ItemID.Stinger).Sum(item => item.stack),
            ["drop_active"] = Main.item[drop].active, ["drop_type"] = Main.item[drop].type, ["model_calls"] = 0 });
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "drop-pickup-native.json"), pickupReport.ToJsonString());
        if (pickup["outcome"]?.ToString() != "enough" || pickupReport["actual"].GetValue<int>() != 1)
            throw new Exception("native pickup failed to close the remaining distance: " + pickupReport);
        Console.WriteLine("DROP_PICKUP_NATIVE_PASS " + pickupReport.ToJsonString());
    }

    async Task AtomicPolicyTrial()
    {
        int cx = Main.spawnTileX + 30, ground = Main.spawnTileY + 5;
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); AgentTools.NewTask();
            Manual.On = false; Concessions.Enabled = false;
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false; AgentPermissions.Attack = AgentPermissions.Dodge = false;
            for (int x = cx - 8; x <= cx + 80; x++)
            for (int y = ground - 8; y <= ground + 1; y++)
            {
                var tile = Main.tile[x, y]; tile.ClearEverything();
                if (y >= ground) { tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Dirt; }
            }
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            p.inventory[2].SetDefaults(ItemID.LifeCrystal); p.inventory[2].stack = 2;
            p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = 100; p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 0;
            p.position = new Vector2(cx * 16, ground * 16 - p.height); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.position.Y / 16);
            return true;
        });
        var full = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
        var fields = new JsonArray("player", "inventory_slots", "permanent_max_health");
        var focused = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject { ["fields"] = fields });
        var counts = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject { ["fields"] = new JsonArray("inventory_counts") });
        if (focused["snapshot_tick"] != null && counts["inventory_counts"]?[ItemID.LifeCrystal.ToString()]?.GetValue<int>() != 2)
            throw new Exception("native inventory count projection disagrees with the starting bag");
        string code = """
            let reaction=null;
            on('nearby_chest',e=>{
              const s=tool('get_state',{fields:['player','inventory_slots','permanent_max_health']});
              const observedTick=s.player.tick;
              const crystal=s.inventory_slots.find(i=>i.type===29&&i.stack>0);
              const receipt=tool('use_item',{slot:crystal.slot,x:Math.floor(s.player.pos.x/16),y:Math.floor(s.player.pos.y/16),duration_ticks:30});
              reaction={delay_ticks:observedTick-e.evidence.tick,observed_tick:observedTick,receipt};
              return {handled:true};
            });
            tool('watch',{events:['nearby_chest']});
            const movement=tool('act',{steps:[{keys:['right'],until:{frames:180}}]});
            const next=tool('act',{steps:[{keys:['left'],until:{frames:1}}]});
            return {movement,next,reaction,after:tool('get_state',{fields:['player','inventory_slots','permanent_max_health']})};
            """;
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        var execution = AgentTools.Execute("run_script", new JsonObject { ["code"] = code }, default);
        while (!await OnMain(() => ((AgentControlSession)SessionField.GetValue(null)).Tool == "act"))
        {
            if (execution.IsCompleted || clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds > 5) throw new Exception("native policy movement did not start");
            await Task.Delay(10);
        }
        int emitFrame = await OnMain(() => frames + 15);
        while (await OnMain(() => frames < emitFrame)) await Task.Delay(10);
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            var watches = (AgentWatch)typeof(AgentTools).GetField("Watches", HiddenStatic).GetValue(null);
            watches.Emit("nearby_chest", "native-atomic-policy", new JsonObject { ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount,
                ["x"] = cx + 5, ["y"] = ground - 2 });
            return true;
        });
        var result = JsonNode.Parse(await execution.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20))).AsObject();
        var after = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject { ["fields"] = new JsonArray("player", "inventory_slots", "permanent_max_health") });
        var report = new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["full_state_chars"] = full.ToJsonString().Length,
            ["focused_state_chars"] = focused.ToJsonString().Length, ["count_state_chars"] = counts.ToJsonString().Length, ["result"] = result,
            ["after"] = after, ["model_calls"] = 0, ["fixture_terrain"] = true, ["event_injected"] = true,
            ["assembly_mvid"] = typeof(AgentTools).Assembly.ManifestModule.ModuleVersionId.ToString() };
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "atomic-policy-native.json"), report.ToJsonString());
        if (result["ok"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true || result["value"]?["reaction"] == null
            || after["permanent_max_health"]?.GetValue<int>() != 120
            || after["inventory_slots"].AsArray().Where(i => i["type"]?.GetValue<int>() == ItemID.LifeCrystal).Sum(i => i["stack"].GetValue<int>()) != 1)
            throw new Exception("native atomic policy failed: " + report);
        Console.WriteLine("ATOMIC_POLICY_NATIVE_PASS " + report.ToJsonString());
    }

    async Task FallPlacementTrial()
    {
        int x = Main.spawnTileX, y = Main.spawnTileY + 7;
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; Config.I.AgentEnabled = true;
            Concessions.Enabled = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false;
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            foreach (var item in p.armor) item.TurnToAir();
            for (int tx = x - 3; tx <= x + 4; tx++)
            {
                for (int ty = y - 10; ty <= y + 3; ty++) Main.tile[tx, ty].ClearEverything();
                Main.tile[tx, y + 4].ResetToType(TileID.Stone);
            }
            Main.tile[x - 1, y].ResetToType(TileID.Stone);
            p.inventory[4].SetDefaults(ItemID.Starfury);
            p.inventory[5].SetDefaults(ItemID.WoodPlatform); p.inventory[5].stack = 20;
            p.position = new Vector2(x * 16 + 2, (y - 4) * 16 - p.height);
            p.velocity = new Vector2(0, .4f); p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.position.Y / 16);
            p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 100;
            p.selectedItem = 4; p.itemTime = p.itemAnimation = 5; p.controlUseItem = false; p.releaseUseItem = true;
            var replay = new List<PhysicsSimulator.ControlInput> { new() { Px = p.position.X, Py = p.position.Y,
                Vx = 0, Vy = .4f, Place = true, PlaceCx = x, PlaceCy = y } };
            typeof(StateSpacePlanner).GetField("_execFrames", HiddenStatic).SetValue(null, replay);
            typeof(StateSpacePlanner).GetField("_execIdx", HiddenStatic).SetValue(null, 0);
            return true;
        });
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        var samples = new JsonArray();
        while (clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < 8)
        {
            await Task.Delay(16);
            var sample = await OnMain(() =>
            {
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                return new JsonObject { ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount, ["y"] = p.position.Y, ["vy"] = p.velocity.Y,
                    ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["animation"] = p.itemAnimation, ["item_time"] = p.itemTime,
                    ["active"] = StateSpacePlanner.IsActive, ["platforms"] = p.inventory[5].stack,
                    ["placed"] = Main.tile[x, y].HasTile && Main.tile[x, y].TileType == TileID.Platforms,
                    ["inventory_open"] = Main.playerInventory };
            });
            samples.Add(sample);
            if (sample["placed"].GetValue<bool>() && sample["platforms"].GetValue<int>() == 19
                && sample["vy"].GetValue<float>() == 0 && !sample["active"].GetValue<bool>())
            {
                if (sample["hp"].GetValue<int>() != 100) throw new Exception("placement trial lost health");
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "fall-placement-live.json"), samples.ToJsonString());
                Console.WriteLine($"FALL_PLACEMENT_LIVE_PASS elapsed_ms={clock.ElapsedMilliseconds} real_item_use=true platforms_consumed=1 hp=100");
                return;
            }
        }
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "fall-placement-live.json"), samples.ToJsonString());
        throw new Exception("actual platform placement and landing did not complete within eight seconds");
    }

    async Task ConsumableHandoffTrial()
    {
        var results = new JsonArray();
        foreach (bool mining in new[] { false, true })
        {
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
                Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false;
                Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = Concessions.Enabled = false;
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                int cx = Main.spawnTileX, floor = Main.spawnTileY + 2;
                for (int x = cx - 12; x <= cx + 12; x++)
                for (int y = floor - 8; y <= floor + 2; y++)
                {
                    var tile = Main.tile[x, y]; tile.ClearEverything();
                    if (y >= floor) { tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Dirt; }
                }
                var stone = Main.tile[cx + 3, floor - 2]; stone.HasTile = true; stone.TileType = TileID.Stone;
                p.Teleport(new Vector2(cx * 16, floor * 16 - p.height)); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 0; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 100;
                p.itemAnimation = p.itemTime = 0; p.releaseUseItem = true;
                foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
                p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.LifeCrystal); p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe);
                p.selectedItem = 1; sourceInputSamples.Clear(); samplingSources = true;
                if (mining) ItemUseCoordinator.Start(new ItemUseRequest { Slot = 1, TargetWx = cx + 3, TargetWy = floor - 2, Strict = true });
                return true;
            });
            var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            if (mining)
                while (!await OnMain(() => Main.LocalPlayer.itemAnimation > 0))
                {
                    if (clock.ElapsedMilliseconds > 3000) throw new Exception("native mining animation never started");
                    await Task.Delay(5);
                }
            var animation = await OnMain(() =>
            { ItemUseCoordinator.Stop(); return Main.LocalPlayer.itemAnimation; });
            var used = await Call("use_item", new JsonObject { ["slot"] = 0,
                ["x"] = Main.spawnTileX, ["y"] = Main.spawnTileY, ["duration_ticks"] = 30 });
            var state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
            await OnMain(() => { samplingSources = false; return true; });
            File.WriteAllLines(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "consumable-handoff-" + mining + ".csv"),
                new[] { "tick,active,outcome,x,y,has_tile,use,animation,item_time,item_type" }.Concat(sourceInputSamples));
            results.Add(new JsonObject { ["mining_before"] = mining, ["animation_before"] = animation,
                ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["result"] = used.DeepClone(),
                ["health_after"] = state["permanent_max_health"].DeepClone() });
        }
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "consumable-handoff.json"), results.ToJsonString());
        if (results.Any(r => r["health_after"].GetValue<int>() != 120 || r["result"]["verified"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true))
            throw new Exception("consumable handoff failed: " + results);
        Console.WriteLine("CONSUMABLE_HANDOFF_PASS idle=true mining_animation=true duration_ticks=30 actual_health=120 model_calls=0");
    }

    async Task DesertContactTrial()
    {
        var before = await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
            Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false;
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = Concessions.Enabled = false;
            VanillaNavBridge.Enabled = VanillaNavBridge.RegionsEnabled = false;
            Main.dontStarveWorld = false;
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            p.Teleport(new Vector2(35629.824f, 4852.176f)); p.velocity = new Vector2(2.8f, -2.8f);
            p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 0; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 100;
            p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.Bottom.Y / 16);
            foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            foreach (var item in p.armor) item.TurnToAir();
            p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe);
            p.inventory[2].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperAxe);
            p.inventory[3].SetDefaults(ItemID.Wood); p.inventory[3].stack = 9999;
            p.selectedItem = 1;
            if (!Main.tile[2234, 305].HasTile || Main.tile[2234, 305].TileType != TileID.Cactus)
                throw new Exception("natural desert fixture cactus is missing");
            int Damage() => TerrainInspection.Read(12)["contact_damage_tiles"].AsArray()
                .Where(t => t["type"].GetValue<ushort>() == TileID.Cactus).Select(t => t["damage"].GetValue<int>()).FirstOrDefault();
            int classicDamage = Damage();
            var predicted = (Collision.HurtTile)typeof(AgentContactEscape).GetMethod("PredictedContact", HiddenStatic)
                .Invoke(null, new object[] { p });
            Main.dontStarveWorld = true;
            int specialDamage = Damage(); Main.dontStarveWorld = false;
            var facts = new JsonObject { ["classic_cactus_damage"] = classicDamage,
                ["special_cactus_damage"] = specialDamage, ["predicted_contact_type"] = predicted.type };
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "desert-contact-before.json"), facts.ToJsonString());
            if (classicDamage != 0 || specialDamage <= 0 || predicted.type >= 0)
                throw new Exception("passable desert terrain was classified as contact damage: " + facts);
            var tiles = new JsonArray();
            for (int x = 2220; x <= 2265; x++)
            for (int y = 295; y <= 320; y++)
                if (Main.tile[x, y].HasTile && Main.tile[x, y].TileType is TileID.Sand or TileID.Cactus)
                    tiles.Add(new JsonObject { ["x"] = x, ["y"] = y, ["type"] = (int)Main.tile[x, y].TileType });
            p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            evasionFrames.Clear(); samplingEvasion = true;
            return tiles;
        });
        await Call("watch", new JsonObject { ["events"] = new JsonArray("suffocating", "low_health") });
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        var result = await Call("nav_to", new JsonObject { ["x"] = 2256, ["y"] = 310 });
        var route = await OnMain(() =>
        {
            samplingEvasion = false;
            int changed = before.AsArray().Count(t => !Main.tile[t["x"].GetValue<int>(), t["y"].GetValue<int>()].HasTile
                || Main.tile[t["x"].GetValue<int>(), t["y"].GetValue<int>()].TileType != t["type"].GetValue<int>());
            var facts = new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["result"] = result.DeepClone(),
                ["sand_or_cactus_removed"] = changed, ["escape"] = EscapeField.GetValue(null) is true,
                ["frames"] = evasionFrames.DeepClone(), ["model_calls"] = 0 };
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "desert-contact-route.json"), facts.ToJsonString());
            Halt.All(); return facts;
        });
        if (result["done"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true || route["sand_or_cactus_removed"].GetValue<int>() != 0)
            throw new Exception("desert crossing required clearing passable terrain: " + result);
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            int cx = (int)(p.Center.X / 16), cy = (int)(p.Center.Y / 16);
            for (int x = cx - 1; x <= cx + 1; x++)
            for (int y = cy - 2; y <= cy + 2; y++)
            { var tile = Main.tile[x, y]; tile.ClearEverything(); tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Sand; }
            p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            return true;
        });
        await Task.Delay(400);
        var buried = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
        if (buried["player"]["suffocating"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("native burial did not trigger suffocation");
        var stopped = await Call("nav_to", new JsonObject { ["x"] = 2260, ["y"] = 310 });
        if (stopped["error"]?.ToString() != "navigation_suffocating") throw new Exception("burial guard was lost: " + stopped);
        Console.WriteLine("DESERT_CONTACT_PASS classic_cactus_damage=0 special_cactus_damage=true actual_arrival=true sand_or_cactus_removed=0 burial_guard=true model_calls=0");
    }

    async Task NativeRejectionNaturalTrial()
    {
        var start = new Vector2(22354.605f, 9062f);
        var warming = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        while (!await OnMain(() => (bool)typeof(VanillaNavigationDemo).GetProperty("WorkerReady", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic)
            .GetValue(VanillaNavigationDemo.Instance)))
        {
            if (warming.ElapsedMilliseconds > 30000) throw new Exception("native worker did not warm before route replay");
            await Task.Delay(100);
        }
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
            Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false;
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = Concessions.Enabled = false;
            VanillaNavBridge.Enabled = VanillaNavBridge.RegionsEnabled = false;
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            if (Collision.SolidCollision(start, p.width, p.height))
                throw new Exception("recorded starting body is blocked in this world copy");
            p.Teleport(start); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.Bottom.Y / 16);
            p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 4; p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 180; p.statLife = 112;
            p.itemTime = p.itemAnimation = 0; p.releaseJump = true;
            foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            foreach (var item in p.armor) item.TurnToAir();
            p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe);
            p.inventory[2].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperAxe);
            p.inventory[3].SetDefaults(ItemID.Wood); p.inventory[3].stack = 9999;
            p.inventory[4].SetDefaults(ItemID.Starfury);
            p.inventory[5].SetDefaults(ItemID.WoodPlatform); p.inventory[5].stack = 100;
            p.selectedItem = 1;
            p.AddBuff(BuffID.Gills, 36000); p.AddBuff(BuffID.Shine, 36000);
            evasionFrames.Clear(); samplingEvasion = true;
            return true;
        });
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        JsonNode result;
        using var deadline = new CancellationTokenSource(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(45));
        try { result = JsonNode.Parse(await AgentTools.Execute("nav_to", new JsonObject { ["x"] = 1418, ["y"] = 638 }, deadline.Token)); }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) { result = new JsonObject { ["error"] = "probe_deadline" }; }
        var after = await OnMain(() =>
        {
            samplingEvasion = false;
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            var state = new JsonObject { ["x"] = p.position.X, ["y"] = p.position.Y, ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["dead"] = p.dead };
            Halt.All(); return state;
        });
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "native-rejection-natural.json"), new JsonObject {
            ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["start_x"] = start.X, ["start_y"] = start.Y,
            ["goal_x"] = 1418, ["goal_y"] = 638, ["terrain_edits_before_start"] = 0,
            ["model_calls"] = 0, ["result"] = result, ["after"] = after, ["frames"] = evasionFrames.DeepClone() }.ToJsonString());
        if (result?["done"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true)
            throw new Exception("native natural route did not arrive: " + result);
        Console.WriteLine("NATIVE_REJECTION_NATURAL_PASS actual_arrival=true terrain_edits_before_start=0 model_calls=0 elapsed_ms=" + clock.ElapsedMilliseconds);
    }

    async Task NativeRejectionTrial()
    {
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Manual.On = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
            Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false;
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = Concessions.Enabled = false;
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            int cx = Main.spawnTileX, ground = Main.spawnTileY + 2;
            for (int x = cx - 40; x <= cx + 40; x++)
            for (int y = ground - 12; y <= ground + 3; y++)
            {
                var tile = Main.tile[x, y]; tile.ClearEverything();
                if (y >= ground) { tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Dirt; }
            }
            p.Teleport(new Vector2(cx * 16, ground * 16 - p.height)); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = ground; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 100;
            p.itemTime = p.itemAnimation = 0; p.releaseJump = true;
            foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            var native = typeof(RecedingNav).Assembly.GetType("TerraBlind.LegacyNativePrediction", true);
            var from = ((int, int))typeof(StateSpacePlanner).GetMethod("StandCell", HiddenStatic)
                .Invoke(null, new object[] { p.position.X, p.position.Y });
            void Set(Type type, string name, object value) => type.GetField(name, HiddenStatic).SetValue(null, value);
            object Get(string name) => typeof(RecedingNav).GetField(name, HiddenStatic).GetValue(null);
            var field = new Dictionary<(int, int), int>();
            for (int dx = -30; dx <= 30; dx++) field[(from.Item1 + dx, from.Item2)] = 100 - dx;
            Set(typeof(MazeWand), "_cachedField", field);
            Set(typeof(MazeWand), "_cachedGoal", (from.Item1 + 20, from.Item2));
            Set(typeof(RecedingNav), "_goalWx", from.Item1 + 20); Set(typeof(RecedingNav), "_goalWy", from.Item2);
            Set(typeof(RecedingNav), "_fieldReady", true);
            VanillaNavBridge.Enabled = VanillaNavBridge.RegionsEnabled = false;
            StateSpacePlanner.SSResult Inject(bool rejected)
            {
                var anchor = VanillaPrediction.Observe(p, false);
                var sample = anchor.DeepClone().AsObject();
                if (rejected) sample["hp"] = p.statLife - 1;
                var candidate = new StateSpacePlanner.SSResult { Found = true, StartPx = p.position.X,
                    StartPy = p.position.Y, GoalWx = from.Item1, GoalWy = from.Item2, CurH = 100 };
                candidate.Steps.Add(new StateSpacePlanner.ExecStep { Frames = new() {
                    new PhysicsSimulator.ControlInput { Px = p.position.X, Py = p.position.Y } } });
                Set(native, "pending", Task.FromResult(new JsonObject { ["outcome"] = "complete", ["frames"] = new JsonArray(sample) }));
                Set(native, "cancellation", new CancellationTokenSource()); Set(native, "plan", candidate);
                Set(native, "snapshot", VanillaFrame.Capture(p, new Rectangle(cx - 30, ground - 15, 61, 20), true));
                Set(native, "anchor", anchor); Set(native, "requestedAt", Stopwatch.GetTimestamp());
                return candidate;
            }
            var valid = Inject(false);
            object[] poll = { p, null };
            if (!(bool)native.GetMethod("Poll", HiddenStatic).Invoke(null, poll) || !ReferenceEquals(poll[1], valid)
                || !valid.NativeVerified) throw new Exception("valid native prediction was rejected");
            RecedingNav.Active = true;
            Set(typeof(RecedingNav), "_noProgressSteps", 0);
            Set(typeof(RecedingNav), "_failureRecoveryTried", false);
            Set(typeof(RecedingNav), "_failureRecoveryPending", false);
            typeof(RecedingNav).GetMethod("BeginAttempt", HiddenStatic).Invoke(null, new object[] { p });
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { Inject(true); RecedingNav.Tick(); }
            if (!(bool)Get("_failureRecoveryTried"))
                throw new Exception("four rejected forecasts did not enter no-progress recovery: " + Get("_noProgressSteps"));
            Console.WriteLine("NATIVE_REJECTION_RECOVERY_PASS attempts=" + Get("_noProgressSteps") + " hp=" + p.statLife);
            Halt.All();
            return true;
        });
        int goalX = Main.spawnTileX + 12, goalY = Main.spawnTileY + 1;
        var moved = await Call("nav_to", new JsonObject { ["x"] = goalX, ["y"] = goalY });
        if (moved["done"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("normal flat navigation regressed: " + moved);
        Console.WriteLine("NATIVE_REJECTION_PASS valid_forecast=true damage_rejected=true recovery_after=4 actual_flat_arrival=true model_calls=0");
    }

    async Task ReplyCoverTrial()
    {
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false;
            Concessions.Enabled = false; Manual.On = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false;
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            int cx = Main.spawnTileX, ground = Main.spawnTileY + 2;
            for (int x = cx - 90; x <= cx + 90; x++)
                for (int y = ground - 12; y <= ground + 3; y++)
                {
                    var tile = Main.tile[x, y];
                    tile.ClearEverything();
                    if (y >= ground) { tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Dirt; }
                }
            p.Teleport(new Vector2(cx * 16, ground * 16 - p.height));
            p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = ground;
            foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 100;
            evasionFrames.Clear(); handoffProbeTick = samplingEvasion = true;
            return true;
        });
        string first = "step('first',()=>{const r=tool('act',{steps:[{keys:['right'],until:{frames:180}}]});return {outcome:r.outcome};});";
        string second = "step('second',()=>{const r=tool('act',{steps:[{keys:['left'],until:{frames:120}}]});return {outcome:r.outcome};});";
        string third = "step('third',()=>{const r=tool('act',{steps:[{keys:['right'],until:{frames:90}}]});return {outcome:r.outcome};});";
        await Call("task_plan", new JsonObject { ["goal"] = "cover delayed successor preparation", ["mode"] = "dag",
            ["steps"] = new JsonArray(new JsonObject { ["id"] = "route", ["description"] = "native movement segments",
                ["depends_on"] = new JsonArray(), ["script"] = first + "return {status:'yield'};",
                ["conditions"] = new JsonArray(new JsonObject { ["kind"] = "item", ["type"] = (int)ItemID.Wood, ["count"] = 9999 }) }) });
        string workflow = (await Call("get_state", new JsonObject()))["task_plan"]["steps"][0]["workflow"].ToString();
        await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["task_node"] = "route", ["background"] = true });
        await Task.Delay(1000);
        async Task Patch(string source)
        {
            var state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
            var result = await Call("task_plan_patch", new JsonObject { ["expected_revision"] = state["task_plan"]["revision"].DeepClone(),
                ["reason"] = "simulated delayed reasoning publishes executable successor", ["nodes"] = new JsonArray(new JsonObject {
                    ["id"] = "route", ["script"] = source + "return {status:'yield'};" }) });
            if (result["error"] != null) throw new Exception("reply cover patch failed: " + result);
        }
        await Patch(first + second);
        var joined = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["task_node"] = "route", ["background"] = true });
        if (joined["already_running"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true)
            throw new Exception("duplicate publication did not join the live native script: " + joined);
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        JsonObject state;
        do
        {
            await Task.Delay(50);
            state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
        } while ((state["script_workflows"][workflow]["steps"].AsArray().Count(s => s["status"]?.ToString() == "completed") < 2
            || state["script_workflows"][workflow]["running"]?.GetValue<bool>() == true) && clock.ElapsedMilliseconds < 12000);
        if (clock.ElapsedMilliseconds >= 12000) throw new Exception("native prepared segment did not continue: " + state["script_workflows"][workflow]);
        await Task.Delay(600);
        await OnMain(() => { typeof(AgentTools).GetMethod("ObserveContinuity", HiddenStatic).Invoke(null, null); return true; });
        double published = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() * 1000.0 / Stopwatch.Frequency;
        await Patch(first + second + third);
        clock.Restart();
        do
        {
            await Task.Delay(25);
            state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
        } while (state["script_workflows"][workflow]["steps"].AsArray().Count(s => s["status"]?.ToString() == "completed") < 3
            && clock.ElapsedMilliseconds < 6000);
        await OnMain(() => { handoffProbeTick = samplingEvasion = false; return true; });
        var directions = evasionFrames.Select(frame => frame["right"].GetValue<bool>() ? 1 : frame["left"].GetValue<bool>() ? -1 : 0).ToArray();
        int segments = 0, previous = 0;
        foreach (int direction in directions)
            if (direction != 0 && direction != previous) { segments++; previous = direction; }
        var next = evasionFrames.FirstOrDefault(frame => frame["time_ms"].GetValue<double>() >= published && frame["right"].GetValue<bool>());
        double startDelay = next == null ? double.PositiveInfinity : next["time_ms"].GetValue<double>() - published;
        var evidence = new JsonObject { ["native_direction_segments"] = segments, ["late_publish_to_input_ms"] = startDelay,
            ["model_calls"] = 0, ["simulated_preparation_delay_ms"] = 1000, ["intentional_uncovered_delay_ms"] = 600,
            ["workflow"] = state["script_workflows"][workflow].DeepClone(), ["frames"] = evasionFrames.DeepClone() };
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "reply-cover.json"), evidence.ToJsonString());
        if (segments != 3 || startDelay > 300 || state["script_workflows"][workflow]["steps"].AsArray()
            .Count(s => s["status"]?.ToString() == "completed") != 3)
            throw new Exception("native reply coverage did not continue after publication: " + evidence);
        Console.WriteLine("REPLY_COVER_PASS native_engine=true model_calls=0 segments=3 late_publish_to_input_ms=" + startDelay);
    }

    async Task PreparedHandoffTrial()
    {
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false;
            Concessions.Enabled = false; Manual.On = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false;
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            p.inventory[2].SetDefaults(ItemID.Wood); p.inventory[2].stack = 1;
            return true;
        });
        var nodes = new JsonArray();
        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
            nodes.Add(new JsonObject { ["id"] = "leg" + i, ["description"] = "measure native input dispatch " + i,
                ["depends_on"] = i == 0 ? new JsonArray() : new JsonArray("leg" + (i - 1)),
                ["script"] = "return tool('act',{steps:[{keys:['" + (i % 2 == 0 ? "right" : "left") + "'],until:{frames:12}}]});",
                ["conditions"] = new JsonArray(new JsonObject { ["kind"] = "item", ["type"] = (int)ItemID.Wood, ["count"] = 1 }) });
        await Call("task_plan", new JsonObject { ["goal"] = "measure already prepared action handoffs", ["mode"] = "dag", ["steps"] = nodes });
        await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["task_node"] = "leg0", ["background"] = true });
        await OnMain(() => { handoffProbeTick = samplingEvasion = true; return true; });
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        JsonObject state;
        do
        {
            await Task.Delay(50);
            state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
        } while (state["task_plan"]["status"]?.ToString() != "completed" && clock.ElapsedMilliseconds < 10000);
        await OnMain(() => { handoffProbeTick = samplingEvasion = false; return true; });
        var handoffs = new JsonArray();
        const string marker = "[agent_input_handoff] ";
        foreach (string line in Directory.EnumerateFiles(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "TerraBlindLogs"), "*.log", SearchOption.AllDirectories).SelectMany(File.ReadLines))
            if (line.Contains(marker)) handoffs.Add(JsonNode.Parse(line[(line.IndexOf(marker) + marker.Length)..]));
        double? released = null;
        double neutralMax = 0;
        int lastDirection = 0, switches = 0;
        foreach (var frame in evasionFrames)
        {
            int direction = frame["right"].GetValue<bool>() ? 1 : frame["left"].GetValue<bool>() ? -1 : 0;
            double time = frame["time_ms"].GetValue<double>();
            if (direction == 0) { if (switches > 0) released ??= time; continue; }
            if (released != null) { neutralMax = Math.Max(neutralMax, time - released.Value); released = null; }
            if (direction != lastDirection) { switches++; lastDirection = direction; }
        }
        var evidence = new JsonObject { ["handoffs"] = handoffs, ["native_neutral_max_ms"] = neutralMax,
            ["native_direction_segments"] = switches, ["frames"] = evasionFrames.DeepClone(), ["plan"] = state["task_plan"].DeepClone() };
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "prepared-handoffs.json"), evidence.ToJsonString());
        if (handoffs.Count != 5 || switches != 6 || neutralMax > 100
            || handoffs.Any(h => h["elapsed_ms"].GetValue<double>() > 100) || state["task_plan"]["status"]?.ToString() != "completed")
            throw new Exception("prepared handoff exceeded 100ms or skipped actual input: " + evidence);
        Console.WriteLine("PREPARED_HANDOFF_PASS native_engine=true model_calls=0 transitions=5 native_neutral_max_ms=" + neutralMax);
    }

    async Task DungeonGoalTrial()
    {
        var locked = new Dictionary<(int X, int Y), Dictionary<int, int>>();
        var initialInventory = new Dictionary<int, int>();
        const string prompt = "/goal 探索当前已击败骷髅王的地牢，自行获得金钥匙，打开一个此前未开启的上锁金箱，并实际拾取战利品。装备和施工材料已经备好。遇到尖刺、接触后碎裂的砖和无法伤害的怪物时，结合实际地形选择绕行、施工或其他可执行办法。受阻后保留已完成进度调整方案继续。用DAG和脚本执行并核验开锁与战利品入包，超过10秒的停顿记录原因并恢复行动。将接近、取物或局部恢复等可复用行为保存到script_program，并用skill(name,revision,input,key)在DAG脚本中实际调用。已有适用技能优先复用，参数通过当前世界的观察取得。技能效果由实际游戏状态核验。";
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false;
            Manual.On = false; Concessions.Enabled = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false; materialNavigationStub = false;
            NPC.downedBoss3 = true;
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            foreach (var item in p.armor) item.TurnToAir();
            var gear = new[] { (ItemID.Starfury, 1), (ItemID.GoldPickaxe, 1), (ItemID.Wood, 999),
                (ItemID.WoodPlatform, 200), (ItemID.Cobweb, 100), (ItemID.LesserHealingPotion, 10), (ItemID.Torch, 99) };
            for (int i = 0; i < gear.Length; i++) { p.inventory[i].SetDefaults(gear[i].Item1); p.inventory[i].stack = gear[i].Item2; }
            var armor = new[] { ItemID.IronHelmet, ItemID.IronChainmail, ItemID.IronGreaves,
                ItemID.CloudinaBottle, ItemID.HermesBoots };
            for (int i = 0; i < armor.Length; i++) p.armor[i].SetDefaults(armor[i]);
            p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 5; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 200;
            var entries = new List<Vector2>();
            for (int x = Math.Max(10, Main.dungeonX - 30); x <= Math.Min(Main.maxTilesX - 10, Main.dungeonX + 30); x++)
            for (int y = Math.Max(10, Main.dungeonY - 30); y <= Math.Min(Main.maxTilesY - 10, Main.dungeonY + 10); y++)
            {
                var position = new Vector2(x * 16, y * 16 - p.height);
                var support = Main.tile[x, y];
                if (!support.HasUnactuatedTile || !Main.tileSolid[support.TileType] || Main.tileSolidTop[support.TileType]
                    || TileID.Sets.CrackedBricks[support.TileType] || TileID.Sets.TouchDamageImmediate[support.TileType] > 0
                    || Collision.SolidCollision(position, p.width, p.height)) continue;
                entries.Add(position);
            }
            if (!entries.Any()) throw new Exception("natural dungeon entrance has no supported starting position");
            p.Teleport(entries.OrderBy(v => Vector2.DistanceSquared(v, new Vector2(Main.dungeonX * 16, Main.dungeonY * 16))).First());
            p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)p.position.Y / 16;
            foreach (var ch in Main.chest.Where(c => c != null && Chest.IsLocked(c.x, c.y)
                && Main.tile[c.x, c.y].TileType == TileID.Containers && Main.tile[c.x, c.y].TileFrameX / 36 == 2))
                locked[(ch.x, ch.y)] = ch.item.Where(i => !i.IsAir).GroupBy(i => i.type).ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.Sum(i => i.stack));
            foreach (var group in p.inventory.Where(i => !i.IsAir).GroupBy(i => i.type)) initialInventory[group.Key] = group.Sum(i => i.stack);
            if (initialInventory.GetValueOrDefault(ItemID.GoldenKey) != 0 || locked.Count == 0) throw new Exception("invalid natural dungeon fixture");
            Config.I.AgentUseCodex = true; Config.I.AgentCodexModel = "gpt-6.1-sol";
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "dungeon-start.json"), new JsonObject {
                ["prompt"] = prompt, ["world_seed"] = Main.ActiveWorldFileData.SeedText, ["classic"] = Main.GameMode == 0,
                ["skeletron_defeated"] = NPC.downedBoss3, ["x"] = p.Center.X / 16, ["y"] = p.Center.Y / 16,
                ["golden_keys"] = 0, ["natural_locked_gold_chests"] = locked.Count, ["navigation_stub"] = UsingNavigationStub,
                ["starting_health"] = p.statLife, ["inventory"] = new JsonArray(gear.Select(g => (JsonNode)new JsonObject { ["type"] = (int)g.Item1, ["count"] = g.Item2 }).ToArray()),
                ["armor"] = new JsonArray(armor.Select(type => (JsonNode)JsonValue.Create((int)type)).ToArray()),
                ["natural_encounters"] = NaturalEncounters, ["terrain_edits"] = 0, ["model"] = "gpt-6.1-sol",
                ["package_sha256"] = Convert.ToHexString(System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256.HashData(
                    File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "Mods", "TerraBlind.tmod")))) }.ToJsonString());
            dungeonPreviousPosition = p.position; dungeonPreviousHp = p.statLife;
            dungeonClock = Stopwatch.StartNew(); dungeonSampling = true; NativeAgent.Submit(prompt);
            return true;
        });
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        Vector2 anchor = await OnMain(() => Main.LocalPlayer.position);
        long stationarySince = 0; bool moved = false, warned = false, wasDead = false; int deaths = 0;
        using var journal = new StreamWriter(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "dungeon-samples.jsonl")) { AutoFlush = true };
        while (true)
        {
            await Task.Delay(1000);
            var state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
            var observation = await OnMain(() =>
            {
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer; long now = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
                if (p.dead && !wasDead) deaths++; wasDead = p.dead;
                if (Vector2.DistanceSquared(p.position, anchor) >= 16)
                {
                    if (warned)
                    {
                        Console.WriteLine($"DUNGEON_RESUMED elapsed_ms={now} stationary_ms={now - stationarySince}");
                        dungeonStops.Add(new JsonObject { ["started_ms"] = stationarySince, ["ended_ms"] = now,
                            ["duration_ms"] = now - stationarySince, ["resumed_status"] = NativeAgent.Status });
                    }
                    anchor = p.position; stationarySince = now; moved = true; warned = false;
                }
                if (moved && !warned && now - stationarySince >= 10000)
                {
                    warned = true;
                    Console.WriteLine($"DUNGEON_STATIONARY elapsed_ms={now} duration_ms={now - stationarySince} pos={p.Center / 16} status={NativeAgent.Status} nav={RecedingNav.Active} nav_reason={RecedingNav.LastStop} item_use={ItemUseCoordinator.IsActive}");
                }
                var completed = locked.Where(kv => !Chest.IsLocked(kv.Key.X, kv.Key.Y)).FirstOrDefault(kv =>
                {
                    var ch = Main.chest.FirstOrDefault(c => c != null && c.x == kv.Key.X && c.y == kv.Key.Y);
                    return ch != null && kv.Value.Any(item => ch.item.Where(i => i.type == item.Key).Sum(i => i.stack) < item.Value
                        && p.inventory.Where(i => i.type == item.Key).Sum(i => i.stack) > initialInventory.GetValueOrDefault(item.Key));
                });
                string unlockEvidence = null;
                bool keyConsumed = false;
                if (completed.Value != null)
                {
                    string prefix = $"[chest_unlock] ({completed.Key.X},{completed.Key.Y}) native_tile_interaction=true locked_after=False consumed=";
                    unlockEvidence = Directory.EnumerateFiles(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "TerraBlindLogs"), "*.log", SearchOption.AllDirectories)
                        .SelectMany(File.ReadLines).LastOrDefault(line => line.Contains(prefix));
                    if (unlockEvidence != null)
                    {
                        var consumed = JsonNode.Parse(unlockEvidence[(unlockEvidence.IndexOf(prefix) + prefix.Length)..]).AsObject();
                        keyConsumed = consumed[ItemID.GoldenKey.ToString()]?.GetValue<int>() == 1;
                    }
                }
                return new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = now, ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["dead"] = p.dead, ["deaths"] = deaths,
                    ["x"] = p.Center.X / 16, ["y"] = p.Center.Y / 16, ["in_dungeon"] = p.ZoneDungeon,
                    ["key_observed"] = dungeonKeyObserved, ["max_keys"] = dungeonMaxKeys,
                    ["verified_loot"] = dungeonKeyObserved && completed.Value != null && keyConsumed,
                    ["golden_key_consumed"] = keyConsumed, ["unlock_evidence"] = unlockEvidence,
                    ["loot_x"] = completed.Value == null ? null : JsonValue.Create(completed.Key.X),
                    ["loot_y"] = completed.Value == null ? null : JsonValue.Create(completed.Key.Y),
                    ["terrain"] = TerrainInspection.Read(12), ["status"] = NativeAgent.Status,
                    ["time_ms"] = new JsonObject(dungeonTimes.Select(kv => new KeyValuePair<string, JsonNode>(kv.Key, JsonValue.Create(kv.Value)))),
                    ["stationary_ms"] = dungeonStationary.Values.Sum(),
                    ["stationary_by_phase_ms"] = new JsonObject(dungeonStationary.Select(kv => new KeyValuePair<string, JsonNode>(kv.Key, JsonValue.Create(kv.Value)))),
                    ["damage_count"] = dungeonDamageCount, ["damage_total"] = dungeonDamageTotal,
                    ["hp_lost_measured"] = dungeonHpLost, ["hp_recovered_measured"] = dungeonHpRecovered,
                    ["respawn_hp_restored"] = dungeonRespawnHp,
                    ["stationary_segments"] = dungeonStops.DeepClone() };
            });
            observation["state"] = state;
            journal.WriteLine(observation.ToJsonString());
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "dungeon-latest.json"), observation.ToJsonString());
            if (observation["verified_loot"].GetValue<bool>())
            {
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "dungeon-loot-result.json"), observation.ToJsonString());
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "dungeon-skills.json"),
                    await AgentTools.Execute("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "list" }, default));
                Console.WriteLine($"DUNGEON_LOOT_VERIFIED elapsed_ms={clock.ElapsedMilliseconds} key_observed=true initially_locked=true inventory_gain=true");
                NativeAgent.Stop(); await OnMain(() => { Halt.All(); return true; });
                return;
            }
        }
    }
    async Task Opportunities()
    {
        if (Natural) { await NaturalOpportunities(); return; }
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false; Manual.On = false;
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer; originX = (int)p.Center.X / 16; floor = (int)p.Bottom.Y / 16 + 3;
            for (int x = originX - 35; x <= originX + 45; x++)
            {
                for (int y = floor - 20; y < floor; y++) Main.tile[x, y].ClearEverything();
                Main.tile[x, floor].ResetToType(TileID.Stone);
            }
            foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe);
            p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.ThrowingKnife); p.inventory[1].stack = 500;
