            p.inventory[2].SetDefaults(ItemID.Wood); p.inventory[2].stack = 100;
            p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 0; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 100;
            p.position = new Vector2(originX * 16, floor * 16 - p.height); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)p.position.Y / 16;
            int chest = WorldGen.PlaceChest(originX + 5, floor - 1);
            if (chest < 0) throw new Exception("opportunity chest placement failed");
            Main.chest[chest].item[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.IronBar); Main.chest[chest].item[0].stack = 7;
            WorldGen.PlaceObject(originX + 12, floor - 1, TileID.Heart);
            WorldGen.PlaceObject(originX - 12, floor - 1, TileID.Heart);
            WorldGen.PlaceTile(originX - 10, floor - 1, TileID.PressurePlates, forced: true);
            if (!Main.tile[originX + 12, floor - 1].HasTile || !Main.tile[originX - 10, floor - 1].HasTile)
                throw new Exception($"opportunity fixture failed heart={Main.tile[originX + 12, floor - 1].HasTile}/{Main.tile[originX + 12, floor - 1].TileType} trap={Main.tile[originX - 10, floor - 1].HasTile}/{Main.tile[originX - 10, floor - 1].TileType}");
            return true;
        });
        var claim = await Call("control", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "claim", ["scope"] = "task",
            ["channels"] = new JsonArray("movement", "item_use"), ["reason"] = "verify legacy workflows cannot retake agent input" });
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            if (StartRun.Start(out string startReason) || startReason != "control_owned_by_agent")
                throw new Exception("legacy run ignored the agent claim");
            if (TreasureGrab.Start(originX + 5, floor - 2, out string grabReason) || grabReason != "control_owned_by_agent")
                throw new Exception("legacy treasure start ignored the agent claim");
            if (WofPrep.Start(originX, floor - 2, 1, out string prepReason) || prepReason != "control_owned_by_agent")
                throw new Exception("legacy boss preparation ignored the agent claim");
            if (StartRun.IsRunning || TreasureGrab.IsRunning || RecedingNav.Active)
                throw new Exception("denied workflow mutated execution state");
            var npc = Main.npc[Main.maxNPCs - 1];
            bool fighting = WofFight.On;
            try
            {
                var boss = new NPC(); boss.SetDefaults(NPCID.WallofFlesh);
                boss.active = true; boss.Center = Main.LocalPlayer.Center + new Vector2(160, 0);
                Main.npc[Main.maxNPCs - 1] = boss;
                WofFight.On = true;
                Main.LocalPlayer.controlLeft = Main.LocalPlayer.controlRight = Main.LocalPlayer.controlUseItem = false;
                WofFight.Tick();
                if (Main.LocalPlayer.controlLeft || Main.LocalPlayer.controlRight || Main.LocalPlayer.controlUseItem)
                    throw new Exception("legacy boss fight wrote inputs despite agent ownership");
                if (!WofFight.On) throw new Exception("blocked legacy boss fight discarded its intent");
            }
            finally { Main.npc[Main.maxNPCs - 1] = npc; WofFight.On = fighting; }
            return true;
        });
        await Call("control", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "release", ["id"] = claim["id"].DeepClone() });
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            bool day = Main.dayTime;
            long ticket = 0;
            var session = (AgentControlSession)typeof(AgentTools).GetField("ControlSession",
                System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(null);
            try
            {
                if (!WofPrep.Start(originX, floor - 2, 1, out string reason)) throw new Exception(reason);
                var phase = WofPrep.Phase;
                Main.dayTime = false;
                if (session.Begin("gather", null, out ticket) != null)
                    throw new Exception("could not establish gather ownership");
                WofPrep.Tick();
                if (WofPrep.Phase != phase || WofPrep.Outcome != "running")
                    throw new Exception("blocked legacy preparation advanced or discarded its phase");
                session.End(ticket);
                WofPrep.Tick();
                if (WofPrep.Phase == phase && WofPrep.Outcome == "running")
                    throw new Exception("legacy preparation did not resume after ownership release");
            }
            finally { session.End(ticket); WofPrep.Stop(); Main.dayTime = day; }
            return true;
        });
        string code = """
            const goal={x:GOAL_X,y:GOAL_Y};
            const initial=tool('get_state');
            const initialCount=t=>initial.inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===t).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0);
            tool('task_plan',{goal:'Collect selected opportunities while preserving the original destination',steps:[
              {id:'selected-loot',description:'Verify actual chest and crystal inventory gains',conditions:[
                {kind:'item',type:22,count:initialCount(22)+7},{kind:'item',type:29,count:initialCount(29)+1}]}]});
            const premature=tool('task_step',{id:'selected-loot',action:'verify'});
            if(premature.verified!==false||premature.cursor!==0) throw new Error('uncollected opportunities passed host verification');
            tool('watch',{events:['nearby_chest','nearby_tile'],tile_types:[12],radius:35});
            const original=step('original',()=>tool('action_start',{tool:'nav_to',args:goal}));
            let events=[];
            for(let i=0;i<4&&!events.length;i++) events=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:500}).events;
            tool('action_control',{handle:original.handle,command:'pause'});
            if(!events.some(e=>e.kind==='nearby_chest')||!events.some(e=>e.kind==='nearby_tile'))
              throw new Error('opportunities were not discovered during navigation');
            const choices=[];
            const chest=events.find(e=>e.kind==='nearby_chest');
            const hearts=events.filter(e=>e.kind==='nearby_tile').sort((a,b)=>a.evidence.x-b.evidence.x);
            const unsafe=hearts[0], safe=hearts[hearts.length-1];
            for(const e of [unsafe,chest,safe]) {
              const f=tool('opportunity_assess',{x:e.evidence.x,y:e.evidence.y,destination:goal});
              const hazard=f.hazards.some(h=>['trap_tile','lava','falling_block'].includes(h.kind));
              const choice={key:e.key,decision:hazard?'skip':'inspect_and_collect',reason:hazard?'measured hazard near target':'needed inventory; low geometric detour; verify interaction',facts:f};
              choices.push(step('choice:'+e.key,()=>choice));
            }
            if(choices[0].decision!=='skip'||choices[1].decision==='skip'||choices[2].decision==='skip')
              throw new Error('opportunity choice did not distinguish measured hazards');
            function count(type){return tool('get_state').inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===type).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0);}
            const barsBefore=count(22), crystalsBefore=count(29);
            const chestAction=tool('action_start',{tool:'collect_treasure',args:{x:chest.evidence.x,y:chest.evidence.y}});
            const opening=tool('action_query',{handle:chestAction.handle,wait_ms:100});
            if(opening.status!=='running') throw new Error('chest finished before interruption was exercised');
            const interrupted=tool('action_control',{handle:chestAction.handle,command:'pause'});
            if(interrupted.status!=='paused'||tool('action_query',{handle:original.handle}).status!=='paused')
              throw new Error('interrupted chest discarded itself or original navigation');
            tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
            tool('action_control',{handle:chestAction.handle,command:'resume'});
            let looted;
            for(let i=0;i<15;i++) {
              looted=tool('action_query',{handle:chestAction.handle,wait_ms:1000});
              if(looted.status!=='running') break;
            }
            if(looted.status!=='completed'||looted.attempt!==2) throw new Error('chest failed to resume: '+JSON.stringify(looted));
            if(count(22)-barsBefore!==7) throw new Error('chest contents not acquired: '+JSON.stringify(looted));
            let gathered=tool('gather',{targets:[{item_type:29,count:crystalsBefore+1,tile_names:['Heart']}],
              source_area:{x:safe.evidence.x-1,y:safe.evidence.y-1,width:3,height:3}});
            for(let i=0;i<15&&gathered.handle;i++) {
              tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
              const state=tool('action_query',{handle:gathered.handle,wait_ms:1000});
              if(state.status==='completed'){gathered=state;break;}
              if(state.status==='paused') tool('action_control',{handle:gathered.handle,command:'resume'});
              else if(state.status!=='running') throw new Error(JSON.stringify(state));
            }
            if(count(29)-crystalsBefore!==1) throw new Error('crystal inventory delta missing');
            tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
            tool('action_control',{handle:original.handle,command:'resume'});
            let restored;
            for(let i=0;i<25;i++) {
              restored=tool('action_query',{handle:original.handle,wait_ms:1000});
              if(restored.status!=='running') break;
            }
            if(restored.status!=='completed'||restored.result.done!==true) throw new Error('original destination not reached: '+JSON.stringify(restored));
            const verified=tool('task_step',{id:'selected-loot',action:'verify'});
            if(verified.verified!==true||verified.status!=='completed') throw new Error('task plan lost or actual collection not verified');
            return {choices,chest_handle:chestAction.handle,chest_result:{status:looted.status,attempt:looted.attempt,outcome:looted.result.outcome},
              original_handle:original.handle,bars_gained:count(22)-barsBefore,crystals_gained:count(29)-crystalsBefore,
              restored:{handle:restored.handle,status:restored.status,attempt:restored.attempt,done:restored.result.done},
              verification:{verified:verified.verified,status:verified.status,evidence:verified.steps[0].evidence}};
            """.Replace("GOAL_X", (originX + 28).ToString()).Replace("GOAL_Y", (floor - 1).ToString());
        var result = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["code"] = code, ["workflow"] = "opportunity-trial", ["event_delivery"] = "script" });
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "opportunity-result.json"), result.ToJsonString());
        if (result["ok"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true || result["value"]?["crystals_gained"]?.GetValue<int>() != 1)
            throw new Exception("opportunity continuation failed: " + result.ToJsonString());
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            if (Main.LocalPlayer.dead || !Main.tile[originX - 12, floor - 1].HasTile)
                throw new Exception("skipped crystal was collected or player died");
            return true;
        });
        Console.WriteLine("OPPORTUNITY_COMPLETE native_engine=true model_calls=0 teleports_after_start=0");
    }

    async Task ShortJungleCollectionTrial(string stage = null)
    {
        string prefix = stage == null ? "jungle-collection" : "progression-" + stage;
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            bool resume = stage != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(QueenResumeRoot);
            if (!resume && (stage == null || stage == "cobalt") && (jungleSpawn == null || !p.ZoneJungle || p.ZoneCrimson || p.ZoneCorrupt || !p.ZoneOverworldHeight
                || Main.GameMode != 0 || p.statLifeMax != 100 || p.ConsumedLifeCrystals != 0))
                throw new Exception("short collection requires a fresh 100 HP classic jungle surface spawn");
            if (stage == "cobalt" && (NPC.downedBoss3 || Main.hardMode || p.HasItem(ItemID.CobaltShield)))
                throw new Exception("progression requires undefeated Skeletron and no Cobalt Shield");
            if (!resume && (stage == null || stage == "cobalt"))
            {
                jungleSpawn["actual_biome"] = "jungle";
                jungleSpawn["zone_jungle"] = p.ZoneJungle; jungleSpawn["zone_crimson"] = p.ZoneCrimson; jungleSpawn["zone_corrupt"] = p.ZoneCorrupt;
                int groundX = (int)p.Center.X / 16, groundY = (int)(p.Bottom.Y + 1) / 16;
                jungleSpawn["ground_tile_type"] = (int)Main.tile[groundX, groundY].TileType;
                if (!Main.tile[groundX, groundY].HasTile || Main.tile[groundX, groundY].TileType != TileID.JungleGrass)
                    throw new Exception("jungle trial requires standing on native jungle grass");
                jungleSpawn["actual_position"] = new JsonObject { ["x"] = p.position.X / 16, ["y"] = p.position.Y / 16 };
            }
            string checkpoint = Path.Combine(QueenResumeRoot ?? "", prefix + "-progress.json");
            if (resume && File.Exists(checkpoint))
            {
                var prior = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(checkpoint));
                foreach (var type in prior["observed_item_types"]?.AsArray() ?? new JsonArray()) progressionItems.Add(type.GetValue<int>());
                protectedLavaContact |= prior["live"]?["prerequisites"]?["protected_lava_contact"]?.GetValue<bool>() == true;
                var saved = prior["after"]["player"];
                var position = new Vector2(saved["pos"]["x"].GetValue<float>(), saved["pos"]["y"].GetValue<float>());
                if (!saved["on_ground"].GetValue<bool>() || saved["dead"].GetValue<bool>())
                    throw new Exception("progression checkpoint requires a living grounded position");
                p.Teleport(position); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)p.position.Y / 16;
            }
            progressionStage = stage; firstJungleAction = null; shortJungleClock = null;
            Player.SavePlayer(Main.ActivePlayerFileData); WorldFile.SaveWorld(false);
            return true;
        });
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_JUNGLE_SPAWN_PROBE") == "1")
        {
            var report = new JsonObject { ["spawn"] = jungleSpawn.DeepClone(), ["world_seed"] = Main.ActiveWorldFileData.SeedText,
                ["model_calls"] = 0, ["terrain_edits"] = 0 };
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "jungle-spawn-native.json"), report.ToJsonString());
            Console.WriteLine("PURE_JUNGLE_SPAWN_PASS " + report.ToJsonString());
            return;
        }
        string prompt = stage switch
        {
            "cobalt" => "/goal 获取钴护盾，放在背包或装备上。自行查询获取方式、前置和召唤条件，按实际难度准备够用的血量、装备、补给及小型战斗场地，击败骷髅王后探索地牢取得护盾。经典难度，结合头与手的实际机制选择攻击目标。",
            "hellstone" => "/goal 高效率采集60个狱岩，最终随身背包至少保留60个。自行查证并完成本世界邪恶Boss、邪恶镐、waterleaf和火焰花、黑曜石、黑曜石皮药水的制作使用、受其保护时实际接触岩浆、取得地狱熔炉这些环节，按现场安排次序与生存能力。记录去地狱、找矿、开采及恢复分别耗时。",
            "prime" => "/goal 制作机械骷髅王召唤物，并实际使用它召唤机械骷髅王。自行查证进度、材料、制作站和使用条件，完成肉山及所需的采矿、收集、装备和场地准备，召唤成功后验收。可复用已有肉山流程中的建房、准备及炸肉山行为，也可保存新技能。",
            _ => "/goal 从当前丛林地表出生点进入丛林地下，收集并使用3颗生命水晶，使永久生命上限达到160，最后仍在丛林地下。途中顺手采集近便的丛林孢子、搜刮箱子、拾取有用掉落物。"
        };
        prompt += "首段行动前用task_policy挂整趟途中反应，行进期间继续补充知识和后继计划；可读取task_reactions模板并按实际装备与地形修改。处理机会后续接原目标，背包整理与局部恢复由已准备的脚本处理。初始提供基础工具、星怒、足量木头、持续鱼鳃和光芒效果，当前装备和世界进度以get_state为准；天然地形和怪物照常运行。低血量只唤醒观察，当前行动继续。使用DAG和可复用JS技能持续推进，以真实物品、进度和宿主验收完成任务。";
        var before = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
        var deaths = new JsonArray(); bool previouslyDead = false;
        Vector2? anchor = null;
        long stillSince = 0;
        bool warned = false;
        int sample = 0;
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            Config.I.AgentUseCodex = true; Config.I.AgentCodexModel = "gpt-6.1-sol";
            shortJungleClock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            NativeAgent.Submit(prompt); return true;
        });
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, prefix + "-start.json"), new JsonObject
        {
            ["prompt"] = prompt, ["spawn"] = jungleSpawn.DeepClone(), ["before"] = before,
            ["world_seed"] = Main.ActiveWorldFileData.SeedText, ["model"] = "gpt-6.1-sol", ["effort"] = "low",
            ["natural_encounters"] = NaturalEncounters, ["stage"] = stage, ["terrain_edits"] = 0,
            ["mining_speed_multiplier"] = Config.I.FastMining ? ConcessionSpeed.MiningSpeedMultiplier : 1,
            ["resumed_from"] = QueenResumeRoot,
            ["package_sha256"] = Convert.ToHexString(System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256.HashData(
                File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "Mods", "TerraBlind.tmod"))))
        }.ToJsonString());
        using var journal = new StreamWriter(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, prefix + "-samples.jsonl")) { AutoFlush = true };
        using var pauses = new StreamWriter(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, prefix + "-pauses.jsonl")) { AutoFlush = true };
        try
        {
            while (true)
            {
                await Task.Delay(1000);
                var state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject { ["include_watch_history"] = sample++ % 10 == 0 });
                var live = await OnMain(() => new JsonObject { ["used_crystals"] = Main.LocalPlayer.ConsumedLifeCrystals,
                    ["underground_jungle"] = Main.LocalPlayer.ZoneJungle && Main.LocalPlayer.Center.Y / 16 > Main.worldSurface,
                    ["skeletron_defeated"] = NPC.downedBoss3, ["evil_boss_defeated"] = NPC.downedBoss2, ["hardmode"] = Main.hardMode,
                    ["cobalt_shields"] = Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Concat(Main.LocalPlayer.armor.Take(10)).Where(i => i.type == ItemID.CobaltShield).Sum(i => i.stack),
                    ["hellstone"] = Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Where(i => i.type == ItemID.Hellstone).Sum(i => i.stack),
                    ["prerequisites"] = ProgressionPrerequisites(),
                    ["prime_summon"] = primeSummon?.DeepClone() });
                long now = shortJungleClock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
                bool dead = state["player"]["dead"].GetValue<bool>();
                if (dead && !previouslyDead) deaths.Add(new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = now, ["evidence"] = state["last_death"]?.DeepClone() });
                previouslyDead = dead;
                var position = new Vector2(state["player"]["pos"]["x"].GetValue<float>(), state["player"]["pos"]["y"].GetValue<float>());
                if (!anchor.HasValue || Vector2.DistanceSquared(position, anchor.Value) > 16 || dead)
                {
                    if (warned) pauses.WriteLine(new JsonObject { ["started_ms"] = stillSince, ["ended_ms"] = now,
                        ["duration_ms"] = now - stillSince, ["position_pixels"] = state["player"]["pos"]?.DeepClone() }.ToJsonString());
                    anchor = position; stillSince = now; warned = false;
                }
                if (!dead && !warned && now - stillSince >= 10000)
                {
                    warned = true;
                    var warning = new JsonObject { ["kind"] = "stationary_over_10s", ["elapsed_ms"] = now,
                        ["stationary_ms"] = now - stillSince, ["position_pixels"] = state["player"]["pos"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["continuity"] = state["execution_continuity"]?.DeepClone(), ["agent"] = state["agent"]?.DeepClone() };
                    pauses.WriteLine(warning.ToJsonString()); Console.WriteLine(prefix + " WARNING " + warning);
                }
                bool actual = stage switch
                {
                    "cobalt" => live["skeletron_defeated"].GetValue<bool>() && live["cobalt_shields"].GetValue<int>() > 0,
                    "hellstone" => live["hellstone"].GetValue<int>() >= 60 && live["evil_boss_defeated"].GetValue<bool>()
                        && live["prerequisites"].AsObject().All(pair => pair.Value.GetValue<bool>()),
                    "prime" => live["hardmode"].GetValue<bool>() && primeSummon != null,
                    _ => state["permanent_max_health"].GetValue<int>() >= 160
                        && live["used_crystals"].GetValue<int>() >= 3 && live["underground_jungle"].GetValue<bool>()
                };
                bool completed = !dead && actual && state["task_plan"]?["status"]?.ToString() == "completed"
                    && state["goal_verification"]?["verified"]?.GetValue<bool>() == true;
                journal.WriteLine(new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = now, ["live"] = live.DeepClone(), ["state"] = state.DeepClone() }.ToJsonString());
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, prefix + "-progress.json"), new JsonObject
                {
                    ["ok"] = completed, ["elapsed_ms"] = now, ["live"] = live, ["after"] = state,
                    ["observed_item_types"] = new JsonArray(progressionItems.OrderBy(type => type).Select(type => JsonValue.Create(type)).ToArray()),
                    ["deaths"] = deaths.DeepClone(), ["first_action"] = firstJungleAction?.DeepClone(),
                    ["stationary_ms"] = now - stillSince, ["spawn"] = jungleSpawn.DeepClone()
                }.ToJsonString());
                if (completed)
                { Console.WriteLine(prefix + " PASS elapsed_ms=" + now + " deaths=" + deaths.Count + " actual=" + live); return; }
                if (NativeAgent.Status is "failed" or "idle" or "needs_repair" or "paused")
                    throw new Exception(prefix + " stopped before host verification: " + NativeAgent.Status);
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop(); Player.SavePlayer(Main.ActivePlayerFileData); WorldFile.SaveWorld(false);
                shortJungleClock = null;
                Console.WriteLine(prefix + " CHECKPOINT_SAVED root=" + Main.SavePath); return true;
            });
        }
    }

    JsonObject QueenCoverage() => new() { ["covered_ms"] = queenCoveredMs, ["uncovered_ms"] = queenUncoveredMs,
        ["respawn_ms"] = queenRespawnMs, ["navigation_compute_ms"] = queenComputeMs,
        ["current_gap_ms"] = queenGapStart < 0 ? 0 : queenClock.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds - queenGapStart,
        ["gaps"] = queenGaps.DeepClone() };

    async Task<JsonObject> AuthorDecisionPolicy(JsonObject config, JsonObject before, JsonArray waypoints, CancellationToken token)
    {
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_AUTHOR_REPLAY") is string source)
        {
            var previous = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(source));
            var restored = await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "save", ["name"] = "decision_authored_reactions",
                ["expected_revision"] = 0, ["code"] = previous["code"].DeepClone() });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "decision-author.json"), new JsonObject
                { ["mode"] = "replay", ["source"] = source, ["model_calls"] = 0, ["program"] = restored.DeepClone() }.ToJsonString());
            return restored;
        }
        if (!ModLoader.TryGetMod("TerraBlind", out var production)) throw new Exception("production mod missing");
        var schemas = JsonNode.Parse(Encoding.UTF8.GetString(production.GetFileBytes("Agent/Tools.json"))).AsArray();
        AgentScript.DescribePrograms(schemas);
        string[] allowed = { "game_api", "get_state", "combat_assess", "inspect_terrain", "script_program" };
        var tools = new JsonArray(schemas.Where(t => allowed.Contains(t["function"]["name"].ToString())).Select(t => t.DeepClone()).ToArray());
        var scene = new JsonObject { ["config"] = config.DeepClone(), ["route"] = waypoints.DeepClone() };
        foreach (string key in new[] { "player", "inventory_slots", "equipment_slots", "buffs", "depth" })
            scene[key] = before[key]?.DeepClone();
        string request = "导航已在后台执行。为这趟地下丛林旅程准备并保存一份持续现场反应策略，技能名decision_authored_reactions，参数为input。"
            + "希望遇怪仍能赶路，结合装备与射界选武器，必要时避伤，沿途判断资源和箱子是否值得拿。请在合适的位置使用后台Jev快速判断，等待回答期间继续当前动作。"
            + "准备阶段可查询现场、模板与接口并保存技能；行动写入on处理函数，随后由宿主挂到task_policy。"
            + "已有comparison_reactions可读、可复用，input给本轮目标和预算。最多12次工具调用。保存后简短说明采用了哪些现场判断。\n"
            + scene.ToJsonString();
        string model = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_AUTHOR_MODEL") ?? Config.I.AgentCodexModel;
        var calls = new JsonArray(); var replies = new JsonArray(); var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        using var author = new CodexAgent();
        using var timeout = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(token);
        timeout.CancelAfter(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(90));
        try
        {
            await author.RunAsync(request, Config.I.AgentCodexPath, Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "TerraBlindAgent"), model,
                "你为Terraria游戏内Agent编写可复用的JavaScript现场策略。源码是同步函数体，使用input读取参数，tool调用游戏工具，on注册持续处理函数。\n"
                    + AgentScript.RuntimeInstructions, tools, async (name, args, ct) =>
                {
                    if (!allowed.Contains(name) || calls.Count >= 12) throw new InvalidOperationException("decision author scope exceeded");
                    if (name == "script_program" && args["operation"]?.ToString() is "save" or "patch"
                        && args["name"]?.ToString() != "decision_authored_reactions")
                        throw new InvalidOperationException("author may save only decision_authored_reactions");
                    string result = await AgentTools.Execute(name, args, ct);
                    calls.Add(new JsonObject { ["tool"] = name, ["args"] = args.DeepClone(), ["result"] = result });
                    return result;
                }, reply => replies.Add(reply), timeout.Token);
            var saved = await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "get", ["name"] = "decision_authored_reactions" });
            if (saved["found"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("author ended without saving a policy");
            return saved;
        }
        finally
        {
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "decision-author.json"), new JsonObject
            { ["model"] = model, ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["request"] = request,
                ["calls"] = calls, ["replies"] = replies, ["usage"] = author.TokenUsage }.ToJsonString());
        }
    }

    async Task CheckCombatBatch()
    {
        var method = typeof(VanillaNavigationDemo).GetMethod("Observe", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic);
        var snapshot = await OnMain(() =>
        {
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            int x = (int)p.Center.X / 16, y = (int)p.Center.Y / 16;
            return VanillaFrame.Capture(p, Rectangle.Intersect(new Rectangle(x - 32, y - 16, 64, 96),
                new Rectangle(0, 0, Main.maxTilesX, Main.maxTilesY)), true);
        });
        Task<JsonObject> Observe(JsonObject request) => (Task<JsonObject>)method.Invoke(VanillaNavigationDemo.Instance,
            new object[] { request, CancellationToken.None });
        var branches = new JsonArray(new[] { 0, 1, 2, 5, 6 }.Select(keys => (JsonNode)new JsonObject {
            ["id"] = keys.ToString(), ["controls"] = new JsonArray(Enumerable.Repeat(keys, 24).Concat(Enumerable.Repeat(0, 48))
                .Select(k => (JsonNode)JsonValue.Create(k)).ToArray()) }).ToArray());
        var batched = await Observe(new JsonObject { ["command"] = "combat_replays", ["snapshot"] = snapshot.DeepClone(), ["branches"] = branches.DeepClone() });
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        int frames = 0;
        foreach (var branch in branches)
        {
            var single = await Observe(new JsonObject { ["command"] = "replay", ["snapshot"] = snapshot.DeepClone(),
                ["controls"] = branch["controls"].DeepClone(), ["detailed"] = false, ["stop_on_damage"] = true });
            var match = batched["branches"].AsArray().First(b => b["id"].ToString() == branch["id"].ToString());
            if (!JsonNode.DeepEquals(single["frames"], match["frames"]) || !JsonNode.DeepEquals(single["outcome"], match["outcome"]))
                throw new Exception("Combat batch diverged from single replay: " + branch["id"]);
            frames += single["frames"].AsArray().Count;
        }
        var report = new JsonObject { ["passed"] = true, ["branches"] = branches.Count, ["matched_frames"] = frames,
            ["batch_roundtrip_ms"] = batched["roundtrip_ms"].DeepClone(), ["batch_engine_ms"] = batched["total_ms"].DeepClone(),
            ["single_replays_ms"] = clock.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds };
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "combat-batch-check.json"), report.ToJsonString());
        Console.WriteLine("COMBAT_BATCH_CHECK " + report.ToJsonString());
    }

    async Task CheckCombatDamage()
    {
        bool traceDamage = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_DAMAGE_TRACE") == "1";
        var method = typeof(VanillaNavigationDemo).GetMethod("Observe", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic);
        Task<JsonObject> Observe(JsonObject request) => (Task<JsonObject>)method.Invoke(VanillaNavigationDemo.Instance,
            new object[] { request, CancellationToken.None });
        var cases = new JsonArray();
        Vector2 anchor = await OnMain(() => Main.LocalPlayer.position);
        int cx = (int)(anchor.X + Main.LocalPlayer.width / 2f) / 16;
        int floor = (int)(anchor.Y + Main.LocalPlayer.height + 1) / 16;
        try
        {
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                BoundaryInputPlayer.Drive = p =>
                { p.position = anchor; p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.immune = true; p.immuneTime = 60; p.controlUseItem = false; };
                return true;
            });
            foreach (string scenario in new[] { "healthy", "poison", "spikes", "fall", "drowning", "suffocation", "gills", "poison_drowning", "poison_gills" })
            {
                var snapshot = await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                    for (int x = cx - 2; x <= cx + 2; x++)
                        for (int y = floor - 18; y <= floor; y++) Main.tile[x, y].ClearEverything();
                    for (int x = cx - 2; x <= cx + 2; x++)
                    { var tile = Main.tile[x, floor]; tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = scenario == "spikes" ? TileID.Spikes : TileID.Dirt; }
                    p.position = new Vector2(cx * 16 + 8 - p.width / 2f, floor * 16 - p.height - (scenario == "fall" ? 12 * 16 : 0));
                    p.velocity = new Vector2(0, scenario == "fall" ? 10 : 0);
                    p.dead = false; p.immune = false; p.immuneTime = 0; Array.Clear(p.hurtCooldowns);
                    p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 5; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 200;
                    p.itemAnimation = p.itemTime = 0; p.controlUseItem = false;
                    p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = scenario == "fall" ? floor - 31 : (int)p.position.Y / 16;
                    Array.Clear(p.buffType); Array.Clear(p.buffTime); p.lifeRegen = p.lifeRegenCount = 0;
                    p.breath = p.breathMax; p.breathCD = 0;
                    if (scenario is "poison" or "poison_drowning" or "poison_gills")
                    { p.AddBuff(BuffID.Poisoned, 600); p.lifeRegenCount = -119; }
                    if (scenario is "drowning" or "gills" or "suffocation" or "poison_drowning" or "poison_gills")
                    {
                        for (int x = cx - 1; x <= cx + 1; x++)
                            for (int y = floor - 4; y < floor; y++)
                            {
                                var tile = Main.tile[x, y];
                                if (scenario == "suffocation") { tile.HasTile = true; tile.TileType = TileID.Sand; }
                                else { tile.LiquidAmount = 255; tile.LiquidType = LiquidID.Water; }
                            }
                        if (scenario == "suffocation") { p.AddBuff(BuffID.Suffocation, 600); p.lifeRegenCount = -119; }
                        else { p.breath = 0; if (scenario is "gills" or "poison_gills") p.AddBuff(BuffID.Gills, 600); }
                    }
                    int centerY = (int)p.Center.Y / 16;
                    return VanillaFrame.Capture(p, new Rectangle(cx - 32, centerY - 16, 64, 96), true);
                });
                var controls = new JsonArray(Enumerable.Repeat(0, 72).Select(k => (JsonNode)JsonValue.Create(k)).ToArray());
                var stopped = await Observe(new JsonObject { ["command"] = "replay", ["snapshot"] = snapshot.DeepClone(),
                    ["controls"] = controls.DeepClone(), ["detailed"] = false, ["stop_on_damage"] = true });
                var full = await Observe(new JsonObject { ["command"] = "replay", ["snapshot"] = snapshot.DeepClone(),
                    ["controls"] = controls.DeepClone(), ["detailed"] = false, ["stop_on_damage"] = false });
                JsonObject batch = null;
                var legacy = await Observe(new JsonObject { ["command"] = "combat_replays", ["snapshot"] = snapshot.DeepClone(),
                    ["branches"] = new JsonArray(new JsonObject { ["id"] = scenario, ["controls"] = controls.DeepClone() }) });
                if (!JsonNode.DeepEquals(stopped["frames"], legacy["branches"][0]["frames"]))
                    throw new Exception("Default batch behavior changed: " + scenario);
                if (traceDamage)
                    batch = await Observe(new JsonObject { ["command"] = "combat_replays", ["snapshot"] = snapshot.DeepClone(), ["trace_damage"] = true,
                        ["branches"] = new JsonArray(new JsonObject { ["id"] = scenario, ["controls"] = controls.DeepClone() }) });
                var row = new JsonObject { ["scenario"] = scenario, ["snapshot"] = snapshot,
                    ["stopped"] = stopped, ["full"] = full, ["batch"] = batch };
                cases.Add(row);
                if (traceDamage && !JsonNode.DeepEquals(full["frames"], batch["branches"][0]["frames"]))
                    throw new Exception("Damage tracing changed native trajectory: " + scenario);
                if (traceDamage)
                {
                    var health = batch["branches"][0]["health_frames"].AsArray();
                    if (health.Count != full["frames"].AsArray().Count || health.Where((h, i) =>
                        h["frame"].GetValue<int>() != i + 1 || h["hp"].GetValue<int>() != full["frames"][i]["hp"].GetValue<int>()).Any())
                        throw new Exception("PostUpdate health does not match final native frame: " + scenario);
                    var classifier = typeof(CombatForecast).GetMethod("EvaluateLanding", HiddenStatic)
                        ?? throw new Exception("Native landing classifier is missing");
                    foreach (int minimum in new[] { 24, 36 })
                    {
                        var landing = ((int Frame, string Reason))classifier.Invoke(null, new object[] { batch["branches"][0].AsObject(), 200, minimum });
                        bool safe = scenario is "healthy" or "poison" or "gills" or "poison_gills";
                        if (landing.Frame != (safe ? minimum : 0))
                            throw new Exception("Native landing classification incorrect: " + scenario + " " + landing);
                        row["landing_" + minimum] = new JsonObject { ["frame"] = landing.Frame, ["reason"] = landing.Reason };
                    }
                }
                if (scenario is "healthy" or "poison" or "gills" or "poison_gills")
                {
                    if (full["outcome"].ToString() != "complete" || full["frames"].AsArray().Count != 72
                        || full["frames"][71]["grounded"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true)
                        throw new Exception("Damage probe did not complete standing trajectory: " + scenario);
                    if (traceDamage && batch["branches"][0]["hurt_events"].AsArray().Count != 0)
                        throw new Exception("Native hurt event occurred in passive case: " + scenario);
                }
                else if (stopped["outcome"].ToString() != "damaged" || (scenario is "spikes" or "fall")
                    && traceDamage && batch["branches"][0]["hurt_events"].AsArray().Count == 0)
                    throw new Exception("Native damage was not detected: " + scenario);
                if (scenario == "poison" && (stopped["frames"].AsArray().Count >= 24 || full["frames"][71]["hp"].GetValue<int>() >= 200))
                    throw new Exception("Poison probe did not reproduce premature interruption");
                Console.WriteLine("COMBAT_DAMAGE_CASE " + new JsonObject { ["scenario"] = scenario,
                    ["stopped_frames"] = stopped["frames"].AsArray().Count, ["full_frames"] = full["frames"].AsArray().Count,
                    ["end_hp"] = full["frames"].AsArray()[^1]["hp"].DeepClone(),
                    ["landing"] = row["landing_24"]?.DeepClone(),
                    ["hurt_events"] = batch?["branches"]?[0]?["hurt_events"]?.DeepClone() }.ToJsonString());
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            await OnMain(() => { BoundaryInputPlayer.Drive = null; return true; });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "combat-damage-check.json"), new JsonObject {
                ["trace_damage"] = traceDamage, ["terrain_edits"] = 95, ["cases"] = cases }.ToJsonString());
        }
        Console.WriteLine("COMBAT_DAMAGE_CHECK_COMPLETE cases=" + cases.Count + " model_calls=0");
    }

    async Task FightDecisionComparison(string variant)
    {
        if (variant is not ("baseline" or "combat")) throw new ArgumentException("Fight comparison expects baseline or combat");
        bool model = variant == "combat";
        bool codeDodge = !model && Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_IDLE_BASELINE") != "1";
        int duration = int.Parse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_SECONDS") ?? "40");
        int health = int.Parse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_HEALTH") ?? "200");
        var warmup = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        var start = await OnMain(() => Main.LocalPlayer.position);
        var workerReady = typeof(VanillaNavigationDemo).GetProperty("WorkerReady", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic);
        while (!await OnMain(() =>
        {
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            p.position = start; p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.immune = true; p.immuneTime = 60;
            return (bool)workerReady.GetValue(VanillaNavigationDemo.Instance);
        }))
        {
            if (warmup.Elapsed.TotalSeconds > 90) throw new Exception("Native movement worker did not become ready before the encounter");
            await Task.Delay(250);
        }
        warmup.Stop();
        Console.WriteLine("DECISION_FIGHT_WORKER_READY warmup_ms=" + warmup.ElapsedMilliseconds);
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_DAMAGE_CHECK") == "1") { await CheckCombatDamage(); return; }
        var anchor = await OnMain(() =>
        {
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            p.position = start; p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)start.Y / 16;
            foreach (var item in p.inventory.Where(CombatLoadout.Usable)) item.TurnToAir();
            p.inventory[4].SetDefaults(ItemID.ThrowingKnife); p.inventory[4].stack = 250; p.selectedItem = 4;
            p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = (health - 100) / 20;
            p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = health;
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_START_POISON") == "1") p.AddBuff(BuffID.Poisoned, 3600);
            return p.Center;
        });
        await Call("set_combat", new JsonObject { ["auto_attack"] = true, ["auto_dodge"] = codeDodge, ["weapon_type"] = ItemID.ThrowingKnife });
        if (model)
        {
            var helper = await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "save", ["name"] = "joint_combat",
                ["expected_revision"] = 0, ["code"] = ScriptLibrary.Example("joint_combat")["code"].DeepClone() });
            if (helper["error"] != null) throw new Exception("Joint fight skill rejected: " + helper);
            var input = new JsonObject { ["context"] = "native-hornet-fight", ["interval_ms"] = 750, ["max_age_ms"] = 2000,
                ["destination"] = new JsonObject { ["x"] = anchor.X / 16, ["y"] = anchor.Y / 16 },
                ["instruction"] = "Fight the nearby enemies with throwing knives. Evade contact and stingers using the natural terrain. Stay within 20 tiles horizontally and 10 tiles vertically of the destination; keep jumps low." };
            var policy = await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "get" });
            var registered = await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "set",
                ["expected_revision"] = policy["revision"].DeepClone(),
                ["code"] = "const config=" + input.ToJsonString() + ";let last=null;on('tick',()=>{const r=skill('joint_combat',"
                    + helper["revision"] + ",{...config,last_request:last},'combat');if(r.request_id)last=r.request_id;return {handled:true};});" });
            if (registered["error"] != null) throw new Exception("Joint fight policy rejected: " + registered);
        }
        var before = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
        Task<JsonObject> StartFight(int seconds) => Call("action_start", new JsonObject { ["tool"] = "run_script", ["args"] = new JsonObject {
            ["workflow"] = "decision-fight", ["code"] = "return tool('fight',{seconds:" + seconds + ",max_dist:30});" } });
        var fight = await StartFight(duration);
        var samples = new JsonArray(); var spawns = new JsonArray(); var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        var spawned = new List<int>();
        JsonObject action = null;
        Console.WriteLine("DECISION_FIGHT_START variant=" + variant + " code_dodge=" + codeDodge);
        try
        {
            while (clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < duration)
            {
                var measured = await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                    if (samples.Count == 0) { p.immune = false; p.immuneTime = 0; }
                    if (spawns.Count < 8 && !spawned.Any(i => Main.npc[i].active && Main.npc[i].type == NPCID.Hornet)
                        && clock.ElapsedMilliseconds >= spawns.Count * 2500)
                        foreach (int dx in new[] { 200, -200, 160, -160 })
                        {
                            var point = p.Center + new Vector2(dx, -64);
                            if (Collision.SolidCollision(point - new Vector2(20), 40, 40)
                                || !Collision.CanHitLine(p.Center, 4, 4, point, 4, 4)) continue;
                            int who = NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_Misc("DecisionFightTrial"), (int)point.X, (int)point.Y, NPCID.Hornet);
                            spawned.Add(who);
                            spawns.Add(new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["who"] = who,
                                ["x"] = Main.npc[who].position.X, ["y"] = Main.npc[who].position.Y, ["hp"] = Main.npc[who].life });
                            break;
                        }
                    return new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount,
                        ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["dead"] = p.dead, ["x"] = p.position.X, ["y"] = p.position.Y,
                        ["vx"] = p.velocity.X, ["vy"] = p.velocity.Y, ["attack"] = p.controlUseItem,
                        ["item_animation"] = p.itemAnimation, ["held_type"] = p.HeldItem.type,
                        ["selected_slot"] = p.selectedItem, ["mouse_interface"] = p.mouseInterface,
                        ["delay_use_item"] = p.delayUseItem, ["no_items"] = p.noItems, ["cced"] = p.CCed,
                        ["item_time"] = p.itemTime, ["reuse_delay"] = p.reuseDelay, ["inventory_open"] = Main.playerInventory, ["focused"] = Main.hasFocus,
                        ["enemies"] = new JsonArray(Main.npc.Where(n => n.active && !n.friendly && n.damage > 0
                            && Vector2.Distance(n.Center, p.Center) < 480).Select(n => (JsonNode)new JsonObject {
                                ["who"] = n.whoAmI, ["type"] = n.type, ["hp"] = n.life }).ToArray()),
                        ["hostile_projectiles"] = Main.projectile.Count(pj => pj.active && pj.hostile),
                        ["last_damage"] = (DamageField.GetValue(null) as JsonNode)?.DeepClone() };
                });
                samples.Add(measured);
                action = await Call("action_query", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = fight["handle"].DeepClone() });
                if (measured["dead"].GetValue<bool>() || action["status"]?.ToString() is "failed" or "cancelled") break;
                if (action["status"]?.ToString() == "completed")
                {
                    if (spawns.Count >= 8) break;
                    fight = await StartFight(Math.Max(1, duration - (int)clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds));
                }
                await Task.Delay(250);
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            await Call("action_control", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = fight["handle"].DeepClone(), ["command"] = "cancel" });
            var policy = await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "get" });
            await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "clear", ["expected_revision"] = policy["revision"].DeepClone() });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "decision-comparison.json"), new JsonObject {
                ["variant"] = variant, ["fight_trial"] = true, ["code_dodge"] = codeDodge, ["terrain_edits"] = 0, ["unarmed"] = false,
                ["initial_poison"] = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_START_POISON") == "1",
                ["policy_config"] = new JsonObject { ["weapon_order"] = new JsonArray((int)ItemID.ThrowingKnife) },
                ["seed"] = Main.ActiveWorldFileData.SeedText, ["start_health"] = health, ["spawns"] = spawns,
                ["worker_warmup_ms"] = warmup.ElapsedMilliseconds,
                ["preparation_position_held"] = true,
                ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["waypoints"] = new JsonArray(),
                ["before"] = before, ["after"] = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject()), ["samples"] = samples, ["action"] = action
            }.ToJsonString());
        }
        if (spawns.Count == 0) throw new Exception("No open native hornet spawn was found; terrain unchanged");
        Console.WriteLine("DECISION_FIGHT_RECORDED variant=" + variant + " samples=" + samples.Count + " spawns=" + spawns.Count);
    }

    async Task DecisionComparison(string variant)
    {
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_FIGHT_TRIAL") == "1") { await FightDecisionComparison(variant); return; }
        bool intents = variant is "intent" or "intent_baseline";
        bool maneuvers = variant is "maneuver" or "maneuver_baseline";
        bool tactics = intents || maneuvers || variant is "tactics" or "tactics_baseline";
        string[] dimensions = variant is "baseline" or "tactics_baseline" or "maneuver_baseline" or "intent_baseline" ? Array.Empty<string>() : variant.Split(',');
        if (dimensions.Any(d => d is not ("weapon" or "movement" or "opportunity" or "combat" or "tactics" or "maneuver" or "intent")))
            throw new ArgumentException("Unknown comparison dimension: " + variant);
        bool strongWeapon = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_STRONG_WEAPON") == "1";
        int startIndex = int.Parse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_ROUTE_START") ?? "-1");
        int health = int.Parse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_HEALTH") ?? "100");
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            var player = Main.LocalPlayer;
            player.inventory[4].SetDefaults(strongWeapon ? ItemID.Starfury : ItemID.WoodenBow);
            player.inventory[5].SetDefaults(ItemID.ThrowingKnife); player.inventory[5].stack = 250;
            player.inventory[6].SetDefaults(ItemID.WoodenArrow); player.inventory[6].stack = 500;
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_UNARMED") == "1")
                foreach (var item in player.inventory.Where(CombatLoadout.Usable)) item.TurnToAir();
            return true;
        });
        var descent = await Call("find_descent", new JsonObject { ["name"] = "jungle" });
        if (descent["found"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("No natural jungle descent: " + descent);
        var route = await Call("descent_route", new JsonObject { ["name"] = "jungle", ["limit"] = startIndex < 0 ? 500 : 512 });
        var line = route["line"].AsArray();
        if (startIndex >= 0 && route["next_offset"] != null)
        {
            var next = await Call("descent_route", new JsonObject { ["name"] = "jungle", ["offset"] = route["next_offset"].DeepClone(), ["limit"] = 288 });
            foreach (var point in next["line"].AsArray()) line.Add(point.DeepClone());
        }
        if (startIndex >= line.Count) throw new ArgumentException("Comparison start is outside the natural route");
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            if (startIndex >= 0)
            {
                p.Teleport(new Vector2(line[startIndex][0].GetValue<int>() * 16 + 8 - p.width / 2f,
                    line[startIndex][1].GetValue<int>() * 16 + 8 - p.height / 2f));
                p.velocity = Vector2.Zero; p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)p.position.Y / 16;
            }
            p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = (health - 100) / 20;
            p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = health;
            return true;
        });
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_BATCH_CHECK") == "1") await CheckCombatBatch();
        var before = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
        var opportunities = new JsonObject();
        foreach (string name in new[] { "Heart", "Containers", "JunglePlants" })
            opportunities[name] = await Call("find_tiles", new JsonObject { ["name"] = name, ["n"] = 20, ["max_dist"] = 80,
                ["frame_x"] = name == "JunglePlants" ? 144 : null });
        var waypoints = new JsonArray();
        if (startIndex < 0) waypoints.Add(new JsonObject { ["x"] = descent["x"].DeepClone(), ["y"] = descent["y"].DeepClone() });
        foreach (int index in Enumerable.Range(1, startIndex < 0 ? 4 : 6).Select(i => Math.Max(0, startIndex) + i * 80))
            if (index < line.Count) waypoints.Add(new JsonObject { ["x"] = line[index][0].DeepClone(), ["y"] = line[index][1].DeepClone() });
        var config = new JsonObject
        {
            ["baseline"] = variant is "tactics_baseline" or "maneuver_baseline" or "intent_baseline",
            ["decisions"] = new JsonArray(dimensions.Select(d => (JsonNode)JsonValue.Create(d)).ToArray()),
            ["instruction"] = "Travel into the underground jungle efficiently. Handle enemies while keeping the journey moving. Collect useful nearby resources and chests when worth the detour. Use terrain and retreat when equipment is inadequate.",
            ["resources"] = JsonNode.Parse("[{\"item_type\":29,\"count\":10,\"tile_type\":12,\"tile_name\":\"Heart\"},{\"item_type\":331,\"count\":6,\"tile_type\":61,\"tile_name\":\"JunglePlants\",\"frame_x\":144},{\"item_type\":209,\"count\":9}]"),
            ["weapon_order"] = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_WEAPON_ORDER") is string weaponOrder
                ? JsonNode.Parse(weaponOrder) : new JsonArray(strongWeapon ? ItemID.Starfury : ItemID.WoodenBow, ItemID.ThrowingKnife, ItemID.CopperShortsword),
            ["max_detour_tiles"] = 10, ["health_reserve"] = 30, ["allow_trigger_removal"] = true,
            ["trash_types"] = new JsonArray(), ["drop_types"] = new JsonArray(),
            ["decision_interval_ms"] = 750, ["decision_max_age_ms"] = 2000
        };
        if (intents)
        {
            var helper = await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "save", ["name"] = "travel_intent",
                ["expected_revision"] = 0, ["code"] = ScriptLibrary.Example("travel_intent")["code"].DeepClone() });
            if (helper["error"] != null) throw new Exception("Intent skill rejected: " + helper);
            config["intent_skill_revision"] = helper["revision"].DeepClone();
        }
        if (maneuvers)
        {
            var helper = await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "save", ["name"] = "travel_maneuver",
                ["expected_revision"] = 0, ["code"] = ScriptLibrary.Example("travel_maneuver")["code"].DeepClone() });
            if (helper["error"] != null) throw new Exception("Maneuver skill rejected: " + helper);
            config["maneuver_skill_revision"] = helper["revision"].DeepClone();
        }
        if (dimensions.Contains("combat"))
        {
            var helper = await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "save", ["name"] = "joint_combat",
                ["expected_revision"] = 0, ["code"] = ScriptLibrary.Example("joint_combat")["code"].DeepClone() });
            if (helper["error"] != null) throw new Exception("Joint skill rejected: " + helper);
            config["combat_skill_revision"] = helper["revision"].DeepClone();
        }
        var program = await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "save", ["name"] = "comparison_reactions",
            ["expected_revision"] = 0, ["code"] = ScriptLibrary.Example(tactics ? "tactical_reactions"
                : dimensions.Contains("combat") ? "joint_reactions" : "decision_reactions")["code"].DeepClone() });
        if (program["error"] != null) throw new Exception("Comparison skill rejected: " + program);
        bool authored = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_AUTHOR") == "1";
        var initialConfig = (JsonObject)config.DeepClone();
        if (authored) initialConfig["decisions"] = new JsonArray();
        var policy = await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "get" });
        bool candidateSmoke = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_CANDIDATE_SMOKE") == "1";
        if (candidateSmoke) await Call("set_combat", new JsonObject { ["auto_attack"] = true, ["weapon_type"] = ItemID.ThrowingKnife });
        string policyCode = "skill('comparison_reactions'," + program["revision"] + "," + initialConfig.ToJsonString() + ",'journey');";
        if (candidateSmoke) policyCode = """
            let attempts=0;const used=new Set();
            const pose=p=>({x:p.pos.x,y:p.pos.y,vx:p.velocity.x,vy:p.velocity.y,hp:p.hp});
            on('tick',event=>{
              const destination=event.execution?.args;
              if(attempts>=5||!destination) return {handled:true};
              const context='candidate-smoke:'+destination.x+':'+destination.y;
              const c=tool('combat_assess',{forecast:true,route_handoff:true,context,destination,radius:25}),f=c.forecast;
              if(f.status!=='ready'||used.has(f.id)) return {handled:true};
              if(!f.movements.every(m=>m.comparison_frames===f.comparison_frames&&m.observed_comparison_frames<=f.comparison_frames
                  &&m.linear_threat_overlap.evaluated_frames===m.observed_comparison_frames)) throw new Error('comparison_window_mismatch');
              const direction=c.player.controls.left?'left':c.player.controls.right?'right':null;
              const plan=f.plans.find(p=>p.movement===direction&&p.weapon_type===279&&p.movement_available);
              if(!plan) return {handled:true};
              used.add(f.id);attempts++;
              const started=Date.now();
              const r=tool('act',{steps:[{keys:['right'],until:{frames:1}}],combat_candidate:{context,forecast_id:f.id,plan_id:plan.id}});
              const after=tool('combat_assess').player;
              print({candidate_smoke:true,attempt:attempts,forecast:f.id,plan:plan.id,age:f.age_frames,
                elapsed_ms:Date.now()-started,outcome:r.outcome,error:r.error,reason:r.reason,difference:r.difference,
                fresh_age:r.age_frames,lead_frames:r.lead_frames,abort:r.forecast_abort??null,
                decision_forecast_id:r.decision_forecast_id,execution_forecast_id:r.execution_forecast_id,before:pose(c.player),after:pose(after)});
              if(!r.error) attempts=5;
              return {handled:true};
            });
            """;
        var registered = await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "set", ["expected_revision"] = policy["revision"].DeepClone(),
            ["code"] = policyCode });
        if (registered["error"] != null) throw new Exception("Comparison policy rejected: " + registered);
        string script = "const route=" + waypoints.ToJsonString() + ";const results=[];for(let i=0;i<route.length;i++){"
            + "const r=step('leg_'+i,()=>tool('nav_to',route[i]));results.push({i,done:r.done,error:r.error??null,reason:r.reason??null});"
            + "if(r.done!==true)return {status:'yield',blocked:results};}return {status:'done',results};";
        var fight = await Call("action_start", new JsonObject { ["tool"] = "fight", ["args"] = new JsonObject { ["seconds"] = 120d, ["max_dist"] = 25 } });
        var travel = await Call("action_start", new JsonObject { ["tool"] = "run_script", ["args"] = new JsonObject
            { ["workflow"] = "decision-comparison", ["code"] = script } });
        var samples = new JsonArray(); var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        bool encounters = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_JOURNEY_ENCOUNTERS") == "1";
        var spawns = new JsonArray(); var spawned = new List<int>();
        using var authorLifetime = new CancellationTokenSource();
        var authorTask = authored ? AuthorDecisionPolicy(config, before, waypoints, authorLifetime.Token) : null;
        JsonObject authoredProgram = null;
        JsonObject authorFailure = null;
        long? authorAppliedMs = null;
        JsonObject action = null;
        int duration = int.Parse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_SECONDS") ?? "180");
        Console.WriteLine("DECISION_COMPARISON_START " + new JsonObject { ["variant"] = variant, ["waypoints"] = waypoints.DeepClone(),
            ["seed"] = Main.ActiveWorldFileData.SeedText, ["model"] = dimensions.Length > 0 ? "jev-latest" : null });
        try
        {
            while (clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < duration)
            {
                await Task.Delay(250);
                var measured = await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                    if (encounters && spawns.Count < 3 && clock.ElapsedMilliseconds >= 3000 + spawns.Count * 12000
                        && !spawned.Any(i => Main.npc[i].active && Main.npc[i].type == NPCID.Hornet))
                        foreach (int dx in new[] { spawns.Count % 2 == 0 ? 160 : -160, 200, -200 })
                        {
                            var point = p.Center + new Vector2(dx, -64);
                            if (Collision.SolidCollision(point - new Vector2(20), 40, 40)
                                || !Collision.CanHitLine(p.Center, 4, 4, point, 4, 4)) continue;
                            int who = NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_Misc("JourneyDecisionTrial"), (int)point.X, (int)point.Y, NPCID.Hornet);
                            spawned.Add(who);
                            spawns.Add(new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["who"] = who,
                                ["x"] = Main.npc[who].position.X, ["y"] = Main.npc[who].position.Y, ["hp"] = Main.npc[who].life });
                            break;
                        }
                    return new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount,
                        ["hp"] = p.statLife, ["dead"] = p.dead, ["x"] = p.position.X, ["y"] = p.position.Y,
                        ["vx"] = p.velocity.X, ["vy"] = p.velocity.Y, ["attack"] = p.controlUseItem,
                        ["item_animation"] = p.itemAnimation, ["held_type"] = p.HeldItem.type,
                        ["enemies"] = new JsonArray(Main.npc.Where(n => n.active && !n.friendly && n.damage > 0
                            && Vector2.Distance(n.Center, p.Center) < 400).Select(n => (JsonNode)new JsonObject
                            { ["who"] = n.whoAmI, ["type"] = n.type, ["hp"] = n.life }).ToArray()),
                        ["last_damage"] = (DamageField.GetValue(null) as JsonNode)?.DeepClone() };
                });
                samples.Add(measured);
                if (authorTask?.IsCompleted == true && authorAppliedMs == null)
                {
                    authoredProgram = await authorTask;
                    var currentPolicy = await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "get" });
                    var applied = await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "set", ["expected_revision"] = currentPolicy["revision"].DeepClone(),
                        ["code"] = "skill('decision_authored_reactions'," + authoredProgram["revision"] + "," + config.ToJsonString() + ",'journey');" });
                    if (applied["error"] != null) throw new Exception("Authored policy rejected: " + applied);
                    authorAppliedMs = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
                    Console.WriteLine("DECISION_AUTHOR_APPLIED ms=" + authorAppliedMs);
                }
                action = await Call("action_query", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = travel["handle"].DeepClone() });
                if (measured["dead"].GetValue<bool>() || action["status"]?.ToString() is "completed" or "failed" or "cancelled") break;
                if (samples.Count % 4 == 0)
                {
                    var attack = await Call("action_query", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = fight["handle"].DeepClone() });
                    if (attack["status"]?.ToString() == "completed")
                        fight = await Call("action_start", new JsonObject { ["tool"] = "fight", ["args"] = new JsonObject { ["seconds"] = 120d, ["max_dist"] = 25 } });
                }
                if (samples.Count % 4 == 0)
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "decision-comparison-progress.json"), new JsonObject
                        { ["variant"] = variant, ["sample"] = measured.DeepClone(), ["action_status"] = action["status"]?.DeepClone() }.ToJsonString());
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            authorLifetime.Cancel();
            if (authorTask != null)
                try { await authorTask; }
                catch (Exception error) { authorFailure = new JsonObject { ["type"] = error.GetType().Name, ["message"] = error.Message }; }
            await Call("action_control", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = travel["handle"].DeepClone(), ["command"] = "cancel" });
            await Call("action_control", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = fight["handle"].DeepClone(), ["command"] = "cancel" });
            var after = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
            var finalPolicy = await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "get" });
            await Call("task_policy", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "clear", ["expected_revision"] = finalPolicy["revision"].DeepClone() });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "decision-comparison.json"), new JsonObject
            {
                ["variant"] = variant, ["seed"] = Main.ActiveWorldFileData.SeedText,
                ["candidate_smoke"] = candidateSmoke,
                ["route_start_index"] = startIndex, ["start_health"] = health, ["strong_weapon"] = strongWeapon,
                ["policy_config"] = config.DeepClone(),
                ["agent_authored"] = authored, ["author_applied_ms"] = authorAppliedMs, ["authored_program"] = authoredProgram,
                ["author_failure"] = authorFailure,
                ["author_replay_source"] = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_AUTHOR_REPLAY"),
                ["unarmed"] = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_UNARMED") == "1",
                ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["waypoints"] = waypoints,
                ["journey_encounters"] = encounters, ["spawns"] = spawns,
                ["before"] = before, ["nearby_natural_sources"] = opportunities, ["after"] = after, ["samples"] = samples, ["action"] = action
            }.ToJsonString());
        }
        if (authored && (authorAppliedMs == null || authorFailure != null)) throw new Exception("Authored decision policy was not applied; inspect decision-author.json");
        Console.WriteLine("DECISION_COMPARISON_RECORDED variant=" + variant + " samples=" + samples.Count);
    }

    async Task QueenGoalTrial()
    {
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DECISION_COMPARE") is string comparison)
        { await DecisionComparison(comparison); return; }
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(QueenResumeRoot)) await OnMain(() =>
        {
            var player = Main.LocalPlayer;
            var npcs = Main.npc;
            bool manual = Manual.On, use = player.controlUseItem;
            try
            {
                Main.npc = Enumerable.Range(0, Main.maxNPCs).Select(_ => new NPC()).ToArray();
                Manual.On = false;
                FightCoordinator.Start(20);
                player.controlUseItem = true;
                player.GetModPlayer<StateSnapshotPlayer>().SetControls();
                if (player.controlUseItem) throw new Exception("agent input ownership retained raw weapon use");
                FightCoordinator.Stop();
                player.controlUseItem = true;
                player.GetModPlayer<StateSnapshotPlayer>().SetControls();
                if (!player.controlUseItem) throw new Exception("idle input ownership swallowed player weapon use");
                FightCoordinator.Start(20); Manual.On = true;
                player.controlUseItem = true;
                player.GetModPlayer<StateSnapshotPlayer>().SetControls();
                if (!player.controlUseItem) throw new Exception("manual input ownership swallowed player weapon use");
                Console.WriteLine("QUEEN_INPUT_OWNERSHIP_VERIFIED autonomous=true idle=true manual=true");
            }
            finally
            {
                FightCoordinator.Stop(); Main.npc = npcs; Manual.On = manual; player.controlUseItem = use;
            }
            if (Main.GameMode != 0 || player.ConsumedLifeCrystals != 0 || player.statLifeMax != 100)
                throw new Exception("queen trial requires classic mode and a fresh 100-health player");
            int x = (int)player.Center.X / 16, y = (int)(player.Bottom.Y + 1) / 16;
            if (!player.ZoneJungle || player.ZoneCrimson || player.ZoneCorrupt || !player.ZoneOverworldHeight
                || !Main.tile[x, y].HasTile || Main.tile[x, y].TileType != TileID.JungleGrass)
                throw new Exception($"queen trial requires native jungle surface flags and jungle grass underfoot; x={x} y={y} bottom={player.Bottom.Y} jungle={player.ZoneJungle} crimson={player.ZoneCrimson} corrupt={player.ZoneCorrupt} overworld={player.ZoneOverworldHeight} ground={(Main.tile[x, y].HasTile ? Main.tile[x, y].TileType : -1)}");
            jungleSpawn["zone_jungle"] = player.ZoneJungle; jungleSpawn["zone_crimson"] = player.ZoneCrimson;
            jungleSpawn["zone_corrupt"] = player.ZoneCorrupt; jungleSpawn["ground_tile_type"] = (int)Main.tile[x, y].TileType;
            return true;
        });
        var before = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
        if (await OnMain(() => NPC.downedQueenBee)) throw new Exception("queen trial requires fresh world progression");
        int targetHealth = 100;
        string prompt = "/goal 25分钟内制作荆棘旋刃，召唤并击败蜂王，最多死亡一次。生命和盔甲按需要准备。";
        if (before["inventory_slots"].AsArray().Any(item => item["type"]?.GetValue<int>() == ItemID.Starfury))
            throw new Exception("queen trial must start without Starfury");
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(QueenResumeRoot))
        {
            prompt += "这是同一人物和地图的存档续接。先按当前永久生命与背包验收已有进度，推进剩余步骤；地图保留此前采集与放置的改变。";
            string progressFile = Path.Combine(QueenResumeRoot, "queen-progress.json");
            if (File.Exists(progressFile))
            {
                var previous = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(progressFile));
                var state = previous?["after"];
                var evidence = new JsonObject { ["previous_elapsed_ms"] = previous?["elapsed_ms"]?.DeepClone(),
                    ["last_death"] = state?["last_death"]?.DeepClone(),
                    ["last_movement_snapshot"] = state?["boss_control"]?["movement_snapshot"]?.DeepClone(),
                    ["failed_strategies"] = state?["recovery"]?["failure_history"]?.DeepClone(),
                    ["steps"] = new JsonArray((state?["task_plan"]?["steps"] as JsonArray ?? new JsonArray())
                        .Select(step => (JsonNode)new JsonObject { ["id"] = step["id"]?.DeepClone(),
                            ["description"] = step["description"]?.DeepClone(), ["status"] = step["status"]?.DeepClone(),
                            ["actual"] = step["best_actual"]?.DeepClone() }).ToArray()) };
                string priorEvidenceFile = Path.Combine(QueenResumeRoot, "queen-resume-evidence.json");
                if (File.Exists(priorEvidenceFile))
                {
                    var priorEvidence = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(priorEvidenceFile));
                    var unfinished = priorEvidence?["steps"] as JsonArray;
                    evidence["earlier_unfinished_steps"] = new JsonArray((unfinished ?? new JsonArray())
                        .Where(step => step["status"]?.ToString() != "completed").Select(step => step.DeepClone()).ToArray());
                }
                string samplesPath = Path.Combine(QueenResumeRoot, "queen-samples.jsonl");
                if (File.Exists(samplesPath))
                {
                    var encounters = new JsonArray(); JsonObject encounter = null;
                    foreach (string line in File.ReadLines(samplesPath))
                    {
                        var sample = JsonNode.Parse(line); var scene = sample["state"];
                        var bosses = scene?["boss_control"]?["active_bosses"] as JsonArray;
                        if (bosses?.Count is not > 0 || scene["player"]?["dead"]?.GetValue<bool>() == true)
                        { encounter = null; continue; }
                        float px = scene["player"]["pos"]["x"].GetValue<float>() / 16f;
                        float py = scene["player"]["pos"]["y"].GetValue<float>() / 16f;
                        if (encounter == null)
                        {
                            encounter = new JsonObject { ["started_ms"] = sample["elapsed_ms"].DeepClone(),
                                ["bosses_at_start"] = bosses.DeepClone(), ["min_player_hp"] = int.MaxValue,
                                ["player_top_left_start_tiles"] = new JsonObject { ["x"] = px, ["y"] = py },
                                ["player_top_left_extent_tiles"] = new JsonObject
                                    { ["min_x"] = px, ["max_x"] = px, ["min_y"] = py, ["max_y"] = py },
                                ["sample_count"] = 0, ["honey_samples"] = 0, ["held_weapons"] = new JsonObject() };
                            encounters.Add(encounter);
                        }
                        encounter["last_observed_ms"] = sample["elapsed_ms"].DeepClone();
                        encounter["bosses_last_observed"] = bosses.DeepClone();
                        encounter["min_player_hp"] = Math.Min(encounter["min_player_hp"].GetValue<int>(), scene["player"]["hp"].GetValue<int>());
                        encounter["sample_count"] = encounter["sample_count"].GetValue<int>() + 1;
                        encounter["player_top_left_last_tiles"] = new JsonObject { ["x"] = px, ["y"] = py };
                        var extent = encounter["player_top_left_extent_tiles"].AsObject();
                        extent["min_x"] = Math.Min(extent["min_x"].GetValue<float>(), px);
                        extent["max_x"] = Math.Max(extent["max_x"].GetValue<float>(), px);
                        extent["min_y"] = Math.Min(extent["min_y"].GetValue<float>(), py);
                        extent["max_y"] = Math.Max(extent["max_y"].GetValue<float>(), py);
                        if (scene["boss_control"]?["honey_slowed"]?.GetValue<bool>() == true)
                            encounter["honey_samples"] = encounter["honey_samples"].GetValue<int>() + 1;
                        string weapon = scene["equipment"]?["held_item"]?["name"]?.ToString() ?? "unknown";
                        var weapons = encounter["held_weapons"].AsObject();
                        weapons[weapon] = (weapons[weapon]?.GetValue<int>() ?? 0) + 1;
                    }
                    evidence["combat_observations"] = encounters;
                }
                new ScriptLibrary(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "TerraBlindAgent", "programs"))
                    .Save("resume_observations", "return " + evidence.ToJsonString() + ";", 0);
                prompt += "用script_program get name=resume_observations读取上次会话的历史实测记录；当前情况以本轮工具观察为准。";
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "queen-resume-evidence.json"), evidence.ToJsonString());
            }
        }
        var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        await OnMain(() => { queenClock = clock; return true; });
        var deaths = new JsonArray(); bool wasDead = false;
        long? healthMs = null, chakramMs = null, queenSpawnMs = null;
        string trialModel = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_QUEEN_MODEL") ?? "gpt-6.1-sol";
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            Config.I.AgentUseCodex = true; Config.I.AgentCodexModel = trialModel;
            NativeAgent.Submit(prompt);
            return true;
        });
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "queen-start.json"), new JsonObject
        { ["prompt"] = prompt, ["before"] = before.DeepClone(), ["spawn"] = jungleSpawn?.DeepClone(), ["model"] = trialModel, ["effort"] = "medium",
            ["mining_speed_multiplier"] = Config.I.FastMining ? ConcessionSpeed.MiningSpeedMultiplier : 1,
            ["world_seed"] = Main.ActiveWorldFileData.SeedText, ["natural_encounters"] = NaturalEncounters,
            ["resumed_from"] = QueenResumeRoot }.ToJsonString());
        using var journal = new StreamWriter(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "queen-samples.jsonl")) { AutoFlush = true };
        try
        {
            while (true)
            {
                await Task.Delay(1000);
                var state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                bool dead = state["player"]["dead"].GetValue<bool>();
                if (dead && !wasDead) deaths.Add(new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds,
                    ["evidence"] = state["last_death"]?.DeepClone() });
                wasDead = dead;
                if (healthMs == null && state["permanent_max_health"].GetValue<int>() >= targetHealth) healthMs = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
                bool chakram = state["inventory_slots"].AsArray().Any(i => i["type"]?.GetValue<int>() == ItemID.ThornChakram && i["stack"]?.GetValue<int>() > 0);
                if (chakramMs == null && chakram) chakramMs = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
                var boss = await OnMain(() => new JsonObject { ["alive"] = NPC.AnyNPCs(NPCID.QueenBee), ["defeated"] = NPC.downedQueenBee });
                if (queenSpawnMs == null && boss["alive"].GetValue<bool>()) queenSpawnMs = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
                journal.WriteLine(new JsonObject { ["elapsed_ms"] = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["boss"] = boss.DeepClone(), ["state"] = state.DeepClone() }.ToJsonString());
                bool completed = healthMs != null && chakram && boss["defeated"].GetValue<bool>()
                    && state["task_plan"]?["status"]?.ToString() == "completed";
                long elapsed = clock.ElapsedMilliseconds;
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "queen-progress.json"), new JsonObject
                { ["ok"] = completed && elapsed <= 25 * 60 * 1000 && deaths.Count <= 1, ["elapsed_ms"] = elapsed, ["target_health"] = targetHealth, ["health_target_ms"] = healthMs,
                    ["chakram_ms"] = chakramMs, ["queen_spawn_ms"] = queenSpawnMs, ["deaths"] = deaths.DeepClone(),
                    ["boss"] = boss.DeepClone(), ["coverage"] = await OnMain(QueenCoverage), ["after"] = state.DeepClone() }.ToJsonString());
                if (deaths.Count > 1) throw new Exception("queen goal exceeded the one death limit; checkpoint retained");
                if (completed)
                {
                    if (elapsed > 25 * 60 * 1000) throw new Exception("queen goal completed after the 25 minute limit");
                    Console.WriteLine($"QUEEN_GOAL_PASS elapsed_ms={clock.ElapsedMilliseconds} target_health={targetHealth} health_target_ms={healthMs} chakram_ms={chakramMs} queen_spawn_ms={queenSpawnMs} deaths={deaths.Count}"); return;
                }
                if (clock.Elapsed.TotalMinutes >= 25) throw new Exception("queen goal exceeded the 25 minute limit; checkpoint retained");
                if (NativeAgent.Status == "waiting for answer") throw new Exception("autonomous queen goal requires operator input; checkpoint retained");
                if (NativeAgent.Status is "failed" or "idle") throw new Exception("queen goal stopped before verification: " + NativeAgent.Status);
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                NativeAgent.Stop();
                queenClock = null;
                Player.SavePlayer(Main.ActivePlayerFileData);
                WorldFile.SaveWorld(false);
                Console.WriteLine("QUEEN_CHECKPOINT_SAVED root=" + Main.SavePath);
                return true;
            });
        }
    }

    async Task NaturalOpportunities()
    {
        int chestX = 0, chestY = 0, heartX = 0, heartY = 0, lootType = 0, goalX = 0, goalY = 0;
        await OnMain(() =>
        {
            NativeAgent.Stop(); Halt.All(); Config.I.AgentEnabled = true; Config.I.AiClient = false; Manual.On = false;
            Combat.Enabled = Dodge.Enabled = false; AgentPermissions.Reset();
            var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
            if (FullCraftTrial)
            {
                Concessions.Enabled = false;
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(QueenResumeRoot))
                {
                    if (FullQueenRun)
                    {
                        var saved = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(QueenResumeRoot, "queen-progress.json")))["after"];
                        p.position = new Vector2(saved["player"]["pos"]["x"].GetValue<float>(), saved["player"]["pos"]["y"].GetValue<float>());
                        p.velocity = new Vector2(saved["player"]["vel"]["x"].GetValue<float>(), saved["player"]["vel"]["y"].GetValue<float>());
