                        p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = saved["movement_capabilities"]["fall_start_tile_y"].GetValue<int>();
                    }
                    originX = (int)p.Center.X / 16; floor = (int)p.Bottom.Y / 16;
                    Console.WriteLine($"FULL_CRAFT_RESUME source={QueenResumeRoot} x={originX} y={floor} max_hp={p.statLifeMax} chakram={p.inventory.Where(item => item.type == ItemID.ThornChakram).Sum(item => item.stack)} concessions=false");
                    return true;
                }
                foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
                p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperShortsword);
                p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe);
                p.inventory[2].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperAxe);
                p.inventory[3].SetDefaults(ItemID.Wood); p.inventory[3].stack = 9999;
                if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_QUEEN_GOAL") != "1") p.inventory[4].SetDefaults(ItemID.Starfury);
                p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 0; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 100;
                if (FullQueenRun)
                {
                    jungleSpawn["native_spawn_position"] = new JsonObject { ["x"] = p.position.X, ["y"] = p.position.Y };
                    var position = new Vector2(Main.spawnTileX * 16 + 8 - p.width / 2f, Main.spawnTileY * 16 - p.height);
                    if (Collision.SolidCollision(position, p.width, p.height)) throw new Exception("jungle surface placement is blocked");
                    p.Teleport(position); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)(p.position.Y / 16);
                }
                originX = (int)p.Center.X / 16; floor = (int)p.Bottom.Y / 16;
                Console.WriteLine($"FULL_CRAFT_START x={originX} y={floor} spawn={Main.spawnTileX},{Main.spawnTileY} starter_tools=true wood=9999 starfury={p.inventory.Any(item => item.type == ItemID.Starfury)} concessions=false");
                return true;
            }
            (int X, int Y)? Stand(int cx, int cy)
            {
                for (int r = 0; r <= 8; r++)
                    for (int dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++)
                        for (int dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++)
                        {
                            int x = cx + dx, y = cy + dy;
                            if (x < 2 || y < 10 || x >= Main.maxTilesX - 2 || y >= Main.maxTilesY - 2) continue;
                            if (WorldGen.SolidTile(x, y) && WorldGen.SolidTile(x + 1, y)
                                && !Collision.SolidCollision(new Vector2(x * 16, y * 16 - p.height), p.width, p.height)
                                && Main.tile[x, y - 1].LiquidAmount == 0 && Main.tile[x, y - 2].LiquidAmount == 0)
                                return (x, y);
                        }
                return null;
            }
            foreach (var chest in Main.chest)
            {
                if (chest == null || chest.y < Main.worldSurface || !chest.item.Any(i => !i.IsAir) || Chest.IsLocked(chest.x, chest.y)) continue;
                int jungle = 0;
                for (int x = Math.Max(1, chest.x - 25); x <= Math.Min(Main.maxTilesX - 2, chest.x + 25); x++)
                    for (int y = Math.Max(1, chest.y - 25); y <= Math.Min(Main.maxTilesY - 2, chest.y + 25); y++)
                        if (Main.tile[x, y].HasTile && Main.tile[x, y].TileType == TileID.JungleGrass) jungle++;
                if (jungle < 60) continue;
                var start = Stand(chest.x - 5, chest.y + 2);
                if (start == null) continue;
                bool found = false;
                for (int x = Math.Max(1, chest.x - 25); x <= Math.Min(Main.maxTilesX - 2, chest.x + 25) && !found; x++)
                    for (int y = Math.Max(1, chest.y - 25); y <= Math.Min(Main.maxTilesY - 2, chest.y + 25) && !found; y++)
                    {
                        var tile = Main.tile[x, y];
                        if (!tile.HasTile || tile.TileType != TileID.Heart || tile.TileFrameX % 36 != 0 || tile.TileFrameY % 36 != 0) continue;
                        var goal = Stand(x + 6, y + 2);
                        if (goal == null || Math.Abs(goal.Value.X - start.Value.X) + Math.Abs(goal.Value.Y - start.Value.Y) < 8) continue;
                        heartX = x; heartY = y; goalX = goal.Value.X; goalY = goal.Value.Y - 1; found = true;
                    }
                if (!found) continue;
                chestX = chest.x; chestY = chest.y; lootType = chest.item.First(i => !i.IsAir && i.type != ItemID.Wood).type;
                originX = start.Value.X; floor = start.Value.Y;
                break;
            }
            if (lootType == 0) throw new Exception("no native jungle chest/crystal scene found; no terrain was modified");
            foreach (var item in p.inventory) item.TurnToAir();
            p.inventory[0].SetDefaults(ItemID.CopperPickaxe);
            p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.ThrowingKnife); p.inventory[1].stack = 500;
            p.inventory[2].SetDefaults(ItemID.Wood); p.inventory[2].stack = 100;
            p.ConsumedLifeCrystals = 0; p.statLife = p.statLifeMax = p.statLifeMax2 = 100;
            p.position = new Vector2(originX * 16, floor * 16 - p.height); p.velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            p.fallStart = p.fallStart2 = (int)p.position.Y / 16;
            Console.WriteLine($"OPPORTUNITY_NATURAL start={originX},{floor} chest={chestX},{chestY} heart={heartX},{heartY} goal={goalX},{goalY} loot={lootType} fixture_tile_edits=0");
            return true;
        });
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_DAG_HANDOFF") == "1")
        {
            var timer = System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.StartNew();
            JsonObject Node(string id, string[] parents, string code, JsonObject condition) => new()
            {
                ["id"] = id, ["description"] = id, ["depends_on"] = new JsonArray(parents.Select(p => (JsonNode)JsonValue.Create(p)).ToArray()),
                ["script"] = code, ["conditions"] = new JsonArray(condition)
            };
            JsonObject Position(int x, int y, int radius) => new() { ["kind"] = "position", ["count"] = 1, ["x"] = x, ["y"] = y, ["radius"] = radius };
            JsonObject ItemCondition(int type) => new() { ["kind"] = "item", ["type"] = type, ["count"] = 1 };
            string travel = "const a=step('start',()=>tool('action_start',{tool:'nav_to',args:{x:" + goalX + ",y:" + goalY
                + "}}));let q=tool('action_query',{handle:a.handle});if(q.status==='paused')q=tool('action_control',{handle:a.handle,command:'resume'});return {handle:a.handle,status:q.status};";
            var plan = await Call("task_plan", new JsonObject { ["goal"] = "native DAG opportunity handoff", ["mode"] = "dag",
                ["steps"] = new JsonArray(
                    Node("approach", Array.Empty<string>(), "return tool('get_state',{});", Position(originX, floor - 1, 3)),
                    Node("travel", new[] { "approach" }, travel, Position(goalX, goalY, 3)),
                    Node("crystal", new[] { "travel" }, "return tool('gather',{targets:[{item_type:" + ItemID.LifeCrystal
                        + ",count:1,tile_names:['Heart']}],source_area:{x:" + (heartX - 1) + ",y:" + (heartY - 1) + ",width:4,height:4}});", ItemCondition(ItemID.LifeCrystal))) });
            var approach = await Call("task_step", new JsonObject { ["id"] = "approach", ["action"] = "verify" });
            if (approach["verified"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("DAG shared prerequisite not verified: " + approach);
            await Call("watch", new JsonObject { ["events"] = new JsonArray("nearby_chest"), ["passive_events"] = new JsonArray("nearby_chest"), ["radius"] = 60 });
            var started = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["task_node"] = "travel", ["event_delivery"] = "script" });
            string handle = started["value"]?["handle"]?.ToString() ?? throw new Exception("DAG failed to start: " + started);
            var paused = await Call("action_control", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = handle, ["command"] = "pause" });
            if (paused["status"]?.ToString() != "paused") throw new Exception("DAG original action was not paused: " + paused);
            var assessment = await Call("opportunity_assess", new JsonObject { ["x"] = chestX, ["y"] = chestY });
            var beforeInsertion = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
            var inserted = await Call("task_plan_patch", new JsonObject
            {
                ["expected_revision"] = beforeInsertion["task_plan"]["revision"].DeepClone(),
                ["reason"] = "当前箱子邻近起点，先验证真实搜刮所得，再恢复已经暂停的原路线",
                ["add_nodes"] = new JsonArray(Node("chest", new[] { "approach" },
                    "return step('loot',()=>tool('collect_treasure',{x:" + chestX + ",y:" + chestY + "}));", new JsonObject { ["kind"] = "chests_looted", ["count"] = 1 })),
                ["nodes"] = new JsonArray(new JsonObject { ["id"] = "travel", ["depends_on"] = new JsonArray("approach", "chest") })
            });
            var loot = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["task_node"] = "chest", ["event_delivery"] = "script" });
            var chestVerified = await Call("task_step", new JsonObject { ["id"] = "chest", ["action"] = "verify" });
            if (chestVerified["verified"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("DAG chest produced no verified loot: " + loot);
            var resumed = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["task_node"] = "travel", ["event_delivery"] = "script" });
            if (resumed["value"]?["handle"]?.ToString() != handle) throw new Exception("DAG continuation replaced handle: " + resumed);
            JsonObject arrival = null;
            for (int sample = 0; sample < 60; sample++)
            {
                arrival = await Call("action_query", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = handle, ["wait_ms"] = 1000 });
                if (arrival["status"]?.ToString() != "running") break;
            }
            var travelVerified = await Call("task_step", new JsonObject { ["id"] = "travel", ["action"] = "verify" });
            if (travelVerified["verified"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("DAG resumed route did not arrive: " + arrival);
            var crystal = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["task_node"] = "crystal", ["event_delivery"] = "script" });
            var verified = await Call("task_step", new JsonObject { ["id"] = "crystal", ["action"] = "verify" });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "dag-handoff.json"), new JsonObject
            {
                ["initial_plan"] = plan, ["paused"] = paused, ["assessment"] = assessment, ["inserted_plan"] = inserted, ["loot"] = loot,
                ["resumed"] = resumed, ["arrival"] = arrival, ["crystal"] = crystal, ["verified"] = verified,
                ["after"] = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject()), ["elapsed_ms"] = timer.ElapsedMilliseconds,
                ["navigation_stub"] = UsingNavigationStub, ["model_calls"] = 0
            }.ToJsonString());
            if (verified["verified"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true || verified["status"]?.ToString() != "completed")
                throw new Exception("DAG crystal not actually acquired: " + crystal);
            Console.WriteLine($"DAG_HANDOFF_PASS original_handle={handle} actual_loot=true actual_crystal=true navigation_stub={UsingNavigationStub} model_calls=0 elapsed_ms={timer.ElapsedMilliseconds}");
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_ATTACK_LANES_TRIAL") == "1")
        {
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                materialNavigationStub = false;
                var p = Main.LocalPlayer;
                p.inventory[1].SetDefaults(ItemID.Starfury);
                target = NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_Misc("AttackLanesTrial"), (int)p.Center.X + 48, (int)p.Center.Y, NPCID.Hornet);
                Main.npc[target].life = Main.npc[target].lifeMax = 10000;
                Main.npc[target].damage = 0;
                return true;
            });
            var nav = await Call("action_start", new JsonObject { ["tool"] = "nav_to",
                ["args"] = new JsonObject { ["x"] = goalX, ["y"] = goalY } });
            var fight = await Call("action_start", new JsonObject { ["tool"] = "fight",
                ["args"] = new JsonObject { ["npc_types"] = new JsonArray((int)NPCID.Hornet), ["seconds"] = 30d, ["max_dist"] = 60 } });
            if (nav["handle"] == null || fight["handle"] == null) throw new Exception("parallel attack/navigation rejected: " + nav + fight);
            int movingBefore = movingAttackFrames;
            var samples = new JsonArray();
            for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++)
            {
                await Task.Delay(250);
                samples.Add(await OnMain(() => new JsonObject { ["tick"] = Main.GameUpdateCount,
                    ["x"] = Main.LocalPlayer.position.X, ["y"] = Main.LocalPlayer.position.Y,
                    ["attack"] = Main.LocalPlayer.controlUseItem, ["nav"] = RecedingNav.Active,
                    ["moving_attack_frames"] = movingAttackFrames, ["target_hp"] = Main.npc[target].life }));
            }
            var stopped = await Call("action_control", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = fight["handle"].DeepClone(), ["command"] = "cancel" });
            var navigation = await Call("action_query", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = nav["handle"].DeepClone() });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "attack-lanes-native.json"), new JsonObject
                { ["nav"] = nav, ["fight"] = fight, ["samples"] = samples, ["stopped_attack"] = stopped,
                    ["navigation_after"] = navigation, ["moving_attack_frames"] = movingAttackFrames - movingBefore,
                    ["navigation_stub"] = false }.ToJsonString());
            if (movingAttackFrames == movingBefore || navigation["status"]?.ToString() is not ("running" or "completed")
                || stopped["status"]?.ToString() != "cancelled")
                throw new Exception("attack did not overlap movement or its cleanup interrupted navigation");
            await Call("action_control", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = nav["handle"].DeepClone(), ["command"] = "cancel" });
            Console.WriteLine("ATTACK_LANES_PASS moving_attack_frames=" + (movingAttackFrames - movingBefore) + " navigation_stub=false");
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_KNOWLEDGE_PROBE") == "1")
        {
            var sources = await Call("npc_drop_sources", new JsonObject { ["item_type"] = (int)ItemID.Stinger });
            var search = JsonNode.Parse(await AgentTools.Execute("wiki_search", new JsonObject { ["query"] = "Jungle Spores" }, default));
            JsonNode page = null;
            if (search is JsonArray matches && matches.Count > 0)
                page = JsonNode.Parse(await AgentTools.Execute("wiki_page", new JsonObject { ["title"] = matches[0]["title"].ToString() }, default));
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "source-knowledge.json"), new JsonObject
            { ["npc_sources"] = sources, ["wiki_search"] = search, ["wiki_page"] = page, ["model_calls"] = 0 }.ToJsonString());
            if (sources["sources"]?.AsArray().Count is not > 0 || page?["text"] == null)
                throw new Exception("source knowledge query unavailable; inspect source-knowledge.json");
            Console.WriteLine("SOURCE_KNOWLEDGE_PASS model_calls=0");
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_KNOWLEDGE_AGENT") == "1")
            {
                var before = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                var samples = new JsonArray();
                var knowledge = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["workflow"] = "material-knowledge",
                    ["code"] = "step('npc-drop-rules',()=> (" + sources.ToJsonString() + "));step('wiki-source',()=> ("
                        + page.ToJsonString() + "));return {saved:true};" });
                if (knowledge["ok"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("knowledge workflow could not be saved: " + knowledge);
                await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    Config.I.AgentUseCodex = true; Config.I.AgentCodexModel = "gpt-6-luna";
                    NativeAgent.Submit("/goal --tokens 250000 继续丛林材料阶段：为制作荆棘旋刃收集孢子和毒刺。当前先实际新增获得至少1个丛林孢子，并把来源判断、实际所得及后续毒刺来源选择保存到workflow步骤。观察附近有用机会，依据现场决定拿、暂缓或跳过并保存理由，不要仅查询后结束。已有投刀可防身。material-knowledge工作流的npc-drop-rules和wiki-source步骤保存了刚查询的真实来源证据，用script_progress读取；这不是已收集证明，不必重复同一查询。");
                    return true;
                });
                bool started = false;
                try
                {
                    for (int sample = 0; sample < 120; sample++)
                    {
                        await Task.Delay(1000);
                        string agentStatus = NativeAgent.Status;
                        var observed = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                        started |= agentStatus != "queued" && agentStatus != "idle";
                        samples.Add(new JsonObject { ["tick"] = observed["tick"]?.DeepClone(), ["status"] = agentStatus,
                            ["player"] = observed["player"]?.DeepClone(), ["actions"] = observed["action_tasks"]?.DeepClone(),
                            ["codex_goal"] = observed["codex_goal"]?.DeepClone() });
                        string status = observed["codex_goal"]?["native"]?["status"]?.ToString();
                        if (status is "complete" or "budgetLimited" or "usageLimited" || agentStatus == "failed" || started && agentStatus == "idle") break;
                    }
                }
                finally
                {
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "source-knowledge-agent.json"), new JsonObject
                    { ["before"] = before, ["after"] = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject()), ["samples"] = samples,
                        ["scheduler"] = "NativeAgent", ["navigation_stub"] = UsingNavigationStub }.ToJsonString());
                    await OnMain(() => { NativeAgent.Stop(); return true; });
                }
                throw new Exception("bounded material model test ended; inspect actual gains and saved decisions");
            }
            return;
        }
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_REVIEW_PROBE") == "1"
            || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_GLOBAL_REPLAY") == "1"
            || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_REVIEW_AGENT") == "1"
            || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_EXAMPLE_SCRIPT") == "1"
            || Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_VARIANT_SCRIPT") == "1")
        {
            await OnMain(() => { materialNavigationStub = true; return true; });
            var sourceDepthState = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
            await OnMain(() =>
            {
                var depth = sourceDepthState["depth"];
                if (depth?["world_surface_y_tiles"]?.GetValue<double>() != Main.worldSurface
                    || depth?["rock_layer_y_tiles"]?.GetValue<double>() != Main.rockLayer
                    || depth?["underground"]?.GetValue<bool>() != Main.LocalPlayer.ZoneDirtLayerHeight
                    || depth?["cavern"]?.GetValue<bool>() != Main.LocalPlayer.ZoneRockLayerHeight)
                    throw new Exception("source depth observation does not match native world layers");
                return true;
            });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "source-depth-state.json"), sourceDepthState["depth"].ToJsonString());
            var sourceArgs = new JsonObject { ["targets"] = new JsonArray(new JsonObject
                { ["item_type"] = (int)ItemID.JungleSpores, ["count"] = 6,
                    ["tile_names"] = new JsonArray("JungleGrass") }) };
            var sourceAction = await Call("action_start", new JsonObject { ["tool"] = "gather", ["args"] = sourceArgs });
            string sourceHandle = sourceAction["handle"].GetValue<string>();
            JsonObject sourceQuery;
            do
            {
                sourceQuery = await Call("action_query", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = sourceHandle, ["wait_ms"] = 1000 });
            } while (sourceQuery["status"]?.ToString() == "running");
            var sourceReview = sourceQuery["result"].AsObject();
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "source-review-probe.json"), sourceReview.ToJsonString());
            if (sourceReview["ok"]?.GetValue<bool>() != false || sourceReview["outcome"]?.ToString() != "source_needs_review"
                || sourceReview["actions"]?.GetValue<int>() < 3 || sourceReview["targets"][0]["actual"]?.GetValue<int>() != 0
                || sourceReview["source_error"]["observations"]?.AsArray().Count != 3
                || sourceReview["source_error"]["observations"].AsArray().Select(o => o["candidate"]["id"].ToString()).Distinct().Count() != 3
                || sourceReview["source_error"]["observations"].AsArray().Any(o => o["count_after"].GetValue<int>() != o["count_before"].GetValue<int>()))
                throw new Exception("native unproductive harvesting failed to return measured source review after three removals");
            Console.WriteLine($"SOURCE_REVIEW_PROBE_PASS native_removals=3 target_count=0 navigation_stub={UsingNavigationStub} attempts=" + sourceReview["actions"]);
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_GLOBAL_REPLAY") == "1")
            {
                var replay = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["workflow"] = "source-global-replay",
                    ["event_delivery"] = "script", ["code"] = """
                    const handle=HANDLE;
                    const notices=[];
                    tool('watch',{events:['nearby_chest'],passive_events:['nearby_chest'],radius:40});
                    tool('action_control',{handle,command:'resume',args:{targets:[{
                      item_type:331,count:6,tile_names:['JunglePlants'],tile_frame_x:144,tile_frame_y:0}]}});
                    let q;
                    do {
                      q=tool('action_query',{handle,wait_ms:1000});
                      const events=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0}).events;
                      notices.push(...events.map(e=>({kind:e.kind,key:e.key,gained:e.evidence?.gained})));
                      if(events.some(e=>['low_health','player_died','suffocating'].includes(e.kind)))
                        tool('decision_point',{reason:'Source replay interrupted by danger',evidence:{handle,events}});
                    } while(q.status==='running');
                    const actual=tool('get_state').inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===331).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0);
                    return {handle,status:q.status,result:q.result,actual,notices};
                    """.Replace("HANDLE", JsonValue.Create(sourceHandle).ToJsonString()) });
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "source-global-replay.json"), replay.ToJsonString());
                if (replay["value"]?["actual"]?.GetValue<int>() is not >= 6
                    || replay["value"]?["status"]?.ToString() != "completed"
                    || replay["value"]?["handle"]?.ToString() != sourceHandle)
                    throw new Exception("recorded global source selection did not complete the original gather; inspect source-global-replay.json");
                Console.WriteLine($"SOURCE_GLOBAL_REPLAY_PASS actual_spores={replay["value"]["actual"]} original_handle=true navigation_stub={UsingNavigationStub} model_calls=0 recorded_model_selection=true");
                return;
            }
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_EXAMPLE_SCRIPT") == "1")
            {
                var example = await Call("script_program", new JsonObject { ["operation"] = "example", ["name"] = "sample_tile_variants" });
                var config = new JsonObject { ["handle"] = sourceHandle, ["item_type"] = (int)ItemID.JungleSpores,
                    ["count"] = 1, ["tile_name"] = "JunglePlants", ["max_samples"] = 12,
                    ["source_area"] = new JsonObject { ["x"] = 3276, ["y"] = 438, ["width"] = 120, ["height"] = 120 } };
                await Call("watch", new JsonObject { ["events"] = new JsonArray() });
                await Call("script_events", new JsonObject { ["wait_ms"] = 0, ["max_events"] = 16 });
                var trial = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["workflow"] = "source-library-example",
                    ["event_delivery"] = "script", ["code"] = example["code"].ToString()
                        .Replace("const config = null;", "const config = " + config.ToJsonString() + ";") });
                var after = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "source-example-native.json"), new JsonObject
                    { ["config"] = config, ["script"] = trial, ["after"] = after, ["navigation_stub"] = UsingNavigationStub }.ToJsonString());
                if (trial["value"]?["target_met"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true
                    || after["inventory_slots"].AsArray().Where(i => i["type"]?.GetValue<int>() == ItemID.JungleSpores).Sum(i => i["stack"].GetValue<int>()) < 1
                    || after["action_tasks"].AsArray().Count(a => a["tool"]?.ToString() == "gather") != 1
                    || trial["value"]?["handle"]?.ToString() != sourceHandle)
                    throw new Exception("editable example did not verify native gain on original gather handle; inspect source-example-native.json");
                Console.WriteLine($"SOURCE_EXAMPLE_NATIVE_PASS original_handle=true native_inventory=true navigation_stub={UsingNavigationStub} model_calls=0");
                return;
            }
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_SOURCE_VARIANT_SCRIPT") == "1")
            {
                samplingSources = true;
                var variantTrial = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["workflow"] = "source-variant-sampling",
                    ["event_delivery"] = "script", ["code"] = """
                    const handle=HANDLE, item=ITEM, wanted=6, tileName=TILE;
                    const count=()=>tool('get_state').inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===item).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0);
                    const start=step('origin',()=>{const s=tool('get_state');return {x:Math.floor(s.player.pos.x/16),y:Math.floor(s.player.pos.y/16),count:count()};});
                    const area={x:start.x-60,y:start.y-60,width:120,height:120};
                    tool('watch',{events:['nearby_chest'],passive_events:['nearby_chest'],radius:40});
                    const candidates=tool('find_tiles',{name:tileName,n:1,source_area:area}).variants.map(v=>v.nearest);
                    const tried=new Set(), positive=new Set(), decisions=[];
                    const regions=[{...area,x:area.x+120},{...area,y:area.y+120},{...area,x:area.x-120},{...area,y:area.y-120}];
                    let learned=null;
                    let currentCount=count();
                    while(candidates.length||learned&&regions.length){
                      if(currentCount>=wanted) break;
                      if(!candidates.length) {
                        const nextArea=regions.shift();
                        candidates.push(...tool('find_tiles',{name:tileName,n:100,source_area:nextArea,frame_x:learned.frame_x,frame_y:learned.frame_y}).tiles);
                        continue;
                      }
                      const preferred=candidates.findIndex(c=>positive.has(c.tile_type+':'+c.frame_x+':'+c.frame_y));
                      const c=candidates.splice(preferred<0?0:preferred,1)[0];
                      const key=c.tile_type+':'+c.frame_x+':'+c.frame_y;
                      if(tried.has(key)&&!positive.has(key)) continue;
                      const evidence=step('sample-'+c.x+'-'+c.y,()=>{
                        const events=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0}).events;
                        if(events.some(e=>['low_health','player_died','suffocating'].includes(e.kind)))
                          tool('decision_point',{reason:'Unsafe to continue source sampling',evidence:{handle,events}});
                        let assessment=tool('opportunity_assess',{x:c.x,y:c.y});
                        let approachHandle=null;
                        if(assessment.error==='target_out_of_observation_range'&&positive.has(key)) {
                          const approach=tool('action_start',{tool:'nav_to',args:{x:c.x,y:c.y}});
                          approachHandle=approach.handle;
                          if(!approachHandle) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(approach));
                          let arrival;
                          do {
                            arrival=tool('action_query',{handle:approachHandle,wait_ms:1000});
                            if(arrival.status==='paused') tool('decision_point',{reason:'Approach interrupted before source inspection',evidence:{handle,approachHandle,candidate:c,arrival}});
                          } while(arrival.status==='running');
                          if(arrival.status!=='completed') tool('decision_point',{reason:'Approach failed; retain source and original gather',evidence:{handle,approachHandle,candidate:c,arrival}});
                          assessment=tool('opportunity_assess',{x:c.x,y:c.y});
                        }
                        if(!assessment.ok) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Source access remains unknown',evidence:{handle,approachHandle,candidate:c,assessment}});
                        const relevant=(assessment.hazards??[]).filter(h=>h.on_direct_corridor||Math.abs(h.x-c.x)<=1&&Math.abs(h.y-c.y)<=2);
                        if(!assessment.ok||relevant.length)
                          return {key,x:c.x,y:c.y,decision:'skip',reason:assessment.error??'hazard intersects approach or target neighborhood',hazards:relevant};
                        const before=count();
                        const resumed=tool('action_control',{handle,command:'resume',args:{
                          targets:[{item_type:item,count:wanted,tile_names:[tileName],tile_frame_x:c.frame_x,tile_frame_y:c.frame_y}],
                          source_area:{x:c.x-2,y:c.y-2,width:5,height:5}}});
                        if(resumed.error) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(resumed));
                        let q;
                        do {
                          q=tool('action_query',{handle,wait_ms:1000});
                          if(q.status==='paused') tool('decision_point',{reason:'Sample interrupted; inspect before resuming',evidence:{handle,status:q.status}});
                        } while(q.status==='running');
                        if(['unsafe','interrupted_by_death','combat_obstructed'].includes(q.result?.outcome))
                          tool('decision_point',{reason:'Source sampling requires recovery before another candidate',evidence:{handle,result:q.result}});
                        const after=count();
                        return {key,x:c.x,y:c.y,decision:'sample',before,after,gained:after-before,
                          removed:q.result?.last_result?.result?.outcome==='removed',
                          handle:q.handle,outcome:q.result?.outcome,approach:assessment.approach_direct_distance,approach_handle:approachHandle};
                      });
                      decisions.push(evidence);
                      if(evidence.after!==undefined) currentCount=evidence.after;
                      if(evidence.removed) tried.add(key);
                      if(evidence.gained>0&&!positive.has(key)) {
                        positive.add(key);
                        learned=c;
                        const matching=tool('find_tiles',{name:tileName,n:100,source_area:area,frame_x:c.frame_x,frame_y:c.frame_y}).tiles;
                        candidates.splice(0,candidates.length,...matching.filter(t=>!decisions.some(d=>d.x===t.x&&d.y===t.y)));
                      }
                      if(decisions.length>=12) break;
                    }
                    return {handle,before:start.count,after:count(),wanted,positive_variants:[...positive],decisions};
                    """.Replace("HANDLE", JsonValue.Create(sourceHandle).ToJsonString())
                        .Replace("ITEM", ((int)ItemID.JungleSpores).ToString())
                        .Replace("TILE", JsonValue.Create("JunglePlants").ToJsonString()) });
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "source-variant-script.json"), variantTrial.ToJsonString());

                        new[] { "tick,active,outcome,x,y,has_tile,use,animation,item_time,item_type" }.Concat(sourceInputSamples));
                    return true;

                    throw new Exception("variant sampling incomplete; inspect actual inventory, decisions and original handle");

"/goal 从出生点的新人物初始装备开始，优先前往丛林主道入口并沿主道进入地下丛林：先调用find_descent与descent_route（name=jungle）查证主入口和主路线，导航到返回的入口再沿主道下降。不要把find_biome返回的生态边缘当作下行入口，不要在边缘为附近箱子或水晶另凿竖井；宝物仅作为主道沿途短支线，受阻就回主道。目标是地下丛林采集，不要求一路下到地狱。抵达丛林前，沿途只拿无需明显绕路、深挖或长时间施工就能取得的物品；不要为了出生点附近的箱子或水晶延后出发。插入拾取遇到受阻或耗时明显增加时，记录位置并恢复前往丛林，不把开箱作为抵达丛林的前置。生命水晶和丛林孢子越多越好：在探索、开箱、采集和打怪过程中持续留意并收集，不要拿到1颗水晶或凑够荆棘旋刃的孢子就停止关注，也不要把这些最低数量当作收集上限。生命水晶获得后及时使用，提升生命上限。顺路搜刮附近值得拿的箱子，开过一个不代表之后的箱子不再有价值；插入行动后继续原任务，跳过时记录现场依据。从零收集毒刺和孢子，制作一把荆棘旋刃；额外孢子保留用于后续丛林防具，食人怪掉落的藤蔓也值得收集，可与孢子等材料制作丛林头盔、丛林裤腿以提高防御，具体配方和属性自行查询当前游戏。使用DAG组织共享前置与可并行推进的子目标；制作所需材料凑齐只解锁制作，不关闭水晶、额外孢子、藤蔓和箱子的机会观察。越多越好是贯穿本次旅程的收集偏好，不是无限采集才允许完成的前置；结束前检查已发现未处理的机会，报告实际所得及跳过理由。执行遇到失败或新事实时修改方案并继续。最终必须实际提升生命上限并持有荆棘旋刃，开箱核实所得。已额外提供9999木头和星怒，不要砍树，用星怒防身。允许合理利用沿途装备与工作站。请实际行动，不只规划。"
                    : $"/goal --tokens {modelTokenBudget} 前往丛林目标格({goalX},{goalY})。优先到达丛林，途中只拿无需明显绕路、深挖或长时间施工的物品；插入拾取受阻或耗时明显增加时记录位置并恢复主路线，不把开箱设为抵达丛林的前置。生命水晶和丛林孢子越多越好，在导航、开箱、采集和打怪过程中持续收集，不以1颗水晶或荆棘旋刃所需孢子数量为上限；水晶获得后及时使用，提升生命上限。顺路搜刮附近值得拿的箱子和材料，开过一个箱子后继续关注新机会；跳过时记录实际依据，插入行动后继续原任务。收集毒刺和孢子制作一把荆棘旋刃。额外孢子保留用于后续丛林防具，食人怪掉落的藤蔓也值得收集；查询当前丛林头盔、丛林裤腿的配方与属性，为提高防御储备材料。制作条件满足不关闭上述机会观察；越多越好是本次旅程的收集偏好，不是无限采集前置。结束前检查已发现未处理的机会，报告实际所得与跳过理由。已有投刀可防身。请实际执行、验证和恢复失败，不只给方案。";
                if (JungleHealthTrial)
                    trialPrompt = "/goal 给自己搞到300点永久生命上限。硬性要求：生命水晶在地下丛林采集并及时使用，完成时也必须位于地下丛林。先前往丛林地下，赶路期间准备后续采集，持续执行直到实际达到300血并验证。已有星怒可防身，木头足够，不用砍树；本轮有永久鱼鳃和光芒效果。低血量只通知，继续执行。";
                else
                {
                    trialPrompt += " 玩家明确要求：不制作木盔甲，收益不值得为此中断采集；死亡恢复也不要新增木甲准备节点。";
                    if (FullCraftTrial) trialPrompt += " 本轮硬性验收从收到任务开始计时：6分钟内从查证的丛林主入口沿主道开始下降；18分钟内永久生命上限至少200；24分钟内实际制作荆棘旋刃；30分钟内完成全部任务并验收。第二次死亡或任一节点超时直接退出游戏。第一步启动后重点减少每次超过5秒的原地等待，走路期间完成后续查询规划。最终生命条件必须至少200，不是只吃一颗水晶。";
                }
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "trial-start.json"), new JsonObject
                {
                    ["started_utc"] = startedUtc.ToString("O"), ["model"] = trialModel, ["effort"] = "low",
                    ["prompt"] = trialPrompt, ["before"] = before.DeepClone(), ["recipe"] = recipe.DeepClone(),

                    ["objective"] = JungleHealthTrial ? "health_300_collected_and_used_in_underground_jungle" : "craft_trial",
                    ["permanent_gills"] = FullCraftTrial, ["permanent_shine"] = FullCraftTrial,
                    ["world_name"] = Main.worldName, ["world_seed"] = Main.ActiveWorldFileData.SeedText,
                    ["world_width"] = Main.maxTilesX, ["world_height"] = Main.maxTilesY,
                    ["game_version"] = Main.versionNumber, ["assembly_mvid"] = typeof(NativeAgent).Assembly.ManifestModule.ModuleVersionId.ToString(),
                    ["source_revision"] = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_TRIAL_REVISION"),
                    ["source_status"] = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_TRIAL_SOURCE_STATUS"),

                        File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "Mods", "TerraBlind.tmod")))),

                        File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "Worlds", "Checks.wld")))),

                }.ToJsonString());
                if (FullCraftTrial) acceptance = new AcceptanceClock(Main.SavePath, acceptanceRoute);
                NativeAgent.Submit(trialPrompt);
                return true;
            });
            var samples = new JsonArray();
            JsonObject after = null;
            bool started = false;
            bool lootedChest = false;
            var elapsed = System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.StartNew();
            string endReason = "time_limit";
            bool HasResults(JsonObject state) => JungleHealthTrial
                ? acceptance.HealthGoalVerified && state["permanent_max_health"]?.GetValue<int>() >= 300
                    && state["task_plan"]?["status"]?.ToString() == "completed"
                : lootedChest && state["task_plan"]?["mode"]?.ToString() == "dag"
                && state["task_plan"]?["status"]?.ToString() == "completed"
                && (FullCraftTrial ? state["permanent_max_health"]?.GetValue<int>() >= 200
                    : state["player"]?["max_hp"]?.GetValue<int>() > before["player"]?["max_hp"]?.GetValue<int>())
                && state["inventory_slots"].AsArray()
                    .Any(i => i["type"]?.GetValue<int>() == ItemID.ThornChakram && i["stack"]?.GetValue<int>() > 0);
            try
            {
                while (JungleHealthTrial || elapsed.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < (FullCraftTrial ? 1800 : 120))
                {
                    await Task.Delay(1000);
                    string agentStatus = NativeAgent.Status;
                    after = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                    lootedChest |= after["action_tasks"].AsArray().Any(a => a["tool"]?.ToString() == "collect_treasure"
                        && a["status"]?.ToString() == "completed" && a["result"]?["got"] is JsonObject got && got.Count > 0);
                    started |= agentStatus != "queued" && agentStatus != "idle";
                    var sample = new JsonObject { ["tick"] = after["player"]?["tick"]?.DeepClone(), ["status"] = agentStatus,
                        ["navigation_stub"] = UsingNavigationStub,
                        ["idle_defense_active"] = after["idle_defense_active"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["player"] = after["player"]?.DeepClone(), ["task_plan"] = after["task_plan"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["actions"] = after["action_tasks"]?.DeepClone(), ["codex_goal"] = after["codex_goal"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["inventory"] = after["inventory_slots"]?.DeepClone(), ["elapsed_ms"] = elapsed.ElapsedMilliseconds,
                        ["utc"] = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("O"),
                        ["equipment"] = after["equipment_slots"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["last_damage"] = after["last_damage"]?.DeepClone(), ["last_death"] = after["last_death"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["last_automatic_heal"] = after["last_automatic_heal"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["contact_escape"] = after["contact_escape"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["health_dynamics"] = after["health_dynamics"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["watch"] = after["watch"]?.DeepClone(), ["recovery"] = after["recovery"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["script_workflows"] = after["script_workflows"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["physiology"] = await OnMain(() => new JsonObject
                        {
                            ["tick"] = (long)Main.GameUpdateCount, ["breath"] = Main.LocalPlayer.breath,
                            ["breath_max"] = Main.LocalPlayer.breathMax, ["gills"] = Main.LocalPlayer.gills,
                            ["no_fall_damage"] = Main.LocalPlayer.noFallDmg, ["fall_start"] = Main.LocalPlayer.fallStart,
                            ["selected_item"] = Main.LocalPlayer.HeldItem.type,
                            ["buffs"] = new JsonArray(Main.LocalPlayer.buffType.Select((type, index) => (type, index))
                                .Where(b => b.type > 0).Select(b => (JsonNode)new JsonObject
                                    { ["type"] = b.type, ["ticks"] = Main.LocalPlayer.buffTime[b.index] }).ToArray())
                        }) };
                    samples.Add(sample);
                    journal.WriteLine(sample.ToJsonString());
                    string status = after["codex_goal"]?["native"]?["status"]?.ToString();
                    if (FullCraftTrial && HasResults(after)) { acceptance.Finish(true); endReason = "verified_results"; break; }
                    if (status is "complete" or "budgetLimited" or "usageLimited" or "paused" || agentStatus == "failed" || started && agentStatus == "idle")
                    { endReason = "agent_terminal:" + status + ":" + agentStatus; break; }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception error) { endReason = "exception:" + error.Message; throw; }
            finally
            {
                after = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "opportunity-agent-live.json"), new JsonObject
                { ["before"] = before, ["samples"] = samples, ["after"] = after,
                    ["navigation_stub"] = UsingNavigationStub, ["spawned_hornets"] = hornetsSpawned,
                    ["natural_encounters"] = NaturalEncounters,
                    ["model"] = trialModel,
                    ["prompt"] = trialPrompt, ["started_utc"] = startedUtc.ToString("O"),
                    ["ended_utc"] = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("O"), ["end_reason"] = endReason,
                    ["model_token_budget"] = FullCraftTrial ? null : JsonValue.Create(modelTokenBudget),
                    ["elapsed_ms"] = elapsed.ElapsedMilliseconds, ["looted_chest"] = lootedChest,
                    ["full_craft_trial"] = FullCraftTrial }.ToJsonString());
                await OnMain(() => { collectingStingers = false; NativeAgent.Stop(); return true; });
            }
            if (FullCraftTrial && HasResults(after))
            {
                Console.WriteLine((JungleHealthTrial ? "JUNGLE_HEALTH_PASS health=300 native_underground_jungle=true navigation_stub=false elapsed_ms="
                    : "FULL_CRAFT_PASS native_inventory=true navigation_stub=false dag=true chest=true crystal=true elapsed_ms=") + elapsed.ElapsedMilliseconds);
                return;
            }
            throw new Exception("bounded production agent trial ended; inspect actual gains, choices and preserved handles before acceptance");
        }
        string code = """
            const goal={x:GOAL_X,y:GOAL_Y};
            tool('watch',{events:['nearby_chest','nearby_tile'],passive_events:['nearby_tile'],tile_types:[12,61,16],radius:60});
            const original=step('original',()=>tool('action_start',{tool:'nav_to',args:goal}));
            const notice=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:1000});
            tool('action_control',{handle:original.handle,command:'pause'});
            if(!notice.events.some(e=>e.kind==='nearby_chest')||!notice.events.some(e=>e.kind==='nearby_tile'&&e.evidence.tile_type===12))
              throw new Error('native chest/crystal not observed');
            const decisions=[];
            const candidates=new Map();
            for(const e of notice.events) {
              const t=e.evidence;
              if(e.kind!=='nearby_chest'&&!(e.kind==='nearby_tile'&&t.tile_type===12)) continue;
              const kind=e.kind==='nearby_chest'?'chest':'crystal';
              const x=t.x-(kind==='crystal'?(t.frame_x%36)/18:0);
              const y=t.y-(kind==='crystal'?(t.frame_y%36)/18:0);
              candidates.set(kind+':'+x+','+y,{kind,x,y});
            }
            const selected={};
            const position=tool('get_state').player.pos;
            const ordered=[...candidates.values()].sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-position.x/16,a.y-position.y/16)-Math.hypot(b.x-position.x/16,b.y-position.y/16));
            for(const point of ordered) {
              let decision='defer', reason='one target of this kind already selected for this bounded detour', evidence=null;
              {
                const facts=tool('opportunity_assess',{x:point.x,y:point.y,destination:goal});
                evidence={error:facts.error||null,distance:facts.approach_direct_distance,route_relation:facts.route_relation,
                  solid_cells:facts.direct_corridor_solid_cells?.length,hazards:facts.hazards};
                if(facts.error) {decision='skip';reason='target not assessed: '+facts.error;}
                else if(facts.hazards.some(h=>h.kind==='lava'||h.kind==='trap_tile'||h.kind==='falling_block'&&h.on_direct_corridor)) {
                  decision='skip';reason='measured lava/trap or falling block on approach';
                } else if(facts.route_relation.extra_direct_distance>20) {decision='skip';reason='geometric detour exceeds this script policy of 20 tiles; actual route cost remains unknown';}
                else if(!selected[point.kind]) {decision='collect';reason='needed target within detour budget; reachability and gain still require execution';selected[point.kind]=point;}
              }
              decisions.push(step('choice:'+point.kind+':'+point.x+','+point.y,()=>({point,decision,reason,evidence})));
            }
            if(!selected.chest||!selected.crystal) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Observed candidates did not provide both acceptable targets; review recorded choices and preserve original navigation'});
            function count(type){return tool('get_state').inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===type).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0);}
            const beforeCrystal=count(29);
            const loot=tool('collect_treasure',{x:selected.chest.x,y:selected.chest.y});
            const lootGained=Object.values(loot.got||{}).reduce((n,v)=>n+v,0);
            if(lootGained<=0) throw new Error('natural chest not looted: '+JSON.stringify(loot));
            let gather=tool('gather',{targets:[{item_type:29,count:beforeCrystal+1,tile_names:['Heart']}],source_area:{x:selected.crystal.x,y:selected.crystal.y,width:3,height:3}});
            for(let i=0;i<30&&gather.handle;i++) {
              const events=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
              if(events.events.some(e=>['player_died','low_health','suffocating'].includes(e.kind))) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Danger during natural gathering'});
              const current=tool('action_query',{handle:gather.handle,wait_ms:1000});
              if(current.status==='completed'){gather=current;break;}
              if(current.status==='paused') tool('action_control',{handle:gather.handle,command:'resume'});
              else if(current.status!=='running') throw new Error(JSON.stringify(current));
            }
            if(count(29)<=beforeCrystal) throw new Error('natural crystal not acquired: '+JSON.stringify(gather));
            tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
            tool('action_control',{handle:original.handle,command:'resume'});
            let restored;
            for(let i=0;i<30;i++) {
              restored=tool('action_query',{handle:original.handle,wait_ms:1000});
              if(restored.status!=='running') break;
            }
            if(restored.status!=='completed'||restored.result.done!==true) throw new Error('natural original goal not reached: '+JSON.stringify(restored));
            return {decisions,original_handle:original.handle,loot_gained:lootGained,crystals_gained:count(29)-beforeCrystal,restored};
            """.Replace("GOAL_X", goalX.ToString()).Replace("GOAL_Y", goalY.ToString());
        var result = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["code"] = code, ["workflow"] = "natural-opportunity-trial", ["event_delivery"] = "script" });
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "opportunity-result.json"), result.ToJsonString());
        if (result["ok"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true || result["value"]?["crystals_gained"]?.GetValue<int>() != 1)
            throw new Exception("natural opportunity continuation incomplete: " + result["detail"] + " " + result["value"]);
        if (!result["value"]["decisions"].AsArray().Any(d => d["decision"]?.ToString() == "skip"
            && d["evidence"] != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(d["reason"]?.ToString())))
            throw new Exception("natural opportunity trial did not exercise an evidence-based skip");
        Console.WriteLine("OPPORTUNITY_NATURAL_COMPLETE native_engine=true model_calls=0 fixture_tile_edits=0 teleports_after_start=0");
        var loadout = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["code"] = """
            const state=tool('get_state');
            const weapons=[];
            for(const item of state.inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type>0)) {
              const info=tool('item_info',{slot:item.slot});
              if(info.damage>0&&info.pick===0&&info.axe===0&&info.hammer===0)
                weapons.push({slot:item.slot,type:item.type,useAmmo:info.useAmmo,hasAmmo:info.hasAmmo,
                  shoot:info.shoot,shootSpeed:info.shootSpeed,useTime:info.useTime,useAnimation:info.useAnimation});
            }
            const knives=weapons.find(w=>w.type===279);
            const gun=weapons.find(w=>w.type===964);
            if(!knives||knives.useAmmo!==0||knives.hasAmmo!==true||knives.shoot<=0||knives.shootSpeed<=0||knives.useTime<=0)
              throw new Error('throwing weapon facts missing');
            if(gun&&(gun.useAmmo<=0||gun.hasAmmo!==false)) throw new Error('empty looted gun reported usable ammo');
            return {weapons,empty_gun_checked:!!gun};
            """ });
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "weapon-facts.json"), loadout.ToJsonString());
        if (loadout["ok"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("weapon facts failed: " + loadout.ToJsonString());
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_OPPORTUNITY_MATERIALS") == "1")
        {
            await OnMain(() => { materialNavigationStub = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MATERIAL_NAV_STUB") == "1"; return true; });
            var recipe = await Call("recipe", new JsonObject { ["name"] = "ThornChakram" });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "material-recipe.json"), recipe.ToJsonString());
            string materials = """
                const recipe=tool('recipe',{name:'ThornChakram'}).recipes.find(r=>r.internal==='ThornChakram');
                if(!recipe) throw new Error('crafting recipe unavailable');
                function itemType(name){
                  const found=tool('game_catalog',{kind:'item',name}).matches.filter(m=>m.internal===name);
                  if(found.length!==1) throw new Error('ambiguous recipe ingredient: '+name);
                  return found[0].type;
                }
                step('recipe-plan',()=>tool('task_plan',{goal:'Collect recipe materials and craft Thorn Chakram after opportunistic loot',steps:[
                  {id:'materials',description:'Acquire all materials required by the loaded recipe',
                    conditions:recipe.ingredients.map(i=>({kind:'item',type:itemType(i.internal),count:i.need}))},
                  {id:'crafted',description:'Verify the crafted weapon in inventory',
                    conditions:[{kind:'item',type:itemType(recipe.internal),count:1}]}]}));
                tool('watch',{events:['nearby_chest','nearby_tile','nearby_drop'],passive_events:['nearby_chest','nearby_tile','nearby_drop'],tile_types:[12,61,16],radius:40});
                const state=tool('get_state');
                const x=Math.floor(state.player.pos.x/16), y=Math.floor(state.player.pos.y/16);
                const before=step('spore-baseline',()=>state.inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===331).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0));
                const required=recipe.ingredients.find(i=>i.internal==='JungleSpores').need;
                tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
                const sourceTile=tool('game_catalog',{kind:'tile',name:'61'}).matches.find(m=>m.type===61).internal;
                const sources=[];
                for(const dx of [-120,0,120]) for(const dy of [-120,0,120]) {
                  const query=tool('find_tiles',{name:sourceTile,n:10,frame_x:144,source_area:{x:x+dx-60,y:y+dy-60,width:120,height:120}});
                  if(query.error) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(query));
                  sources.push(...query.tiles);
                }
                let after=state.inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===331).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0);
                const collected=[];
                const visited=new Set();
                for(let round=0;round<6&&after<required;round++) {
                  const live=tool('get_state');
                  const px=live.player.pos.x/16,py=live.player.pos.y/16;
                  sources.sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-px,a.y-py)-Math.hypot(b.x-px,b.y-py));
                  const selection=step('source:'+round,()=>{
                    const source=sources.find(s=>!visited.has(s.x+','+s.y));
                    if(!source) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Searched material sources exhausted before recipe quantity; retain crafting goal'});
                    return {source,before:after};
                  });
                  const source=selection.source, sourceBefore=selection.before;
                  visited.add(source.x+','+source.y);
                  const pending=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
                  if(pending.events.some(e=>['player_died','low_health','suffocating'].includes(e.kind)))
                    tool('decision_point',{reason:'Danger before starting the next material source'});
                  const started=step('source-action:'+round,()=>tool('action_start',{tool:'gather',args:{targets:[{item_type:331,count:sourceBefore+1,tile_names:[sourceTile],tile_frame_x:144}],source_area:{x:source.x-1,y:source.y-1,width:3,height:3}}}));
                  let result=tool('action_query',{handle:started.handle});
                  if((result.events??[]).some(e=>['player_died','low_health','suffocating'].includes(e.kind)))
                    tool('decision_point',{reason:'Danger at the selected material source'});
                  for(let i=0;i<30&&result.status!=='completed'&&result.status!=='failed';i++) {
                    const events=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
                    if(events.events.some(e=>['player_died','low_health','suffocating'].includes(e.kind))) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Danger while collecting the next recipe material'});
                    const current=tool('action_query',{handle:result.handle,wait_ms:1000});
                    result=current;
                    if(current.status==='completed'||current.status==='failed'){result=current;break;}
                    if(current.status==='paused') tool('action_control',{handle:result.handle,command:'resume'});
                    else if(current.status!=='running') throw new Error(JSON.stringify(current));
                  }
                  if(result.handle&&result.status!=='completed'&&result.status!=='failed')
                    tool('decision_point',{reason:'Material action still pending; inspect and continue its preserved handle rather than declaring the source failed'});
                  after=tool('get_state').inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===331).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0);
                  collected.push(step('material:'+source.x+','+source.y,()=>({source,before:sourceBefore,after,gained:after-sourceBefore})));
                  if(after<=sourceBefore) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Selected source produced no verified gain; inspect obstruction before retrying'});
                }
                return {before,after,gained:after-before,required,fulfilled:after>=required,collected};
                """;
            JsonObject progress = null;
            for (int segment = 0; segment < 3; segment++)
            {
                progress = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["code"] = materials,
                    ["workflow"] = "material-continuation", ["event_delivery"] = "script" });
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, $"material-segment-{segment}.json"), progress.ToJsonString());
                if (progress["value"]?["wake"]?["event"]?["reason"]?.ToString()
                    != "Material action still pending; inspect and continue its preserved handle rather than declaring the source failed") break;
            }
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "material-result.json"), progress.ToJsonString());
            if (progress["value"]?["fulfilled"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true)
                throw new Exception("recipe spore quantity not fulfilled; inspect material-result.json");
            Console.WriteLine("OPPORTUNITY_MATERIAL_PROGRESS verified_recipe_spores=true");
            if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_OPPORTUNITY_STINGERS") == "1")
            {
                var preparation = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["workflow"] = "material-equipment", ["event_delivery"] = "script",
                    ["code"] = """
                        const planBefore=tool('get_state').task_plan;
                        function resolve(name){
                          const matches=tool('game_catalog',{kind:'item',name}).matches.filter(i=>i.internal===name);
                          if(matches.length!==1) throw new Error('ambiguous equipment item: '+name);
                          return matches[0].type;
                        }
                        function inventory(type){return tool('get_state').inventory_slots.find(i=>i.type===type);}
                        function requireResult(result){if(result.error||result.ok===false) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(result));return result;}
                        const benchTile=tool('game_catalog',{kind:'tile',name:'WorkBenches'}).matches.find(t=>t.internal==='WorkBenches').type;
                        tool('watch',{events:['nearby_tile'],passive_events:['nearby_tile'],tile_types:[benchTile],radius:12});
                        tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
                        const stations=tool('find_tiles',{name:'WorkBenches',max_dist:60,n:5}).tiles;
                        let destination=null;
                        for(const station of stations) {
                          const facts=tool('opportunity_assess',{x:station.x,y:station.y});
                          if(!facts.hazards.length){destination={x:station.x,y:station.y,reach:true};break;}
                        }
                        if(!destination) destination=tool('get_state').action_tasks.find(a=>a.tool==='nav_to'&&a.status==='completed')?.args;
                        if(destination) {
                          const arrived=tool('nav_to',destination);
                          if(arrived.done!==true) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Workstation approach interrupted; retain navigation handle and material plan'});
                        }
                        let noticed=false;
                        const ready=tool('recipe',{name:'WoodHelmet'}).recipes.some(r=>r.internal==='WoodHelmet'&&r.can_craft_now);
                        if(!ready) {
                        const benchType=resolve('WorkBench');
                        const benchRecipe=tool('recipe',{name:'WorkBench'}).recipes.find(r=>r.internal==='WorkBench');
                        if(!benchRecipe?.can_craft_now) tool('decision_point',{reason:'No available workbench recipe; retain material plan and find a station'});
                        requireResult(tool('craft',{name:'WorkBench'}));
                        const bench=inventory(benchType);
                        if(!bench) throw new Error('crafted workbench missing from inventory');
                        const benchInfo=tool('item_info',{slot:bench.slot});
                        tool('watch',{events:['nearby_tile'],passive_events:['nearby_tile'],tile_types:[benchInfo.createTile],radius:12});
                        let placed=requireResult(tool('place_at',{item:String(bench.type)}));
                        if(placed.outcome!=='done') {
                          const terrain=tool('inspect_terrain',{radius:12}), candidates=[];
                          for(let y=0;y<terrain.rows.length-1;y++) for(let x=0;x<terrain.rows[y].length-1;x++) {
                            if(terrain.rows[y].slice(x,x+2)!=='..'||terrain.rows[y+1].slice(x,x+2)!=='##') continue;
                            const wx=terrain.origin.x+x,wy=terrain.origin.y+y,b=terrain.player_bounds;
                            if(wx<=b.right&&wx+1>=b.left&&wy>=b.top&&wy<=b.bottom) continue;
                            const shaped=terrain.shaped_tiles.filter(t=>t.y===wy+1&&t.x>=wx&&t.x<=wx+1);
                            candidates.push({x:wx,y:wy,d:Math.abs(wx-b.left)+Math.abs(wy-b.bottom),shaped});
                          }
                          candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.shaped.length-b.shaped.length||a.d-b.d);
                          for(const location of candidates.slice(0,3)) {
                            const facts=tool('opportunity_assess',{x:location.x,y:location.y});
                            if(facts.hazards.length) continue;
                            for(const support of location.shaped) {
                              tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
                              const approach=tool('nav_to',{x:support.x,y:support.y,reach:true});
                              if(approach.done!==true) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Support repair approach not completed; inspect preserved navigation before mining'});
                              let pick=null;
                              for(const item of tool('get_state').inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type>0)) {
                                const info=tool('item_info',{slot:item.slot});
                                if(info.pick>0&&(!pick||info.pick>pick.power)) pick={slot:item.slot,power:info.pick};
                              }
                              if(!pick) tool('decision_point',{reason:'No pickaxe for measured support repair'});
                              const mined=tool('use_item',{slot:pick.slot,x:support.x,y:support.y,strict:true,duration_ticks:0});
                              if(mined.outcome!=='removed') tool('decision_point',{reason:'Single support tile removal not verified: '+JSON.stringify(mined)});
                              const material=inventory(resolve('Wood'));
                              if(!material) tool('decision_point',{reason:'No full support material; preserve workstation preparation evidence'});
                              const foundation=tool('place_at',{item:String(material.type),x:support.x,y:support.y});
                              if(foundation.outcome!=='done') tool('decision_point',{reason:'Replacement support not verified: '+JSON.stringify(foundation)});
                            }
                            placed=tool('place_at',{item:String(bench.type),x:location.x,y:location.y});
                            if(placed.outcome==='done') break;
                          }
                          step('workstation-placement',()=>({terrain,candidates,result:placed}));
                        }
                        if(placed.outcome!=='done') tool('decision_point',{reason:'Measured workstation candidates failed; preserve bench and material plan'});
                        }
                        noticed=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:500}).events.some(e=>e.kind==='nearby_tile'&&e.evidence.tile_type===benchTile);
                        if(!noticed) throw new Error('placed workstation was not observed');
                        const equipped=[];
                        for(const [name,slot] of [['WoodHelmet',0],['WoodBreastplate',1],['WoodGreaves',2]]) {
                          const type=resolve(name);
                          const recipe=tool('recipe',{name}).recipes.find(r=>r.internal===name);
                          if(!recipe?.can_craft_now) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Equipment recipe unavailable at current station: '+name});
                          requireResult(tool('craft',{name}));
                          const item=inventory(type), previous=tool('equipment_list').find(i=>i.slot===slot);
                          if(!item) throw new Error('crafted equipment missing: '+name);
                          requireResult(tool('equip_item',{inventory_slot:item.slot,equipment_slot:slot,expected_inventory:item,expected_equipment:previous}));
                          const actual=tool('equipment_list').find(i=>i.slot===slot);
                          if(actual.type!==type) throw new Error('equipment not actually worn: '+name);
                          equipped.push({slot,type});
                        }
                        const planAfter=tool('get_state').task_plan;
                        if(planBefore.goal!==planAfter.goal||planAfter.current_step!=='materials') throw new Error('equipment preparation replaced material goal');
                        return {workstation_observed:noticed,equipped,original_plan_preserved:true};
                        """ });
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "material-equipment.json"), preparation.ToJsonString());
                if (preparation["value"]?["original_plan_preserved"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true)
                    throw new Exception("material equipment preparation incomplete; inspect material-equipment.json");
                await OnMain(() =>
                {
                    stingersRequired = recipe["recipes"].AsArray().First(r => r["internal"]?.ToString() == "ThornChakram")
                        ["ingredients"].AsArray().First(i => i["internal"]?.ToString() == "Stinger")["need"].GetValue<int>();
                    collectingStingers = true;
                    if (!SpawnMaterialHornet()) throw new Exception("no clear native encounter position; terrain was not modified");
                    return true;
                });
                var combat = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["workflow"] = "stinger-continuation", ["event_delivery"] = "script",
                    ["code"] = """
                        function count(){return tool('get_state').inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===209).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0);}
                        function review(events){
                          if((events??[]).some(e=>['player_died','low_health','suffocating'].includes(e.kind)))
                            tool('decision_point',{reason:'Health or terrain danger during material combat; preserve collected resources and review before resuming'});
                        }
                        const before=step('stinger-baseline',()=>count());
                        const required=tool('recipe',{name:'ThornChakram'}).recipes.find(r=>r.internal==='ThornChakram').ingredients.find(i=>i.internal==='Stinger').need;
                        tool('watch',{events:['nearby_chest','nearby_tile','nearby_drop','target_acquired'],passive_events:['nearby_chest','nearby_tile','nearby_drop','target_acquired'],tile_types:[12,61,16],radius:40});
                        tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
                        const attempts=[];
                        const started=step('stinger-action',()=>tool('action_start',{tool:'gather',args:{targets:[{item_type:209,count:required,npc_types:[NPC_TYPE]}]}}));
                        if(!started.handle) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(started));
                        for(let round=0;round<16&&count()<required;round++) {
                          review(tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0}).events);
                          const action=tool('action_query',{handle:started.handle,wait_ms:1000});
                          review(action.events);
                          attempts.push({handle:action.handle,status:action.status,error:action.error,outcome:action.result?.outcome??action.outcome});
                          if(['combat_obstructed','combat_out_of_range'].includes(action.result?.outcome??action.outcome))
                            tool('decision_point',{reason:'Material combat requires a changed approach before resuming the same handle',
                              evidence:{handle:started.handle,status:action.status,outcome:action.result?.outcome,
                                last_result:action.result?.last_result??action.last_result}});
                          if((action.result?.outcome??action.outcome)==='source_unavailable')
                            tool('decision_point',{reason:'Material source exhausted; preserve handle '+(action.handle??action.managed_handle)+' and inspect fresh sources before continuing'});
                          if(action.error==='repeated_failure_blocked')
                            tool('decision_point',{reason:'Repeated material acquisition failure requires a different source or plan'});
                          if(action.status==='failed')
                            tool('decision_point',{reason:'Material action failed; preserve handle '+action.handle+' and inspect failure rather than starting another gather'});
                          if(action.status==='paused') tool('action_control',{handle:started.handle,command:'resume'});
                          if(action.status==='completed') break;
                        }
                        if(count()<required) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Material collection still incomplete; inspect and continue preserved handle '+started.handle});
                        return {before,after:count(),gained:count()-before,attempts,recipe:tool('recipe',{name:'ThornChakram'})};
                        """.Replace("NPC_TYPE", NPCID.Hornet.ToString()) });
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "stinger-first-segment.json"), combat.ToJsonString());
                var materialActions = (await Call("get_state", new JsonObject()))["action_tasks"].AsArray()
                    .Where(a => a["tool"]?.ToString() == "gather"
                        && a["args"]?["targets"]?.AsArray().Any(t => t["item_type"]?.GetValue<int>() == 209) == true).ToArray();
                if (materialActions.Length > 1)
                    throw new Exception("material trial duplicated the original gather instead of preserving its handle");
                if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MATERIAL_AGENT_LIVE") == "1")
                {
                    int tokenBudget = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MATERIAL_TOKEN_BUDGET") is string configuredBudget
                        ? int.Parse(configuredBudget) : 150000;
                    if (tokenBudget <= 0) throw new ArgumentException("material model token budget must be positive");
                    string originalHandle = materialActions.Single()["handle"].ToString();
                    var original = await Call("action_query", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = originalHandle });
                    if (original["status"]?.ToString() == "running")
                        await Call("action_control", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = originalHandle, ["command"] = "pause" });
                    var beforeRecovery = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                    var samples = new JsonArray();
                    try
                    {
                        await OnMain(() =>
                        {
                            Config.I.AgentUseCodex = true; Config.I.AgentCodexModel = "gpt-6-luna";
                            NativeAgent.Submit($"/goal --tokens {tokenBudget} 继续现有荆棘旋刃材料任务。之前顺路开箱、收集生命水晶的进度保留，水晶先别使用。"
                                + "原毒刺采集句柄是" + originalHandle + "，工作流是stinger-continuation。不要重建计划或清空步骤。"
                                + "根据当前健康、地形和附近来源决定恢复方案，允许直接执行工具与JS，不只是保存代码。"
                                + "沿途值得拿的箱子、水晶、材料和工作站要考虑；用实际代价决定插入或跳过并记录依据。"
                                + "处理后恢复同一采集句柄，验证实际材料和合成结果。已有投刀可防身。");
                            return true;
                        });
                        for (int sample = 0; sample < 60; sample++)
                        {
                            await Task.Delay(1000);
                            var state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                            samples.Add(new JsonObject { ["tick"] = state["tick"]?.DeepClone(), ["status"] = NativeAgent.Status,
                                ["player"] = state["player"]?.DeepClone(), ["task_plan"] = state["task_plan"]?.DeepClone(),
                                ["actions"] = state["action_tasks"]?.DeepClone(), ["codex_goal"] = state["codex_goal"]?.DeepClone() });
                            if (state["codex_goal"]?["native"]?["status"]?.ToString() is "complete" or "budgetLimited" or "usageLimited") break;
                        }
                    }
                    finally
                    {
                        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "material-agent-live.json"), new JsonObject
                        { ["before"] = beforeRecovery, ["original_handle"] = originalHandle, ["samples"] = samples,
                            ["after"] = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject()), ["navigation_stub"] = UsingNavigationStub,
                            ["model_token_budget"] = tokenBudget }.ToJsonString());
                        await OnMain(() => { collectingStingers = false; NativeAgent.Stop(); return true; });
                    }
                    throw new Exception("bounded production material recovery ended; inspect verified effects and preserved original handle");
                }
                if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MATERIAL_CONTINUED_DEFENSE") == "1")
                {
                    var state = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                    var original = state["action_tasks"].AsArray().First(a => a["tool"]?.ToString() == "gather"
                        && a["args"]?["targets"]?.AsArray().Any(t => t["item_type"]?.GetValue<int>() == 209) == true);
                    var handoff = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["event_delivery"] = "script", ["code"] = """
                        const guard=tool('action_start',{tool:'fight',args:{max_dist:25,seconds:15,movement:'auto'}});
                        if(guard.error||!guard.handle) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(guard));
                        tool('decision_point',{reason:'Keep short defense during recovery observation',continue_action:guard.handle});
                        """ });
                    string guardHandle = handoff["value"]?["wake"]?["continue_action"]?.ToString()
                        ?? throw new Exception("defense review did not retain an explicit handle");
                    var samples = new JsonArray();
                    for (int sample = 0; sample < 4; sample++)
                    {
                        await Task.Delay(500);
                        var current = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                        var guard = current["action_tasks"].AsArray().First(a => a["handle"]?.ToString() == guardHandle);
                        samples.Add(new JsonObject { ["tick"] = current["tick"]?.DeepClone(), ["hp"] = current["player"]?["hp"]?.DeepClone(),
                            ["pos"] = current["player"]?["pos"]?.DeepClone(), ["status"] = guard["status"]?.DeepClone(),
                            ["fight_active"] = await OnMain(() => FightCoordinator.IsActive),
                            ["knives"] = current["inventory_slots"].AsArray().Where(i => i["type"]?.GetValue<int>() == ItemID.ThrowingKnife).Sum(i => i["stack"].GetValue<int>()) });
                    }
                    var cancelled = await Call("action_control", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = guardHandle, ["command"] = "cancel" });
                    var retained = await Call("action_query", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = original["handle"].DeepClone() });
                    var sources = await Call("gather_observe", original["args"].AsObject());
                    JsonObject resumed = null;
                    if (sources["candidates"]?.AsArray().Any(c => c["kind"]?.ToString() == "npc" && c["clear_line"]?.GetValue<bool>() == true) == true)
                    {
                        resumed = await Call("action_control", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = original["handle"].DeepClone(), ["command"] = "resume" });
                        await Task.Delay(1000);
                        await Call("action_control", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = original["handle"].DeepClone(), ["command"] = "pause" });
                    }
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "continued-defense.json"), new JsonObject
                    { ["original"] = original.DeepClone(), ["samples"] = samples, ["cancelled"] = cancelled, ["retained"] = retained,
                        ["sources"] = sources, ["resumed"] = resumed, ["after"] = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject()) }.ToJsonString());
                    if (samples.Any(s => s["status"]?.ToString() != "running" || s["fight_active"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true)
                        || cancelled["status"]?.ToString() != "cancelled" || retained["args"].ToJsonString() != original["args"].ToJsonString())
                        throw new Exception("continued defense lost its active control or original collection intent");
                    Console.WriteLine($"CONTINUED_DEFENSE_VERIFIED samples={samples.Count} resumed={resumed != null} model_calls=0");
                    throw new Exception("continued defense control verified; material quantity and full acceptance remain unverified");
                }
                if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MATERIAL_MODEL_REVIEW") == "1")
                {
                    if (FrameLimit - frames < ReviewExecutionFrames)
                        throw new Exception("insufficient scene budget for model review and execution; model not called");
                    using var modelSession = new CodexAgent();
                    JsonObject defense = null;
                    if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MATERIAL_MODEL_GUARD") == "1")
                    {
                        defense = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["event_delivery"] = "script", ["code"] = """
                            const guard=tool('action_start',{tool:'fight',args:{max_dist:25,seconds:45,movement:'auto'}});
                            if(guard.error||!guard.handle) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(guard));
                            tool('decision_point',{reason:'Continue bounded defense while reviewing material recovery',continue_action:guard.handle});
