                        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "material-review-defense.json"), defense.ToJsonString());
                    }
                    var reviewed = await Review(new JsonObject { ["workflow"] = "stinger-continuation",
                        ["continued_defense"] = defense?["value"]?["wake"]?["continue_action"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["reason"] = combat["value"]?["wake"]?["event"]?["reason"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["decision"] = combat["value"]?["wake"]?["event"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["status"] = combat["value"]?["status"]?.DeepClone(),
                        ["state"] = combat["state"]?.DeepClone() }, materials: true, session: modelSession);
                    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "material-reviewed-program.json"), reviewed.ToJsonString());
                    if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MATERIAL_MODEL_EXECUTE") == "1")
                    {
                        int segments = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_MATERIAL_MODEL_REPAIR") == "1" ? 2 : 1;
                        for (int segment = 0; segment < segments; segment++)
                        {
                        if (reviewed["found"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true) throw new Exception("model did not save a runnable recovery program");
                        var before = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                        var execution = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["program"] = "material_recovery",
                            ["expected_revision"] = reviewed["revision"].DeepClone(), ["workflow"] = "stinger-continuation",
                            ["event_delivery"] = "script", ["seconds"] = 20 });
                        var after = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                        var background = new JsonArray();
                        bool yielded = execution["value"]?["status"]?.ToString() == "yield";
                        bool Interrupted(JsonObject state)
                        {
                            var passive = state["watch"]["passive_events"].AsArray().Select(e => e.ToString()).ToHashSet();
                            return state["watch"]["pending"].AsArray().Any(e => !passive.Contains(e["kind"].ToString()));
                        }
                        string running = after["action_tasks"].AsArray()
                            .FirstOrDefault(a => a["status"]?.ToString() == "running")?["handle"]?.ToString();
                        if (running != null && !yielded)
                            for (int sample = 0; sample < 20 && !Interrupted(after); sample++)
                            {
                                var observation = await Call("action_query", new JsonObject { ["handle"] = running, ["wait_ms"] = 1000 });
                                background.Add(observation);
                                after = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                                if (observation["status"]?.ToString() != "running") break;
                            }
                        after = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                        bool awaitingAction = !yielded && !Interrupted(after)
                            && after["action_tasks"].AsArray().Any(a => a["status"]?.ToString() == "running");
                        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, segment == 0 ? "material-model-execution.json" : "material-model-repair-execution.json"), new JsonObject
                        { ["before"] = before, ["execution"] = execution, ["background_observations"] = background,
                            ["awaiting_action"] = awaitingAction, ["after"] = after }.ToJsonString());
                        if (awaitingAction)
                        {
                            Console.WriteLine($"MATERIAL_ACTION_PENDING handle={running} observation_budget_exhausted=true repair_requested=false");
                            break;
                        }
                        if (segment + 1 < segments)
                        {
                            if (FrameLimit - frames < ReviewExecutionFrames)
                            {
                                Console.WriteLine("MATERIAL_REPAIR_SKIPPED insufficient_review_and_execution_budget=true prior_execution_saved=true");
                                break;
                            }
                            var feedback = new JsonObject { ["workflow"] = "stinger-continuation",
                                ["program_revision"] = reviewed["revision"].DeepClone(),
                                ["execution"] = new JsonObject { ["ok"] = execution["ok"]?.DeepClone(), ["error"] = execution["error"]?.DeepClone(),
                                    ["detail"] = execution["detail"]?.DeepClone(), ["tool_counts"] = execution["tool_counts"]?.DeepClone(),
                                    ["failure"] = execution["failure"]?.DeepClone(),
                                    ["yield_reason"] = execution["value"]?["wake"]?["event"]?["reason"]?.DeepClone() },
                                ["inventory_before"] = new JsonArray(before["inventory_slots"].AsArray().Where(i => i["type"].GetValue<int>() > 0).Select(i => i.DeepClone()).ToArray()),
                                ["inventory_after"] = new JsonArray(after["inventory_slots"].AsArray().Where(i => i["type"].GetValue<int>() > 0).Select(i => i.DeepClone()).ToArray()),
                                ["background_action"] = background.LastOrDefault()?.DeepClone(),
                                ["task_plan"] = after["task_plan"].DeepClone(),
                                ["review_request"] = "Execution ended but the host has not verified the goal. Read the saved revision, account for actual effects, and repair missing or failed continuation without replaying completed work." };
                            reviewed = await Review(feedback, materials: true, session: modelSession, reportName: "behavior-review-repair");
                            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "material-repaired-program.json"), reviewed.ToJsonString());
                        }
                        }
                        throw new Exception("bounded material model execution ended; inspect inventory and preserved handles, not model text, for progress; full acceptance remains unverified");
                    }
                    throw new Exception("material model review ended without executing the saved continuation; full acceptance remains unverified");
                }
                string previousReviewReason = null;
                string previousReviewActions = null;
                for (int review = 0; review < 12 && (combat["value"]?["status"]?.ToString() == "yield"
                    || combat["value"]?["action"]?["result"]?["outcome"]?.ToString() is "combat_obstructed" or "combat_out_of_range" or "source_unavailable"); review++)
                {
                    string reviewReason = combat["value"]?["wake"]?["event"]?["reason"]?.ToString();
                    var reviewSnapshot = await Call("get_state", new JsonObject());
                    string reviewActions = string.Join(";", reviewSnapshot["action_tasks"].AsArray()
                        .Where(a => a["tool"]?.ToString() == "gather")
                        .Select(a => $"{a["handle"]}:{a["attempt"]}:{a["status"]}"))
                        + string.Join(";", (combat["workflow"]?["steps"]?.AsArray() ?? new JsonArray())
                            .Where(s => s["status"]?.ToString() == "completed"
                                && s["id"]?.ToString().StartsWith("combat-obstruction-edit:") == true
                                && s["value"]?["result"]?["outcome"]?.ToString() == "removed")
                            .Select(s => s["id"].ToString()));
                    if (reviewReason != null && reviewReason == previousReviewReason && reviewActions == previousReviewActions)
                    {
                        Console.WriteLine("MATERIAL_REVIEW_UNCHANGED " + reviewReason);
                        break;
                    }
                    previousReviewReason = reviewReason;
                    previousReviewActions = reviewActions;
                    combat = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["workflow"] = "stinger-continuation", ["event_delivery"] = "script",
                        ["code"] = """
                            let state=tool('get_state');
                            const action=state.action_tasks.find(a=>a.tool==='gather'&&(a.status==='paused'||a.status==='failed'&&['combat_obstructed','combat_out_of_range','source_unavailable'].includes(a.result?.outcome))
                              &&a.args.targets.some(t=>t.item_type===209));
                            if(!action||state.player.suffocating)
                              tool('decision_point',{reason:'Cannot safely resume the preserved material action; further review required'});
                            if(action.result?.outcome==='source_unavailable') {
                              let sources=tool('gather_observe',action.args), waited=false;
                              const last=tool('action_query',{handle:action.handle}).result?.last_result;
                              if(!sources.error&&!sources.candidates?.length&&last?.candidate?.kind==='npc'&&last.result?.target_present===false) {
                                const pending=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
                                const next=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:1000});
                                if([...pending.events,...next.events].some(e=>['player_died','low_health','suffocating'].includes(e.kind)))
                                  tool('decision_point',{reason:'Danger during bounded source observation; retain gather '+action.handle});
                                waited=true;
                                sources=tool('gather_observe',action.args);
                              }
                              step('material-source-review:'+action.attempt,()=>({handle:action.handle,waited,
                                counts:sources.counts,source_count:sources.candidates?.length,candidates:sources.candidates?.slice(0,12),error:sources.error}));
                              if(sources.error||!sources.candidates?.length)
                                tool('decision_point',{reason:'No new observed source; choose another area or activity, retaining gather '+action.handle});
                            }
                            function restoreShotLine() {
                              const detail=tool('action_query',{handle:action.handle}).result.last_result;
                              const target={npc:detail.candidate.who,npc_type:detail.candidate.npc_type};
                              const scene=tool('npc_combat_observe',target);
                              if(scene.error) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Obstructed target changed; inspect sources before resuming'});
                              const terrain=tool('inspect_terrain',{radius:12}), cells=[];
                              const defaults=new Set(scene.terrain.stance_probes.map(p=>p.support_x+':'+p.support_y));
                              for(let y=1;y<terrain.rows.length;y++) for(let x=0;x<terrain.rows[y].length;x++) {
                                const cell={x:terrain.origin.x+x,y:terrain.origin.y+y};
                                if('#='.includes(terrain.rows[y][x])&&terrain.rows[y-1][x]==='.'&&!defaults.has(cell.x+':'+cell.y)) cells.push(cell);
                              }
                              cells.sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x*16+8-scene.target_pos.x,a.y*16-state.player.height/2-scene.target_pos.y)
                                -Math.hypot(b.x*16+8-scene.target_pos.x,b.y*16-state.player.height/2-scene.target_pos.y));
                              const surveyed=cells.length?tool('npc_combat_observe',{...target,stance_cells:cells.slice(0,12)}):null;
                              if(surveyed?.error) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Custom stance survey target changed; inspect current sources'});
                              const custom=surveyed?.requested_stances??[];
                              const candidates=[...scene.terrain.stance_probes,...custom.filter(p=>p.supported&&p.body_fits&&!p.support_half_block&&p.support_slope===0)]
                                .filter(p=>p.shot_line?.clear&&Math.hypot(p.x-scene.target_pos.x,p.y-scene.target_pos.y)<=(detail.result.combat_range_tiles??40)*16)
                                .sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-state.player.pos.x,a.y-state.player.pos.y)-Math.hypot(b.x-state.player.pos.x,b.y-state.player.pos.y));
                              let selected=null;
                              for(const p of candidates) {
                                const facts=tool('opportunity_assess',{x:p.support_x,y:p.support_y-1});
                                const overlaps=h=>h.x*16<p.x+state.player.width/2&&(h.x+1)*16>p.x-state.player.width/2
                                  &&h.y*16<p.y+state.player.height/2&&(h.y+1)*16>p.y-state.player.height/2;
                                const blocking=facts.hazards.filter(h=>h.kind!=='contact_damage'||h.on_direct_corridor||overlaps(h));
                                if(!blocking.length){selected={probe:p,facts:{hazards:facts.hazards,distance:facts.approach_direct_distance,solid_cells:facts.direct_corridor_solid_cells.length},reason:'Current shot line clear; no reported hazard on direct corridor or candidate body; nearby non-contact thorns do not veto stance. Actual route remains unverified.'};break;}
                              }
                              if(!selected) {
                                const blocker=scene.terrain.direct_line.blocker;
                                if(!blocker) tool('decision_point',{reason:'No acceptable stance or measured obstruction; choose another source or weapon'});
                                const facts=tool('opportunity_assess',{x:blocker.x,y:blocker.y});
                                if(facts.error||facts.hazards.length||!facts.target.has_tile)
                                  tool('decision_point',{reason:'Obstruction cannot be safely selected for a one-tile edit: '+JSON.stringify({blocker,error:facts.error,hazards:facts.hazards})});
                                const picks=tool('inventory_review').inventory.filter(i=>i.pick>0).sort((a,b)=>b.pick-a.pick);
                                const pick=picks.length?{slot:picks[0].slot,power:picks[0].pick}:null;
                                if(!pick) tool('decision_point',{reason:'No pickaxe for the measured obstruction; choose another source or equipment'});
                                const edited=tool('use_item',{slot:pick.slot,x:blocker.x,y:blocker.y,strict:true,duration_ticks:0});
                                const after=tool('npc_combat_observe',target);
                                step('combat-obstruction-edit:'+action.attempt+':'+blocker.x+':'+blocker.y,()=>({target,blocker,pick,
                                  result:{outcome:edited.outcome,error:edited.error,reason:edited.reason},
                                  after:{error:after.error,direct_line:after.terrain?.direct_line},original_handle:action.handle}));
                                if(edited.outcome!=='removed'||after.error||!after.terrain.direct_line.clear)
                                  tool('decision_point',{reason:'One-tile obstruction edit did not establish a clear shot; inspect edit evidence before changing terrain again'});
                                state=tool('get_state');
                                return;
                              }
                              tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
                              const moved=tool('nav_to',{x:selected.probe.support_x,y:selected.probe.support_y-1});
                              if(moved.done!==true) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Combat reposition interrupted; preserve handle '+moved.handle+'; outcome='+moved.outcome+'; hp='+moved.action?.observation?.hp});
                              const after=tool('npc_combat_observe',target);
                              step('combat-reposition:'+action.attempt,()=>({target,selected,after:{error:after.error,hp:after.hp,target_pos:after.target_pos,direct_line:after.terrain?.direct_line},original_handle:action.handle}));
                              if(after.error||!after.terrain.direct_line.clear)
                                tool('decision_point',{reason:'Reposition did not establish current line of sight; do not blindly retry combat'});
                              state=tool('get_state');
                            }
                            const recovery={hp_before:state.player.hp,potion_delay_ticks:state.player.potion_delay_ticks,candidates:[]};
                            if(state.player.hp<state.watch.health_below&&state.player.potion_delay_ticks===0) {
                              recovery.candidates=state.healing_items.filter(i=>i.consumable)
                                .map(i=>({slot:i.slot,type:i.type,heal:i.base_heal_life}));
                              recovery.candidates.sort((a,b)=>b.heal-a.heal);
                              if(recovery.candidates.length) {
                                const medicine=recovery.candidates[0];
                                const used=tool('use_item',{slot:medicine.slot,x:Math.floor(state.player.pos.x/16),y:Math.floor(state.player.pos.y/16)});
                                recovery.used={medicine,outcome:used.outcome,verified:used.verified,consumed:used.consumed,state:used.state,error:used.error};
                              }
                            }
                            state=tool('get_state');
                            if(state.player.hp<state.watch.health_below&&!state.player.suffocating) {
                              const guard=tool('action_start',{tool:'fight',args:{max_dist:25,seconds:15,movement:'auto'}});
                              if(guard.error||!guard.handle) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Temporary defense could not acquire input; inspect current owner'});
                              let retreat=null;
                              const distanceToEnemy=p=>Math.min(1000,...state.enemies.map(e=>Math.hypot(e.pos.x/16-p.x,e.pos.y/16-p.y)));
                              try {
                              const anchors=state.action_tasks.filter(a=>a.status==='completed')
                                .map(a=>a.result?.state?.pos??(a.tool==='nav_to'?{x:a.args.x,y:a.args.y}:null))
                                .filter(p=>p&&Number.isFinite(p.x)&&Number.isFinite(p.y));
                              const nearest=state.enemies.slice().sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.pos.x-state.player.pos.x,a.pos.y-state.player.pos.y)
                                -Math.hypot(b.pos.x-state.player.pos.x,b.pos.y-state.player.pos.y))[0];
                              if(nearest) {
                                const nearby=tool('npc_combat_observe',{npc:nearest.who,npc_type:nearest.type});
                                if(!nearby.error) for(const p of nearby.terrain.stance_probes)
                                  if(p.body_fits) anchors.push({x:p.support_x,y:p.support_y-1});
                              }
                              anchors.sort((a,b)=>distanceToEnemy(b)-distanceToEnemy(a));
                              recovery.retreat_candidates=[];
                              for(const anchor of anchors) {
                                const enemyDistance=distanceToEnemy(anchor);
                                if(Math.hypot(anchor.x+.5-state.player.pos.x/16-state.player.width/32,
                                  anchor.y+.5-state.player.pos.y/16-state.player.height/32)>60) {
                                  recovery.retreat_candidates.push({anchor,rejected:'outside_observation_range'});continue;
                                }
                                if(enemyDistance<20){recovery.retreat_candidates.push({anchor,enemyDistance,rejected:'enemy_nearby'});continue;}
                                const facts=tool('opportunity_assess',anchor);
                                if(facts.error||!Array.isArray(facts.hazards)) {
                                  recovery.retreat_candidates.push({anchor,enemyDistance,rejected:'observation_failed',error:facts.error,detail:facts.detail});
                                  continue;
                                }
                                recovery.retreat_candidates.push({anchor,enemyDistance,hazards:facts.hazards});
                                const cx=anchor.x*16+8,cy=(anchor.y+1)*16-state.player.height/2;
                                const overlaps=h=>h.x*16<cx+state.player.width/2&&(h.x+1)*16>cx-state.player.width/2
                                  &&h.y*16<cy+state.player.height/2&&(h.y+1)*16>cy-state.player.height/2;
                                if(!facts.hazards.some(h=>h.kind!=='contact_damage'||h.on_direct_corridor||overlaps(h))) {
                                  retreat={anchor,facts};break;
                                }
                              }
                              } finally {
                                const released=tool('action_control',{handle:guard.handle,command:'cancel'});
                                recovery.defense={handle:guard.handle,status:released.status,hp:released.observation?.hp,pos:released.observation?.pos};
                              }
                              state=tool('get_state');
                              if(state.player.hp<=0||state.player.suffocating)
                                tool('decision_point',{reason:'Recovery observation ended in danger; retain original gather and defense evidence'});
                              if(retreat) {
                                tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
                                const policy={events:state.watch.events,passive_events:state.watch.passive_events,
                                  tile_types:state.watch.tile_types,radius:state.watch.radius,health_below:state.watch.health_below};
                                tool('watch',{...policy,passive_events:[...new Set([...policy.passive_events,'low_health'])]});
                                let moved;
                                try { moved=tool('nav_to',retreat.anchor); }
                                finally { tool('watch',policy); }
                                recovery.retreat={anchor:retreat.anchor,hazards:retreat.facts.hazards,
                                  result:{done:moved.done,outcome:moved.outcome,handle:moved.handle??moved.managed_handle,state:moved.state,error:moved.error}};
                                step('combat-retreat:'+action.attempt,()=>recovery.retreat);
                                if(recovery.retreat.result.done!==true)
                                  tool('decision_point',{reason:'Recovery retreat interrupted; retain original gather and inspect navigation handle'});
                                state=tool('get_state');
                                recovery.rest=[];
                                let previousHp=state.player.hp;
                                const damageTick=state.last_damage?.tick;
                                for(let sample=0;sample<20&&state.player.hp<state.watch.health_below;sample++) {
                                  const position={x:state.player.pos.x/16,y:state.player.pos.y/16};
                                  if(state.player.suffocating||state.player.hp<=40||state.last_damage?.tick!==damageTick
                                    ||(state.player.hp<previousHp&&state.health_dynamics?.regen_direction!=='damage')||distanceToEnemy(position)<20) break;
                                  previousHp=state.player.hp;
                                  tool('script_events',{wait_ms:1000});
                                  state=tool('get_state');
                                  recovery.rest.push({hp:state.player.hp,pos:state.player.pos,health_dynamics:state.health_dynamics,
                                    last_damage:state.last_damage,buffs:state.buffs});
                                }
                              }
                            }
                            recovery.hp_after=state.player.hp;
                            step('combat-recovery:'+action.attempt,()=>recovery);
                            if(state.player.hp<state.watch.health_below||state.player.suffocating)
                              tool('decision_point',{reason:'Material combat needs a different recovery or equipment plan; health remains below the configured threshold: '+JSON.stringify(recovery)});
                            if(['combat_obstructed','combat_out_of_range'].includes(action.result?.outcome)) restoreShotLine();
                            const before=step('stinger-baseline',()=>{throw new Error('original material baseline missing');});
                            step('combat-review:'+action.attempt,()=>({handle:action.handle,hp:state.player.hp,
                              reason:'Recovery checked against actual inventory and health; retain the same material target'}));
                            tool('script_events',{wait_ms:0});
                            tool('action_control',{handle:action.handle,command:'resume'});
                            for(let i=0;i<20;i++) {
                              const observed=tool('script_events',{wait_ms:1000});
                              if(observed.events.some(e=>['player_died','low_health','suffocating'].includes(e.kind)))
                                tool('decision_point',{reason:'Revised combat health limit reached; do not resume blindly'});
                              const current=tool('action_query',{handle:action.handle});
                              if(current.status==='completed'||current.status==='failed') break;
                              if(current.status==='paused') tool('action_control',{handle:action.handle,command:'resume'});
                            }
                            const after=tool('get_state').inventory_slots.filter(i=>i.type===209).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0);
                            const final=tool('action_query',{handle:action.handle});
                            return {before,after,gained:after-before,resumed_handle:action.handle,
                              action:{handle:final.handle,status:final.status,attempt:final.attempt,
                                result:{outcome:final.result?.outcome,targets:final.result?.targets,last_result:final.result?.last_result}}};
                            """ });
                }
                await OnMain(() => { collectingStingers = false; return true; });
                int actualStingers = await OnMain(() => Main.LocalPlayer.inventory.Where(i => i.type == ItemID.Stinger).Sum(i => i.stack));
                combat["verified_inventory"] = new JsonObject { ["item_type"] = ItemID.Stinger,
                    ["actual"] = actualStingers, ["required"] = stingersRequired };
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "stinger-result.json"), combat.ToJsonString());
                if (actualStingers < stingersRequired)
                    throw new Exception("recipe stinger quantity not fulfilled; inspect stinger-result.json");
                Console.WriteLine($"OPPORTUNITY_STINGER_PROGRESS verified_recipe_stingers=true spawned_hornets={hornetsSpawned}");
            }
            var verification = await Call("task_step", new JsonObject { ["id"] = "materials", ["action"] = "verify" });
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "material-plan-verification.json"), verification.ToJsonString());
            bool allMaterials = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TB_OPPORTUNITY_STINGERS") == "1";
            if (verification["verified"]?.GetValue<bool>() != allMaterials || verification["current_step"]?.ToString() != (allMaterials ? "crafted" : "materials")
                || verification["steps"]?[1]?["id"]?.ToString() != "crafted")
                throw new Exception("partial materials advanced or lost the preserved crafting plan");
            Console.WriteLine($"OPPORTUNITY_RECIPE_PENDING materials_complete={allMaterials} crafting_goal_preserved=true");
            if (allMaterials)
            {
                var crafted = await Call("run_script", new JsonObject { ["workflow"] = "material-crafting", ["event_delivery"] = "script",
                    ["code"] = """
                        function recipe(){return tool('recipe',{name:'ThornChakram'}).recipes.find(r=>r.internal==='ThornChakram');}
                        let current=recipe();
                        if(!current.can_craft_now) {
                          const candidates=[];
                          for(const station of current.stations)
                            candidates.push(...tool('find_tiles',{name:station.tile,max_dist:2000,n:5}).tiles);
                          if(!candidates.length) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Materials verified but crafting station unavailable; preserve crafting step',evidence:{stations:current.stations}});
                          const station=candidates[0];
                          const arrival=tool('nav_to',{x:station.x,y:station.y,reach:true});
                          if(arrival.done!==true) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Station approach unfinished',evidence:{station,arrival}});
                          current=recipe();
                        }
                        if(!current.can_craft_now) tool('decision_point',{reason:'Station arrival did not make recipe craftable',evidence:{recipe:current}});
                        const made=tool('craft',{name:current.internal});
                        if(made.error||made.ok===false) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(made));
                        const verified=tool('task_step',{id:'crafted',action:'verify'});
                        const inventory=tool('get_state').inventory_slots;
                        return {verified:verified.verified,plan_status:verified.status,
                          crystals:inventory.filter(i=>i.type===29).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0),
                          weapon:inventory.filter(i=>i.type===191).reduce((n,i)=>n+i.stack,0)};
                        """ });
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Main.SavePath, "crafted-result.json"), crafted.ToJsonString());
                if (crafted["value"]?["verified"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true
                    || crafted["value"]?["crystals"]?.GetValue<int>() is not >= 1
                    || crafted["value"]?["weapon"]?.GetValue<int>() is not >= 1)
                    throw new Exception("final crafting or preserved crystal unverified; inspect crafted-result.json");
                Console.WriteLine("OPPORTUNITY_CRAFTED_VERIFIED native_inventory=true model_calls=0");
            }
        }
    }
}

public sealed class BoundaryInputPlayer : ModPlayer
{
    public static Action<Player> Drive;
    public override void SetControls() { if (Player.whoAmI == Main.myPlayer) Drive?.Invoke(Player); }
}
